Form Kaynak Kodu
Firma Kodu : BDORA_2014 / Uygulama : PFRM7S
Makro Kodu : BUMU01-01
Hangi Tablo için : BOMU21
Ylave Hangi Uygulamalar için : BOMU01
Açyklama : ÜRÜN AÐACI EXCELE-YAN ÇÝZGÝ SIRALI_RESÝMLÝ_SAYFA AYARLI_SADE-8 PUNTA
Kod : 
Public k(20),satir,sheet,ExcelApp,wb1,mamulkodu,SatirSayisi,SonSatir,SonSutun

Sub PrintForm()

	Dim j, S0P1, S0P2, S0P3,ACIKLAMA_KOLON_BASLANGICI, seviye

	Set ExcelApp = CreateObject("Excel.Application")
	Set Wb1 = ExcelApp.Workbooks.Add
	Set Sheet = Wb1.ActiveSheet

	ExcelApp.Visible = True
	baslangic_satiri = 2
	maxseviye = 0
	SatirSayisi = MULTILEVEL.GetRecCount()

	Grid1=Doc.GetGridColInfo("","MULTILEVEL")
	KolonSayisi1 = Ubound(Grid1)
	
	For i = 1 To SatirSayisi
		Grid1 = Doc.GetGridColInfo("","MULTILEVEL")
		KolonSayisi1 = Ubound(Grid1)
		For KolonNo1 = 0 To KolonSayisi1
			FieldName1 = Grid1(KolonNo1, 0)
			If FieldName1 = "PICTURE_LEVEL1" Then
				SatirDegeri = Trim(MULTILEVEL.GetFieldValue(FieldName1, i))
				If Right(SatirDegeri, 1) > maxseviye Then
					maxseviye = Right(SatirDegeri, 1)
				End If
			End If
		Next
	Next

	BASLANGIC_KOLONU = 2
	ACIKLAMA_KOLON_BASLANGICI = BASLANGIC_KOLONU+maxseviye+3	
	KOLON_BASLIK_KONUMU=0
	
	For Satir = 1 To SatirSayisi
		Grid = Doc.GetGridColInfo("","MULTILEVEL")
		KolonSayisi = Ubound(Grid)
		For KolonNo = 0 To KolonSayisi
			FieldName = Grid(KolonNo, 0)
			If FieldName = "PICTURE_LEVEL1" Then
				SatirDegeri = Trim(MULTILEVEL.GetFieldValue(FieldName, Satir))
				Seviye = Right(SatirDegeri,1) + 1
				
				If Satir=1 Then Wb1.Sheets(1).cells(baslangic_satiri,1).Value = "Resim"
				FieldName = "BOMREC_KAYNAKCODE" 
				KaynakKodu = Trim(MULTILEVEL.GetFieldValue(FieldName, Satir))
		
				Set S0P = Doc.Select1("dddtyy", "STOK0P", "EVRAKNO='" & KaynakKodu & "'", "SUBSRNUM", -1)
				If S0P.GetRowCount > 0 Then
					Resim1 = Trim(Doc.Getd7Directory_DataFirm & "docs\" & Trim(S0P.FULLFILENAME1(1)))
				Else
					'FieldName1 = "BOMREC_INPUTTYPE" 
					'SatirDegeri1 = Trim(MULTILEVEL.GetFieldValue(FieldName1, Satir))

					'If SatirDegeri1 = "H" Then
					'	Set Doc1 = Doc.CreateDocumentNoUI("STOK00", "")
						
					'	Doc1.Load_Voucher KaynakKodu
					'	Set S0P2 = Doc1.GetTableObject("STOK0P")	
					'	S0P2.FULLFILENAME1(1) = "RESIMCEK.jpg"
					'	Doc1.Save_Voucher
					'	Doc1.LoadEmpty_Voucher
					'ElseIf SatirDegeri1 = "I" Then
						Resim1 = ""
					'End If
				End If
				'Wb1.Sheets(1).cells(baslangic_satiri+Satir, 1).Value = Resim1
				
				If Resim1 <> "" Then
					Wb1.Sheets(1).cells(baslangic_satiri+Satir,1).rowheight = Wb1.Sheets(1).cells(baslangic_satiri+Satir,1).rowheight*2.5
					rtop = Wb1.Sheets(1).cells(baslangic_satiri+Satir,1).Top+1.5
					rleft= Wb1.Sheets(1).cells(baslangic_satiri+Satir,1).Left+1.5
					Wb1.Sheets(1).cells(baslangic_satiri+Satir, 1).Select
					Set resim = Wb1.ActiveSheet.Pictures.Insert(Trim(resim1)) 
					resimadi=resim.name
					wb1.ActiveSheet.Shapes(resimadi).Select
					resim.Left = rleft 
					resim.Top = rtop
					ExcelApp.selection.ShapeRange.LockAspectRatio = msoFalse
					ExcelApp.selection.ShapeRange.Height = 10
					ExcelApp.selection.ShapeRange.Width = 25
				End If
	
	
				
				
				FieldName = "BOMREC_SIRANO" 
				If Satir=2 Then Wb1.Sheets(1).cells(baslangic_satiri,2).Value = "Op.No"
				SiraNo = Trim(MULTILEVEL.GetFieldValue(FieldName, Satir))
				Wb1.Sheets(1).cells(baslangic_satiri+Satir, 2).Value = SiraNo
				
				FieldName = "BOMREC_KAYNAKCODE" 
				If Satir=1 Then Wb1.Sheets(1).cells(baslangic_satiri,3).Value = "Kodlar"
				KaynakKodu = Trim(MULTILEVEL.GetFieldValue(FieldName, Satir))
				Wb1.Sheets(1).cells(baslangic_satiri+Satir, BASLANGIC_KOLONU+seviye).Value = KaynakKodu
				
				KOLON_BASLIK_KONUMU = ACIKLAMA_KOLON_BASLANGICI 
				
				FieldName = "ACIKLAMA" 
				If Satir=1 Then Wb1.Sheets(1).cells(baslangic_satiri,ACIKLAMA_KOLON_BASLANGICI).Value = FieldName
				Aciklama = Trim(MULTILEVEL.GetFieldValue(FieldName, Satir))
				Wb1.Sheets(1).cells(baslangic_satiri+Satir, KOLON_BASLIK_KONUMU).Value = Aciklama
				
				KOLON_BASLIK_KONUMU = ACIKLAMA_KOLON_BASLANGICI + 1
				
				FieldName = "BOMREC_OPERASYON_IMLT01_AD" 
				If Satir=1 Then Wb1.Sheets(1).cells(baslangic_satiri, KOLON_BASLIK_KONUMU).Value = "OPERASYON"
				Operasyon = Trim(MULTILEVEL.GetFieldValue(FieldName, Satir))
				Wb1.Sheets(1).cells(baslangic_satiri+Satir, KOLON_BASLIK_KONUMU).Value = Operasyon
				
				KOLON_BASLIK_KONUMU = ACIKLAMA_KOLON_BASLANGICI + 2
				
				FieldName = "BOMREC_KAYNAK0" 
				If Satir=1 Then Wb1.Sheets(1).cells(baslangic_satiri, KOLON_BASLIK_KONUMU).Value = "Hammadde Miktarý / Tezgah Süresi"
				Miktar_Sure = Trim(MULTILEVEL.GetFieldValue(FieldName, Satir))
				Wb1.Sheets(1).cells(baslangic_satiri+Satir, KOLON_BASLIK_KONUMU).Value = Miktar_Sure
				
				KOLON_BASLIK_KONUMU = ACIKLAMA_KOLON_BASLANGICI + 3
				
				FieldName = "KAYNAK_BIRIM" 
				If Satir=1 Then Wb1.Sheets(1).cells(baslangic_satiri, KOLON_BASLIK_KONUMU).Value = "Hammadde Miktarý / Tezgah Süresi"
				Birim = Trim(MULTILEVEL.GetFieldValue(FieldName, Satir))
				Wb1.Sheets(1).cells(baslangic_satiri+Satir, KOLON_BASLIK_KONUMU).Value = Birim
				
				KOLON_BASLIK_KONUMU = ACIKLAMA_KOLON_BASLANGICI + 4
				
				FieldName = "BOMREC_YMAMULPM" 
				If Satir=1 Then Wb1.Sheets(1).cells(baslangic_satiri, KOLON_BASLIK_KONUMU).Value = "Yarý Mamül Miktarý"
				YariMamulMiktari = Trim(MULTILEVEL.GetFieldValue(FieldName, Satir))
				Wb1.Sheets(1).cells(baslangic_satiri+Satir, KOLON_BASLIK_KONUMU).Value = YariMamulMiktari
				
			End If
		Next
	Next

	SonSatir = Sheet.Cells(1, "A").SpecialCells(11).Row
	SonSutun = Sheet.Cells.SpecialCells(11).Column   'xlCellTypeLastCell kodunun karþýlýðý 11 ' dir.

	Wb1.ActiveSheet.Cells.Select
	With ExcelApp.Selection.Font
		.Name = "Agency FB"
		.Size = 8
	End With

	Sheet.Range(Sheet.Cells(baslangic_satiri, ACIKLAMA_KOLON_BASLANGICI+2), Sheet.Cells(SonSatir, ACIKLAMA_KOLON_BASLANGICI)).Select
	ExcelApp.Selection.NumberFormat = "#,##0.00"

	Sheet.Range(Sheet.Cells(baslangic_satiri, ACIKLAMA_KOLON_BASLANGICI+4), Sheet.Cells(SonSatir, ACIKLAMA_KOLON_BASLANGICI)).Select
	ExcelApp.Selection.NumberFormat = "#,##0.00"

	Wb1.Sheets(1).cells(baslangic_satiri-1,SonSutun).Select
	ExcelApp.Selection.WrapText = True

	Sheet.Cells.Select
	ExcelApp.Selection.RowHeight = 12
	ExcelApp.Selection.VerticalAlignment = 2 'xlTop

	Wb1.Sheets(1).Rows(1).RowHeight = 20
	Wb1.Sheets(1).Rows(baslangic_satiri).RowHeight = 25
		
	'BAÞLIK BOYA
	For i = 1 to SonSutun
		Sheet.Cells(baslangic_satiri, i).Select
		'ExcelApp.Selection.Font.Bold = True
		ExcelApp.Selection.Font.Size = 8
		ExcelApp.Selection.Interior.ColorIndex = 19   '15:GRI,1:SIYAH,2:beyaz,3:kýrmýzý,4:yeþil,5:mavi,6:sarý,7:pembe,8:magenta,9:koyukahve,10:KOYUYEÞÝL,11:LACIVERT,12:BOKYESILI,12:MOR
		ExcelApp.Selection.Interior.Pattern =1 '1:xlSolid,2:noktalý3:çizgili
		ExcelApp.Selection.Font.ColorIndex = 1 '1:SÝYAH 2:GRÝ,3:KIRMIZI
		ExcelApp.Selection.WrapText = True 
		ExcelApp.Selection.HorizontalAlignment = 3 'xlCenter 
		ExcelApp.Selection.VerticalAlignment = 2 'xlCenter 
	Next
	
	If Resim2 <> "" THEN
		rtop = Wb1.Sheets(1).cells(1,1).Top+1.5
		rleft= Wb1.Sheets(1).cells(1,1).Left+1.5
		Wb1.Sheets(1).cells(1, SonSutun).Select
		Set resim = Wb1.ActiveSheet.Pictures.Insert(trim(resim2)) 
		resimadi=resim.name
		wb1.ActiveSheet.Shapes(resimadi).Select
		resim.Left = rleft 
		resim.Top = rtop
		ExcelApp.selection.ShapeRange.LockAspectRatio = msoFalse
		ExcelApp.selection.ShapeRange.Height = 15
		ExcelApp.selection.ShapeRange.Width = 30
	End If
	Resim2 = ""

	'RESÝM KOLONU AYARI
	Wb1.Sheets(1).Columns(1).ColumnWidth = 5
	Wb1.ActiveSheet.Columns(1).Select
	ExcelApp.Selection.HorizontalAlignment= 3 'xlCenter

	For g = 3 to ACIKLAMA_KOLON_BASLANGICI
		Wb1.ActiveSheet.Columns(g).Select
		ExcelApp.Selection.HorizontalAlignment= 1
		if g = 3 Then
			ExcelApp.Selection.ColumnWidth = 2
		elseif g = ACIKLAMA_KOLON_BASLANGICI Then
			ExcelApp.Selection.ColumnWidth = 7.5
		Else
			ExcelApp.Selection.ColumnWidth = 3
		End If
	Next
	
	For h = ACIKLAMA_KOLON_BASLANGICI-1 to ACIKLAMA_KOLON_BASLANGICI+2
		Wb1.ActiveSheet.Columns(h).Select
		ExcelApp.Selection.HorizontalAlignment= 1
		Wb1.ActiveSheet.Columns(h).autofit
	Next
	
	For h = ACIKLAMA_KOLON_BASLANGICI+2 to SonSutun
		Wb1.ActiveSheet.Columns(h).Select
		ExcelApp.Selection.HorizontalAlignment= 1
		ExcelApp.Selection.ColumnWidth = 9
	Next

	For h = 2 to 2
		Wb1.Sheets(1).Columns(h).ColumnWidth = 3
		Wb1.ActiveSheet.Columns(h).Select
		ExcelApp.Selection.HorizontalAlignment= 3 'xlCenter
	Next

	Sheet.Range(Sheet.Cells(baslangic_satiri-1, 1), Sheet.Cells(baslangic_satiri-1, SonSutun)).Merge
	Wb1.Sheets(1).cells(1,1).value = Wb1.Sheets(1).cells(baslangic_satiri+1,3).value & " - " & Wb1.Sheets(1).cells(baslangic_satiri+1,ACIKLAMA_KOLON_BASLANGICI).value
	Wb1.Sheets(1).cells(1,1).Select
	ExcelApp.Selection.Font.Bold = True
	ExcelApp.Selection.Font.Size = 10
	'Sheet.Range(Sheet.Cells(baslangic_satiri-2, 3), Sheet.Cells(baslangic_satiri, ACIKLAMA_KOLON_BASLANGICI)).Merge
	Wb1.Sheets(1).cells(1,1).Select
	Wb1.ActiveSheet.Rows(2).Select
	ExcelApp.Selection.HorizontalAlignment= 3 'xlCenter
	Wb1.Sheets(1).cells.Select

	ExcelApp.Selection.Borders(1).LineStyle = 1  		'.Borders(1):sol dik çizgi,.Borders(3)=üst yatay çizgi,(4):alt yatay çizgi, (5):çapraz \,(6):çapraz:/,(10):sað dik çizgi
	ExcelApp.Selection.Borders(1).Weight = 1  			'1:noktalý,2:çizgi
	ExcelApp.Selection.Borders(4).LineStyle = 1			'.Borders(4):alt yatay çigi
	ExcelApp.Selection.Borders(4).Weight = 1  			'1:noktalý,2:çizgi
	ExcelApp.Selection.Borders(3).LineStyle = 1			'.Borders(3):üst yatay çigi
	ExcelApp.Selection.Borders(3).Weight = 1			'1:noktalý,2:çizgi
	ExcelApp.Selection.Borders(10).LineStyle = 1  	'.Border(10):seçili alanýn en sað dik çizgisi
	ExcelApp.Selection.Borders(10).Weight = 1  			'1:noktalý,2:çizgi, 4:kalýn
	Call Yaziciya_Yazdir

	Set Wb1 = Nothing
	Set ExcelApp = Nothing

End Sub
'-------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Sub Yaziciya_Yazdir ()
 	Sheet.Range(Sheet.Cells(1, 1),Sheet.Cells(SonSatir,SonSutun)).Select
	ExcelApp.Selection.Name = "YAZILACAK_ALAN" 
	Sheet.PageSetup.PrintArea = 	"YAZILACAK_ALAN"

	With Sheet.PageSetup
  		'.CenterFooter = "Page &P of &N"
		.LeftMargin = ExcelApp.InchesToPoints(0.39)
     .RightMargin = ExcelApp.InchesToPoints(0)
     .TopMargin = ExcelApp.InchesToPoints(0.19)
     .BottomMargin = ExcelApp.InchesToPoints(0.19)
     .HeaderMargin = ExcelApp.InchesToPoints(0.02)
     .FooterMargin = ExcelApp.InchesToPoints(0.02)
     .Zoom = 100
     .PrintErrors = xlPrintErrorsDisplayed
		'.PaperSize = 1
     .OddAndEvenPagesHeaderFooter = False
     .DifferentFirstPageHeaderFooter = False
     '.ScaleWithDocHeaderFooter = True
     '.AlignMarginsHeaderFooter = True
		.CenterHorizontally = False
		.CenterVertically = False
		.Orientation = 2
		.Draft = False
		'.PrintTitleRows = "$1:$2"
		.PrintTitleColumns = ""
	End With
End Sub                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                 


----------------------------------------------------------------
Form Kaynak Kodu
Firma Kodu : BDORA_2014 / Uygulama : PFRM7S
Makro Kodu : STOK60-0
Hangi Tablo için : STOK60
Ylave Hangi Uygulamalar için : STOK60I
Açyklama : Budora - Sevk Ýrsaliyesi - Kod Ýcmalli
Kod : 
Public sayfaTuru, sayfaBoyu, sayfaEni, sonSatir, satirSonu, sayfaSatirSayisi, ST60, SiparisFaturaNiteligi, sat_rty
Public siraNo, satirBaslangic, satirYuksekligi, SagMargin, solmargin, ustmargin, chkodu, solmargin2
Public Grid()

Sub PrintForm()
	Doc.CreateTableObject "XXX=STOK60T", "TMPCOPY"
	Set ST60 = Doc.GetTableObject("XXX")
	Set S60T = Doc.GetTableObject("STOK60T")
	ST60.CopyTable(S60T)
	ST60.GroupBy2("KOD")

	Dim satirSayisi,pe1

	siraNo = 0
	sat_rty = 0
	sayfaBoyu = 297
	sayfaEni = 210
	satiryuksekligi = 4.3

	Set pe1 = doc.CreatePrintEngineOutput()
	res = pe1.SetConsolePrintOpt(1)
	res = pe1.ConsoleOutput_SetLinesPerPage(66)
	res = pe1.ConsoleOutput_SetCharsPerInch(17)
	res = pe1.Open(0, "Irsaliye", SayfaBoyu, SayfaEni, "")
	ustmargin = 0
	satirBaslangic = ustmargin + 18 * satirYuksekligi
	sayfaSatirSayisi = 23
	solmargin = 0
	solmargin2 = 10
	sagMargin = SayfaEni - 10

	Call Alanlar()
'	If doc.ErrorNumber Then
'		MsgBox ("Hata main : " & doc.ErrorNumber)
'		Exit Sub
'	End If

	satirSayisi = ST60.GetRecCount()
	Call Print_form_sayfabasi(pe1)
	For i = 1 To satirSayisi
		satiryuksekligi = 3.5
		Call Print_form_satir(pe1, i)
	Next
	satiryuksekligi = 4.3
	Call Print_form_sayfasonu(pe1)

	if Doc.GetDocType0 = "TRACK601" Then
		res = pe1.PrintDoc(1)
	Else
		res = pe1.Show()
	End if
End Sub
'----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Sub Print_form_sayfabasi(pe1)
	res = pe1.selectfont("Courier New", 7)

	chkodu = STOK60E.CH_KODU(1)
	chadi = STOK60E.CH_KODU_CARI00_AD(1)
	if trim(STOK60E.SEVKADRESI_1) = "" then
		adres1 = 	STOK60E.CH_KODU_CARI00_ADRES_1(1)
		adres2 = 	STOK60E.CH_KODU_CARI00_ADRES_2(1)
		adres3 = 	STOK60E.CH_KODU_CARI00_ADRES_3(1)
	else
		adres1 = 	STOK60E.SEVKADRESI_1(1)
		adres2 = 	STOK60E.SEVKADRESI_2(1)
		adres3 = 	STOK60E.SEVKADRESI_3(1)
	end if

	vergidairesi = STOK60E.CH_KODU_CARI00_VERGIDAIRESI(1)
	vergino = STOK60E.CH_KODU_CARI00_VERGINO(1)
	tarih = Doc.StrtoNorDate(STOK60E.TARIH(1))
	if trim(STOK60E.RSEVKTARIH) = "" then
		sevktarih = Tarih
	else
		sevktarih = Doc.StrtoNorDate(STOK60E.RSEVKTARIH)
	end if

	res = pe1.Setalign("LEFT")
	res = pe1.write(ustmargin + 9* satirYuksekligi, solmargin + 165, 5,30, tarih)
	res = pe1.write(ustmargin + 10.5* satirYuksekligi, pe1.GetLeft, 5, 40, sevktarih)

	res = pe1.write(ustmargin + 7 * satirYuksekligi, solmargin + 22, 5, 80, chadi)
	res = pe1.write(ustmargin + 8* satirYuksekligi, solMargin + 22, 5, 80, adres1)
	res = pe1.write(ustmargin + 9 * satirYuksekligi, pe1.GetLeft, 5, 80, adres2)
	res = pe1.write(ustmargin + 10 * satirYuksekligi, pe1.GetLeft, 5, 80, adres3)
	res = pe1.write(ustmargin + 14 * satirYuksekligi, solmargin + 50, 5, 30, vergidairesi)
	res = pe1.write(ustmargin + 14 * satirYuksekligi, solmargin + 75, 5, 15, vergino)
End Sub
'--------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Sub Print_form_satir(pe1, satirNo)
	'res = pe1.SetTransparentMode(0)
	res = pe1.SetBkgColor(RGB(255,0,0))

		Dim satirpozr
		If Trim(ST60.KOD(satirNo)) = "" Then Exit Sub
		Degisken = ""
		sonSatir = pe1.GetBottom()
		
		If siraNo > sayfaSatirSayisi Then
			Call Print_form_sayfasonu(pe1)
			siraNo = 0
			res = pe1.PageBreak()
			Call Print_form_sayfabasi(pe1)
			satirpozr = satirBaslangic * eksatir
			eksatir= 0
		End If		

		siraNo = siraNo+1
		
		If eksatir = "0" Then
			satirpozr = satirBaslangic+((siraNo-1))*satiryuksekligi
		Else
			satirpozr = sonSatir+0.5
		end if		

		res = pe1.selectfont("Courier New", 7)
		res = pe1.setbold(0)
		Sol = solMargin + 1
		eksatir = 0
		
		For i = 0 To UBound(Grid,1)
			SutunGenisligi = Grid(i,1) 
			DegiskenAdi = Grid(i,0)
			stokkodu = Trim(ST60.KOD(satirno))
			aciklama = Trim(ST60.NOTES(satirno))
			StokAdi2 = Trim(Doc.GetDBFieldValue("STOK00", "NAME2", StokKodu, ""))
			MStokKodu = Trim(Doc.GetCHStokKodu(stokkodu,chkodu))
			MStokAdi = Trim(Doc.GetCHStokAdi(stokkodu,chkodu))
			SipNo = Trim(ST60.SIPNO(satirno))
			ArtNo = Trim(ST60.SIPARTNO(satirno))
			
			MSipNo = Trim(Doc.getdbfieldvalue("STOK40E", "CHSIPNO", SipNo, ""))
			MSipArtNo = Trim(Doc.getdbfieldvalue("STOK40T", "CHSIPANO", ArtNo, "ARTNO"))
			SiparisFaturaNiteligi = Doc.GetDBFieldValue("STOK40E","MUHT_FATURA_NITELIK", SipNo, "") 

			Select Case Trim(degiskenAdi)
				Case "_SIRA***"
					degisken = siraNo
				Case "_AD"
					If Trim(MStokKodu) = "" Then
						Degisken = Trim(Doc.GetDBFieldValue("STOK00", "AD", StokKodu, ""))
						If StokAdi2 <> "" Then
							Degisken = Degisken & vbcrlf & StokAdi2
							eksatir = eksatir+1
						End If
						
						If Aciklama <> "" Then
							Degisken = Degisken & " - " & Aciklama & " - SipNo:" & MSipArtNo
							'eksatir = eksatir+1
						Else
							If MSipArtNo <> "" Then 
								Degisken = Degisken & " - " & "SipNo:" & MSipArtNo
								'eksatir = eksatir+1
							End If
						End If
					End If		
				Case Else
				Degisken = ST60.GetFieldValue(DegiskenAdi, SatirNo)
			End Select
			
			res = pe1.setalign("LEFT")
			If Grid(i,4) = "N" Then
				res = pe1.SetAlign("RIGHT")
				Ondalik = Grid(i,6)
				Degisken = Doc.numformat(degisken, grid(i,1), grid(i,6))
			ElseIf Grid(i,4) = "D" Then
				Degisken = Doc.StrtoNordate(Degisken)
			End If
			
			If Trim(stokkodu) = "3-02-02-0554" Then
				If degiskenadi="SF_MIKTAR" Then
					degisken=ST60.SF_MIKTAR(satirno) * Doc.Stok00_toUnit(stokkodu,ST60.SF_SF_UNIT(satirno),"TAKIM",-1)
				Elseif degiskenadi="SF_SF_UNIT" Then
					degisken="TAKIM"
				End If
			End If
			'If degiskenadi="_AD" Then msgbox Degisken
 			res = pe1.write(satirpozr, Sol, (eksatir*satiryuksekligi)+satiryuksekligi, SutunGenisligi-2, Trim(Degisken))
			Sol = Sol + SutunGenisligi
			j = j + 1
		Next					 
		sonSatir = pe1.GetBottom()

End Sub
'-------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Sub Print_form_sayfasonu(pe1)
	res = pe1.Setalign("LEFT")
	not1 = Trim(STOK60E.NOTES_1(1))
	not2 = Trim(STOK60E.NOTES_2(1))
	not3 = Trim(STOK60E.NOTES_3(1))
	not4 = Trim(STOK60E.NOTES_3(1))
	Sofor = STOK60E.BOX1_2
	PlakaNo = STOK60E.BOX1_3

	'res = pe1.SetTransparentMode(0)
	res = pe1.SetBkgColor(RGB(255,0,0))

	Genislik = solmargin2+150
	res = pe1.write(250, solmargin2, 5, Genislik, not1)
	res = pe1.write(pe1.GetBottom+0.5, solmargin2, 5, Genislik, not2)
	'res = pe1.write(pe1.GetBottom+0.5, solmargin2, 5, Genislik, not3)
	'res = pe1.write(pe1.GetBottom+0.5, solmargin2, 5, Genislik, not4)
	'res = pe1.write(pe1.GetBottom+1.5, solmargin2, 5, 50, Sofor)
	'res = pe1.write(pe1.GetBottom+0.5, solmargin2, 5, 30, PlakaNo)
End Sub

Sub Alanlar()
	ReDim Grid(6,7)
	Grid(0,0) = "_SIRA"
	Grid(1,0) = "KOD"
	Grid(2,0) = "_AD"
	Grid(3,0) = "SF_MIKTAR"
	Grid(4,0) = "_BOS"
	Grid(5,0) = "SF_SF_UNIT"
	
	
	Grid(0,1) = 9
	Grid(1,1) = 30
	Grid(2,1) = 57
	Grid(3,1) = 25
	Grid(4,1) = 5
	Grid(5,1) = 10
	Grid(6,1) = 26
	
	Grid(0,4) = "C"
	Grid(1,4) = "C"
	Grid(2,4) = "C"
	Grid(3,4) = "N"
	Grid(4,4) = "C"
	Grid(5,4) = "C"
	Grid(6,4) = "C"
	
	For i = 0 To UBound(Grid,1)
		If Grid(i,4) = "N" Then
			Degisken = Grid(i,0)
			ondalik = ST60.MaxSigDig(Degisken)	
			Grid(i,6) = Ondalik		
		End If
	Next	
End Sub

Sub PrintForm()   
	Dim pe1
	Set pe1 = doc.CreatePrintEngineOutput()
	res = pe1.Open(0,"MPS Listeleri",297,210,"")
	satirSayisi = MMPS30T.GetRecCount()
	For i=1 To satirSayisi
		StokKodu = MMPS30T.MAMULSTOKKODU(i)
		Gk3 = Doc.GetDBFieldValue("STOK00", "GK_3", StokKodu, "")
		if Doc.Is_EY1(MMPS30T.SELECTED(i)) and trim(Gk3) <> "FS" then
			satir = satir + 1
			if satir > 1 then res = pe1.PageBreak
			rows = Doc.SelectEQ("MMPS10E","EVRAKNO",MMPS30T.EVRAKNO(i))
			Set MMPS10E = Doc.GetTableObject("AU_MMPS10E_SELECT")

			rows = Doc.SelectEQ("MMPS10T","EVRAKNO",MMPS30T.EVRAKNO(i))
			Set MMPS10T = Doc.GetTableObject("AU_MMPS10T_SELECT")
			Call PrintForm1(pe1)
		end if
	Next	
	res = pe1.Show()
	res = doc.ActivateAfterMacro(1)
End Sub

Sub PrintForm1(pe1)   
	BaslangicSayfaNo = pe1.GetPageCount
	OperasyonSatirNo = 0
	Call SayfaBasi(pe1)
	satirSayisi = MMPS10T.GetRecCount()
	For i=1 To satirSayisi
		Call SatirYazdir(pe1,i)
	Next	
	BitisSayfaNo = pe1.GetPageCount
	sayfaSayisi = BitisSayfaNo - BaslangicSayfaNo + 1
	For i=1 To sayfaSayisi 
		res = pe1.GoToPage(BaslangicSayfaNo + i - 1)
		Baslik = i &  " / " & sayfaSayisi	
		res = pe1.SetBold(0)
		res = pe1.SetAlign("CENTER")
		res = pe1.WriteRTF(282,10,6,190,"- \b Sayfa : \b0  " & Baslik & " -") 	   
	Next   
End Sub

Sub SayfaBasi(pe1)
  	res = pe1.SelectFont("Arial",14)
  	res = pe1.SetBold(1)
  	res = pe1.SetAlign("LEFT")
  	res = pe1.Box(15,10,12,113)		
	res = pe1.Write(18,12,10,100,"ÝÞ PLANI")	
	res = pe1.PenSize(0.3)

  	res = pe1.SelectFont("Arial",10)
  	res = pe1.SetBold(1)
  	res = pe1.SetAlign("LEFT")
	logo = Doc.GetD7Directory_DataFirm & Doc.Customp_Gets("!LOGO")
	res = pe1.Nopen()
	res = pe1.Picture(14,155,15,75,logo, 114)
	
	MPSRefNo = MMPS10E.EVRAKNO(1)
	UretimEmriNo = MMPS10E.URETIMSIPINO(1)
	MamulKodu = MMPS10E.MAMULSTOKKODU(1)
	MamulAdi = doc.GetKartValue("STOK00","AD",MamulKodu,-1)
	PaketSayisi = MMPS10E.SF_PAKETSAYISI(1)
	PaketIcerigi = MMPS10E.SF_PAKETICERIGI(1)
	PaketIcerigi = Doc.NumFormat(PaketIcerigi,20,0)
	ToplamMiktar = MMPS10E.SF_TOPLAMMIKTAR(1)
	ToplamMiktar = FormatNumber(ToplamMiktar,0,,,-1)
	Birim = doc.GetKartValue("STOK00","IUNIT",MamulKodu,-1)
	ToleransTipi = MMPS10E.TOLERANSTYPE(1)
	Tolerans = MMPS10E.TOLERANS(1)
	MusteriKodu = MMPS10E.MUSTERIKODU(1)
	MusteriAdi = Doc.GetDBFieldValue("CARI00","AD",MusteriKodu,"")
	UretimdenTeslimTarihi = MMPS10E.REVURETIMDENTESTARIH(1)
	UretimdenTeslimTarihi = Doc.StrToNorDate(UretimdenTeslimTarihi)
	
	rBaslangic = 30
	satirYuksekligi = 7
	
'	res = pe1.SetBkgColor(RGB(205,205,205))
	res = pe1.SetTransparentMode(0)
	res = pe1.Box(rBaslangic - 2,10,78 - rBaslangic + 2,30)
	res = pe1.SetTransparentMode(1)
	res = pe1.SetBkgColor(RGB(255,255,255))

	Blok10 = 12
	Blok11 = Blok10 + 35
	res = pe1.SetBold(1)
	res = pe1.Write(rBaslangic,12,7,28,"MPS Refer. No")
	res = pe1.SetBold(0)
	res = pe1.Write(rBaslangic,Blok11,6,30,trim(MPSRefNo))
	res = pe1.SetBold(1)
	res = pe1.Write(rBaslangic+satirYuksekligi,12,7,28,"Grup No")
	res = pe1.SetBold(0)
	res = pe1.Write(rBaslangic+satirYuksekligi,Blok11,7,30,trim(GrupNo))
	res = pe1.SetBold(1)
	res = pe1.Write(rBaslangic+2*satirYuksekligi,12,7,30,"YM / Mamul Kodu")
	res = pe1.SetBold(0)
	res = pe1.Write(rBaslangic+2*satirYuksekligi,Blok11,6,150,trim(MamulKodu))

	res = pe1.SetBold(1)
	res = pe1.Write(rBaslangic+3*satirYuksekligi,12,7,30,"Mamul Adý")
	res = pe1.SetBold(0)
	res = pe1.Write(rBaslangic+3*satirYuksekligi,Blok11,6,120 - Blok11,trim(MamulAdi))
	
	res = pe1.SetBold(1)
	res = pe1.Write(rBaslangic+4*satirYuksekligi,12,7,30,"Mamul Miktarý")
	res = pe1.SetBold(0)
	If trim(Tolerans) <> 0 Then
		strTolerans = "    Tolerans : " & trim(trim(ToleransTipi) & " % " & trim(Tolerans))
	Else
		strTolerans = ""
	End If
	res = pe1.Write(rBaslangic+4 * satirYuksekligi,Blok11,6,150, trim(ToplamMiktar) & " " & trim(Birim) & strTolerans)

	res = pe1.SetBold(1)
	res = pe1.Write(rBaslangic+5 * satirYuksekligi,12,7,30,"Üretim Tarihi")
	res = pe1.SetBold(0)
	res = pe1.Write(rBaslangic+5 * satirYuksekligi,Blok11,6,150,UretimdenTeslimTarihi)

	res = pe1.SetBold(1)
	res = pe1.Write(rBaslangic+6 * satirYuksekligi,12,6,30,"Müþteri")
	enalt = pe1.GetBottom()
	res = pe1.SetBold(0)
	res = pe1.Write(rBaslangic+6 * satirYuksekligi,Blok11,6,150,trim(MusteriAdi))
	res = pe1.NoPen()
	resim = doc.GetDBFieldValue("STOK0P","FULLFILENAME1",MamulKodu+"000001","EVRAKNO+TRNUM")

	res = pe1.Picture(rBaslangic - 2,125,Enalt - rBaslangic + 2,75,trim(resim), 82)		
	res = pe1.PenSize(0.3)					
	res = pe1.Box(rBaslangic - 2,10,Enalt - rBaslangic + 2,113)		
	For i = 1 To 6
		res = pe1.Line(rBaslangic + i * satirYuksekligi - 1.5, Blok11 - 2,0,78)
	Next
End Sub
Sub SatirYazdir(pe1,satirNo)
	satirYuksekligi = 5
	iSatir = 5.25
	satirSonu = 272
	KaynakTipi = MMPS10T.R_KAYNAKTYPE(satirNo)
	If trim(KaynakTipi) = "I" Then
		rSiraNo = trim(MMPS10T.R_SIRANO(SatirNo))
		For j=1 To MMPS10T.GetRecCount()
			rSiraNo1 = trim(MMPS10T.R_SIRANO(j))
			KaynakTipi1 = trim(MMPS10T.R_KAYNAKTYPE(j))
			If rSiraNo1 = rSiraNo And KaynakTipi1 = "H" Then
				n=n+1
			End If
		Next
		sonSatir = pe1.GetBottom() + 10*iSatir
		If sonSatir >= satirSonu Then
			 siraNo = 1
			 res = pe1.PageBreak()
			 Call SayfaBasi(pe1)
			 satirpozr = 85
		Else
			 If satirNo = 1 Or OperasyonSatirNo = 0 Then
				  satirpozr = 85
			 Else
				  satirpozr = pe1.GetBottom() + satiryuksekligi
			 End If
			 siraNo = siraNo + 1
		End If
			
		rSiraNo = MMPS10T.R_SIRANO(SatirNo)
		IsMerkeziKodu = MMPS10T.R_KAYNAKKODU(SatirNo)
		IsMerkeziAdi = doc.GetKartValue("IMLT00","AD",IsMerkeziKodu,-1)
		OperasyonKodu = MMPS10T.R_OPERASYON(SatirNo)
		OperasyonAdi = doc.GetKartValue("IMLT01","AD",OperasyonKodu,-1)
		MamulPaketSayisi = MMPS10T.R_YMK_MPAKET(SatirNo)
		YariMamulPaketSayisi = MMPS10T.R_YMK_YMPAKET(SatirNo)
		YariMamulPaketIcerigi = MMPS10T.R_YMK_YMPAKETICERIGI(SatirNo)
		YariMamulPaketIcerigi = FormatNumber(YariMamulPaketIcerigi,0,,,-1)
		ToplamYariMamulMiktari1 = MMPS10T.R_YMAMULMIKTAR(SatirNo)
		ToplamYariMamulMiktari = Doc.NumFormat(ToplamYariMamulMiktari1,20,0)
		BaslamaTarihi = Doc.StrToNorDate(MMPS10T.R_LATEST_B_TARIH(SatirNo))
		BitisTarihi = Doc.StrToNorDate(MMPS10T.R_LATEST_E_TARIH(SatirNo))
		ToplamSure = MMPS10T.R_MIKTAR0(SatirNo)
		BirimSure = Doc.Div(ToplamSure, ToplamYariMamulMiktari1) * 3600
		AyarSuresi = MMPS10T.R_MIKTAR1(SatirNo) * 60
		ReceteNotu = MMPS10T.RECETENOTU(SatirNo)
		res = pe1.NoPen()
		rSiraNo = MMPS10T.R_SIRANO(SatirNo)
		m = HammaddeSatir(rSiraNo)
		If m > 0 Then
			Boy = 5 * iSatir	+ 3
		Else
			Boy = 6 * iSatir - 2 
		End If 
		
		BaslikBasla = SatirPozr - 3
		ToplamSatir = HammaddeSatir(rSiraNo) + MuhendislikSatir(SatirNo)
		DisBoy = ToplamSatir + 4 * iSatir

		res = pe1.SetTransparentMode(0)
'		res = pe1.SetBkgColor(RGB(118,216,195))
		res = pe1.NoPen()
'		res = pe1.Box(BaslikBasla, 10,Boy,190)
		res = pe1.SetBkgColor(RGB(255,255,255))
		res = pe1.NoPen()
'barkodun arka planý
'		res = pe1.Box(satirPozr -3, 10,Boy,20)

'gerçekleþen kutularý
'		res = pe1.Box(satirpozr + iSatir - 1, 170,6,25)
'		res = pe1.Box(satirpozr + 2 * iSatir - 1,170, 6,25)

		res = pe1.PenSize(0.3)
		If MuhendislikSatir(SatirNo) > 0 Then
			res = pe1.Line(BaslikBasla + Boy,10,0,190)						
		End If

		res = pe1.SetTransparentMode(1)

		res = pe1.SetRotation(270)
		ArtNo = MMPS10T.JOBNO(SatirNo)
		pe1.NoPen()
		res = pe1.SelectFont("Courier New",10)
		res = pe1.Barcode(satirPozr, 12,15,4 * iSatir + 2,"CODE128",ArtNo,"")
		res = pe1.SetRotation(0)

		res = pe1.SelectFont("Arial",10)
		res = pe1.SetBold(1)
		res = pe1.Write(satirpozr,32,6,25,"Ýþ Merkezi")
		res = pe1.SetBold(0)
		res = pe1.Write(satirpozr,60,6,60,": " & trim(IsMerkeziKodu) & " - " & trim(IsMerkeziAdi))

		res = pe1.SetBold(1)
		res = pe1.Write(satirpozr + iSatir,32,6,25,"Operasyon")
		res = pe1.SetBold(0)
		res = pe1.Write(satirpozr + iSatir,60,6,60,": " & trim(OperasyonKodu) & " - " & trim(OperasyonAdi))

		res = pe1.SetBold(1)
		res = pe1.Write(satirpozr + 2*iSatir,32,6,25,"Üretim Miktarý")
		res = pe1.SetBold(0)
		res = pe1.Write(satirpozr + 2*iSatir,60,6,13,": " & ToplamYariMamulMiktari)
		res = pe1.SetAlign("LEFT")
		for i = 1 to 4
			res = pe1.Write(pe1.getTop, pe1.GetRight + 2,6,15,"................")		
		Next
		res = pe1.SetBold(1)
		res = pe1.Write(satirpozr + 3*iSatir,32,6,25,"Ayar Süresi")
		res = pe1.SetBold(0)
		res = pe1.Write(satirpozr + 3*iSatir,60,6,60,": " & Doc.Round(AyarSuresi,1) & " dk")

		if trim(ReceteNotu) <> "" Then
			res = pe1.SetBold(1)
			res = pe1.SetUnderlined(1)
			res = pe1.Write(satirpozr + 4*iSatir,32,6,25,"DÝKKAT")
			res = pe1.Write(satirpozr + 4*iSatir,60,6,60,": " & trim(ReceteNotu))
			res = pe1.SetUnderlined(0)
		End if
		res = pe1.SetBold(1)
		res = pe1.SetAlign("CENTER")
'		res = pe1.Write(satirpozr,145,6,25,"Planlanan")
'		res = pe1.Write(satirpozr,170,6,25,"Gerçekleþen")
'		res = pe1.SetAlign("LEFT")		
'		res = pe1.Write(satirpozr + 3*iSatir,150,6,20,"ONAY   :")

		res = pe1.PenSize(0.3)
'		res = pe1.Line(satirpozr + isatir - 2,145,0,25)
'		res = pe1.Line(satirpozr + isatir - 2,170,0,25)
		Blok20 = 145
		Blok21 = Blok20 + 30

		res = pe1.SetAlign("LEFT")		
		res = pe1.SetBold(1)
		res = pe1.Write(satirpozr,Blok20,6,25,"Baþlama Tarihi")
		res = pe1.SetBold(0)
		res = pe1.Write(satirpozr,Blok21,6,25,": " & BaslamaTarihi)
		
		res = pe1.SetAlign("LEFT")		
		res = pe1.SetBold(1)
		res = pe1.Write(satirpozr + iSatir,Blok20,6,25,"Bitiþ Tarihi")
		res = pe1.SetBold(0)
		res = pe1.Write(satirpozr + iSatir,Blok21,6,25,": " & BitisTarihi)

		res = pe1.SetAlign("LEFT")		
		res = pe1.SetBold(1)
		res = pe1.Write(satirpozr + 2 * iSatir,Blok20,6,25,"Birim Süre")
		res = pe1.SetBold(0)
		res = pe1.Write(satirpozr + 2 * iSatir,Blok21,6,25,": " & Doc.Round(BirimSure,0) & " s")

		res = pe1.SetAlign("LEFT")
		res = pe1.SetBold(1)
		res = pe1.Write(satirpozr + 3 * iSatir,Blok20,6,25, "Toplam Süre")
		res = pe1.SetBold(0)
		res = pe1.Write(satirpozr + 3 * iSatir,Blok21,6,25, ": " & Doc.Round(ToplamSure,2) & " saat")

		res = pe1.SetAlign("LEFT")		

'mühendislik tanýmlarý
		satirPozr = pe1.GetBottom()
		BaslikBitis = satirPozr
		Call MuhendislikTanimiYaz(pe1, SatirPozr, SatirNo)

'		satirPozr = pe1.GetBottom() + 2 * iSatir		
		If MuhendislikSatir(SatirNo) > 0 Then
			HSatirBaslangic = satirpozr + iSatir
		Else
			HSatirBaslangic = BaslikBasla + Boy
		End If
		If m > 0 Then
			res = pe1.Line(HSatirBaslangic,10,0,190)						
		End If 

		HSatirBaslangic = pe1.GetBottom() + 5
		For j=1 To MMPS10T.GetRecCount()
			rSiraNo1 = MMPS10T.R_SIRANO(j)
			KaynakTipi1 = MMPS10T.R_KAYNAKTYPE(j)
			If rSiraNo1 = rSiraNo And KaynakTipi1 = "H" Then
				sonSatir1 = pe1.GetBottom()
				If sonSatir1 >= satirSonu Then
			  		 res = pe1.Box(BaslikBasla,10,sonSatir1 - BaslikBasla+3,190)						
					 siraNo = 1
					 res = pe1.PageBreak()
					 Call SayfaBasi(pe1)
					 satirpozr = 92
					 BaslikBasla = 89
					 HSatirBaslangic = 92
					 k = 0
				End If
				res = pe1.StoreSet(1)
'				If (k Mod 2) = 0 Then
'					res = pe1.NoPen()					
'					res = pe1.SetTransparentMode(0)
'					res = pe1.SetBkgColor(RGB(213,239,215))
'					res = pe1.Box(HSatirBaslangic + k*iSatir - 1,10,6,190)
'				End If
				res = pe1.UseSet(1)

				res = pe1.SelectFont("Arial",10)
				HammaddeKodu = MMPS10T.R_KAYNAKKODU(j)
				res = pe1.Write(HSatirBaslangic + k*iSatir,12,6,35,HammaddeKodu)

				HammaddeAdi = doc.GetKartValue("STOK00","AD",HammaddeKodu,-1)
				res = pe1.Write(HSatirBaslangic + k*iSatir,50,6,80,HammaddeAdi)

				HammaddeMiktar = MMPS10T.R_MIKTAR0(j)
				HammaddeMiktar = FormatNumber(HammaddeMiktar,0,,,-1)
				res = pe1.SelectFont("Courier New",10)
				res = pe1.SetAlign("RIGHT")
				res = pe1.Write(HSatirBaslangic + k*iSatir,155,6,30,HammaddeMiktar)

				HammaddeBirim = doc.GetKartValue("STOK00","IUNIT",HammaddeKodu,-1)
				res = pe1.SelectFont("Courier New",10)
				res = pe1.SetAlign("LEFT")
				res = pe1.Write(HSatirBaslangic + k*iSatir,188,6,10,HammaddeBirim)
				k = k + 1
			End If
			res = pe1.SelectFont("Arial",10)
		Next
		If m > 0 Then
			HSatirSonu = pe1.GetBottom()
		Else
			HSatirSonu = pe1.GetBottom() + 2
		end if
		res = pe1.Box(BaslikBasla,10,HSatirSonu - BaslikBasla+3,190)						
		OperasyonSatirNo = OperasyonSatirNo + 1
	End If 
End Sub
Sub MuhendislikTanimiYaz(pe1, SatirPozr, SatirNo)
	MamulKodu = MMPS10T.R_MAMULKODU(SatirNo)
	YariMamulKodu = MMPS10T.R_YMAMULKODU(SatirNo)
	KaynakTipi = MMPS10T.R_KAYNAKTYPE(SatirNo)
	KaynakKodu = MMPS10T.R_KAYNAKKODU(SatirNo)
	OperasyonKodu = MMPS10T.R_OPERASYON(SatirNo)
	MusteriKodu = MMPS10E.MUSTERIKODU(1)
	MPSNo = MMPS10E.EVRAKNO(1)
	satirYuksekligi	= 7

	res = Doc.Tech10_Select(MamulKodu, YariMamulKodu, KaynakTipi, KaynakKodu, OperasyonKodu,MusteriKodu,MPSNo)
	Set Tech = Doc.GetTableObject("_TECH10T_SELECT")
	SatirSayisi = Tech.GetRecCount()
	If SatirSayisi = 0 Then Exit Sub
	res = pe1.SelectFont("Courier New",10)
	res = pe1.SetBold(1)
	For i = 1 To SatirSayisi
		Tanim = Tech.TSTEXT(i)		
		satirPozr = satirPozr + Satiryuksekligi
		res = pe1.Write(SatirPozr,Solmargin + 30, 5,165,Tanim)
	Next
	res = pe1.SelectFont("Arial",10)
	res = pe1.SetBold(0)
End Sub
Function Muhendisliksatir(SatirNo)
'ttt
'	Doc.Tech10_select(mamulkodu,yarimamulkodu,kaynaktype,kaynakkodu,operasyon,musterikodu,mpsno)
	SatirYuksekligi = 7
	MamulKodu = MMPS10T.R_MAMULKODU(SatirNo)
	YariMamulKodu = MMPS10T.R_YMAMULKODU(SatirNo)
	KaynakTipi = MMPS10T.R_KAYNAKTYPE(SatirNo)
	KaynakKodu = MMPS10T.R_KAYNAKKODU(SatirNo)
	OperasyonKodu = MMPS10T.R_OPERASYON(SatirNo)
	MusteriKodu = MMPS10E.MUSTERIKODU(1)
	MPSNo = MMPS10E.EVRAKNO(1)
	
	res = Doc.Tech10_Select(MamulKodu, YariMamulKodu, KaynakTipi, KaynakKodu, OperasyonKodu,MusteriKodu,MPSNo)

	Set Tech = Doc.GetTableObject("_TECH10T_SELECT")

	MuhendislikSatir = Tech.GetRecCount() * SatirYuksekligi

End Function
Sub HammaddeYaz(pe1, SatirPozr, rSiraNo)
'hhh
	SatirSayisi = MMPS10T.GetRecCount()
	For i = 1 To SatirSayisi
		rSiraNo1 = MMPS10T.R_SIRANO(i)
		If rSiraNo1 = rSiraNo And 	trim(MMPS10T.R_KAYNAKTYPE(i)) = "H"  Then
			SatirPozr = SatirPozr + Satiryuksekligi

			HammaddeKodu = MMPS10T.R_KAYNAKKODU(i)
			HammaddeAdi = Doc.GetKartValue("STOK00","AD",HammaddeKodu,-1)
			HammaddeMiktari = MMPS10T.R_MIKTART(i)
			HammaddeMiktari = Doc.NumFormat(HammaddeMiktari,18,Doc.SigDig(HammaddeMiktari))
			HammaddeBirim = Doc.GetKartValue("STOK00","IUNIT",HammaddeKodu,-1)
		
			res = pe1.SetAlign("LEFT")
			res = pe1.SelectFont("Arial",8)
			res = pe1.Write(SatirPozr,Solmargin + 2, 5,40,HammaddeKodu)
			res = pe1.SelectFont("Arial",10)
			res = pe1.Write(SatirPozr,Solmargin + 44, 5,87,HammaddeAdi)
			res = pe1.SetAlign("RIGHT")
			res = pe1.Write(SatirPozr,Solmargin + 132, 5,15,HammaddeMiktari)
			res = pe1.SetAlign("LEFT")
			res = pe1.Write(SatirPozr,Solmargin + 149, 5,12,Hammaddebirim)
			res = pe1.Write(SatirPozr,Solmargin + 161, 5,30,"..............")

		End If
	Next
End Sub
Function HammaddeSatir(rSiraNo)
'hhh
	SatirYuksekligi = 7
	SatirSayisi = MMPS10T.GetRecCount()
	For i = 1 To SatirSayisi
		rSiraNo1 = MMPS10T.R_SIRANO(i)
		If rSiraNo1 = rSiraNo And 	trim(MMPS10T.R_KAYNAKTYPE(i)) = "H"  Then
			n = n + 1
		End If
	Next
	HammaddeSatir = n * SatirYuksekligi
End Function

Public sayfaTuru, sayfaBoyu, sayfaEni, basla, bitir, sonSatir, satirSonu, ToplamEn , BasilabilirAlan
Public UstMargin, SolMargin, SagMargin, AltMargin, satirYuksekligi

Sub PrintForm()
	Dim satirSayisi,pe1
	sayfaBoyu = 297
	sayfaEni = 210

	ToplamTutar = 0
	ToplamProTutar = 0
	siraNo = 0
	satirBaslangic = 0
	UstMargin = 0
	AltMargin = SayfaBoyu - 20
	SolMargin = 10
	SagMargin = SayfaEni - 13
	satiryuksekligi = 5.5 ' 7.6
	satirBaslangic = UstMargin + 77
'	satirSonu = satirBaslangic + 14 * SatirYuksekligi
	satirSonu = satirBaslangic + 11 * SatirYuksekligi
	
	Set pe1 = doc.CreatePrintEngineOutput()
	res = pe1.Open(0, "Satýnalma Sipariþi", SayfaBoyu, SayfaEni, "LANDSCAPE")

	Call Alanlar()
	If doc.ErrorNumber Then
		MsgBox ("Hata main : " & doc.ErrorNumber)
		Exit Sub
	End If
	siraNo = 0        
	satirSayisi = STOK46T.GetRecCount()
	Call Print_form_sayfabasi(pe1)
	logo2 = Doc.Customp_Gets("!LOGO2")
	logo2 = Doc.getd7Directory_Datafirm & trim(logo2)
	res = pe1.nopen()
	res = pe1.Picture(UstMargin + 173, SolMargin + 120, 30,120,logo2,94)
			res = pe1.Line(UstMargin ,  1 , 0, 0) ' hizalama
	For i = 1 To satirSayisi
			Call Print_form_satir(pe1, i)
	Next
		
	Call Print_form_sayfasonu(pe1)

	res = pe1.Show()
	res = Doc.ActivateAfterMacro(1)
End Sub

		res = pe1.pensize(0.3)
		Call Normal(pe1)
End Sub

Sub Print_form_satir(pe1, satirNo)
	Dim satirpozr
		If trim(STOK46T.KOD(satirNo)) = "" Then Exit Sub
		Degisken = ""
		sonSatir = pe1.GetBottom()
		If sonSatir >= satirSonu Then
			Call Print_form_sayfasonu(pe1)
			siraNo = 1
			res = pe1.PageBreak()
			Call Print_form_sayfabasi(pe1)
			satirpozr = satirBaslangic - 1.5
		Else
			If satirNo = 1 Then
					satirpozr = satirBaslangic - 1.5
			Else
					satirpozr = pe1.GetTop() + satiryuksekligi
			End If
			siraNo = siraNo + 1
		End If		
		res = pe1.selectfont("arial", 9)
		res = pe1.setbold(0)
		Sol = SolMargin + 2
		res = pe1.setalign("LEFT")
			For i=0 To UBound(Grid,1)
				SutunGenisligi = Grid(i,1) 
				DegiskenAdi = Grid(i,0)
				res = pe1.selectfont("arial", 9)

				If left(DegiskenAdi,1)<> "_" Then
					Degisken = trim(cstr(STOK46T.GetFieldValue(DegiskenAdi,SatirNo)))
				Else
					If DegiskenAdi = "_POS" Then
						Degisken = satirNo
					ElseIf DegiskenAdi = "_AD" Then
						res = pe1.selectfont("arial", 8)
						ad = STOK46T.KOD_STOK00_AD(satirNo)
						if trim(STOK46T.KOD_STOK00_AD(satirNo)) = "GÝDER FATURALARI" then ad = STOK46T.NONSTOK_AD(satirNo)
						Degisken = trim(ad)
			
					ElseIf DegiskenAdi = "_MUSTERI" Then
						res = pe1.selectfont("arial", 8)
						stokkodu=STOK46T.KOD(satirNo)
						chkodu=STOK46E.CH_KODU(1)
						'musterikodu1=doc.GetCHStokKodu(stokkodu,chkodu)
						musterikodu=doc.getdbfieldvalue("STOK00","NAME2",stokkodu,"")
						Degisken = trim(musterikodu)

					Else
						res = pe1.selectfont("arial", 9)
						Degisken = ""
					End If
				End If	
				res = pe1.SetAlign("LEFT")
				If Grid(i,4) = "N" Then
					res = pe1.SetAlign("RIGHT")
				ElseIf DegiskenAdi = "_POS" Then
					res = pe1.SetAlign("CENTER")
				Elseif Grid(i,4) = "D" Then
					Degisken = " "	 &	doc.StrtoNordate(Degisken)
				Else
					Degisken = " " & Degisken
				End If
				If Grid(i,5) > 0  Then
					Degisken = Doc.NumFormat(Degisken, 18,Grid(i,5))
				end if
				if DegiskenAdi =  "OR_TUTAR" OR  DegiskenAdi =  "OR_FIYAT" then 
					Degisken =  Degisken &  " " & trim(STOK46T.PRICEUNIT(satirno))
				end if	
				res = pe1.write(satirpozr, Sol, satiryuksekligi, SutunGenisligi - 4, Degisken)
				Sol = Sol + SutunGenisligi
				
			Next					 
		sonSatir = pe1.GetBottom()
End Sub

End Sub

Sub Alanlar()
	ReDim Grid(9,7)
	Grid(0,0) = "_POS"
	Grid(1,0) = "KOD"
	Grid(2,0) = "_AD"
	Grid(3,0) = "_MUSTERI"
	Grid(4,0) = "SF_MIKTAR"
	Grid(5,0) = "SF_SF_UNIT"
	Grid(6,0) = "REV_RTESTARIH"
	Grid(7,0) = "OR_FIYAT"
	Grid(8,0) = "OR_TUTAR"		

	Grid(0,1) = 16
	Grid(1,1) = 36
	Grid(2,1) = 82
	Grid(3,1) = 26
	Grid(4,1) = 22
	Grid(5,1) = 16
	Grid(6,1) = 23
	Grid(7,1) = 26
	Grid(8,1) = 28
	
	Grid(0,2) = "Sýra No"
	Grid(1,2) = "STOK KODU"
	Grid(2,2) = "STOK ADI"
	Grid(3,2) = "STOK ADI2"
	Grid(4,2) = "MÝKTAR"
	Grid(5,2) = "BÝRÝM"
	Grid(6,2) = "TERMÝN"
	Grid(7,2) = "FÝYAT"
	Grid(8,2) = "TUTAR"	


	Grid(0,4) = "T"
	Grid(1,4) = "T"
	Grid(2,4) = "T"
	Grid(3,4) = "T"
	Grid(4,4) = "N"
	Grid(5,4) = "T"
	Grid(6,4) = "D"
	Grid(7,4) = "N"
	Grid(8,4) = "N"

	Grid(0,5) = 0
	Grid(1,5) = 0
	Grid(2,5) = 0
	Grid(3,5) = 0
	Grid(4,5) = 2
	Grid(5,5) = 0
	Grid(6,5) = 0
	Grid(7,5) = 2
	Grid(8,5) = 2

	For i = 0 To UBound(Grid,1)
		If Grid(i,4)="N" Then
			Degisken = Grid(i,0)
			if i=4 or i=5 then
				ondalik=2
			else
			ondalik = STOK46T.MaxSigDig(Degisken)	
			end if
			Grid(i,6) = Ondalik		
		End If
	Next	
	Grid(0,6) = 0
	dTutar = STOK46E.MaxSigDig("OR_ISKONTOLUTOPLAM")
	dKDV = STOK46E.MaxSigDig("OR_KDVLERTOPLAM")
	dKDVDahil = STOK46E.MaxSigDig("OR_KDVDAHILTOPLAM")
	
End Sub
Sub Normal(pe1)
		res = pe1.setbold(0)
		res = pe1.setitalic(0)
		res = pe1.pensize(0.4)
		res = pe1.setbkgcolor(RGB(255, 255, 255))
		res = pe1.setrotation(0)
		res = pe1.settransparentmode(1)
		res = pe1.settextcolor(RGB(0, 0, 0))
		res = pe1.selectfont("Arial", 10)
		res = pe1.setalign("LEFT")
End Sub

Public sayfaBoyu, sayfaEni, sonSatir, satirSonu
Public siraNo, satirBaslangic,UstMargin, AltMargin, sagMargin, solMargin, satirYuksekligi, Y5, sayfaSatirSayisi
Public Grid, xCizgi, sayfaNo
Sub PrintForm()
	siraNo = 0
	sayfaNo = 0 

	sayfaBoyu = 297
	sayfaEni = 210
	UstMargin = 10
	AltMargin = sayfaBoyu - 10
	solMargin = 10
	sagMargin = sayfaEni - 10
	satiryuksekligi = 7
	SiraNo = 0
	sayfaSatirsayisi = 28
	Set pe1 = doc.CreatePrintEngineOutput()

	Call Alanlar()
	res = pe1.Open(0, "Satýnalma Talebi", SayfaBoyu, SayfaEni, "")

	If doc.ErrorNumber Then
		MsgBox ("Hata main : " & doc.ErrorNumber)
		Exit Sub
	End If
	Dim satirSayisi_tableT
	satirSayisi_tableT = STOK47T.GetRecCount()
	Call Print_form_sayfabasi(pe1)
	For I = 1 To satirSayisi_tableT
		Call Print_form_satir(pe1, I)
	Next
		
	Call Print_form_sayfasonu(pe1)

	res = pe1.Show()
	res = Doc.ActivateAfterMacro(1)
End Sub

Sub Print_form_sayfabasi(pe1)
	ReDim xCizgi(0)
	D1 = solMargin + 120
	D2 = solMargin + 145
	Y1 = UstMargin + 21
	Y2 = UstMargin + 28
	Y3 = UstMargin + 35
	Y4 = UstMargin + 42
	Y5 = UstMargin + 44
 
	res = pe1.Box(Y5,solMargin,altMargin - Y5,sagMargin - solMargin)
	res = pe1.nopen()
	logo = Doc.Getd7Directory_DataFirm() & trim(Doc.Customp_Gets("!LOGO"))
	res = pe1.selectfont("Arial", 18)
	res = pe1.setbold(1)
	res = pe1.setalign("CENTER")
	res = pe1.write(UstMargin + 3, solMargin + 60, 15, sagMargin - solMargin - 120, "SATINALMA TALEBÝ")
	res = pe1.setalign("LEFT")
	res = pe1.Picture(Ustmargin + 1, solMargin + 1, Y1 - UstMargin - 2, 55, logo, 114)

	res = pe1.setalign("LEFT")
	res = pe1.selectfont("Arial", "10")
'*******************

	BBasla = UstMargin + 15
	SatirAra = 7
	Genislik10 = 27
	Genislik20 = 30

	FisNo = STOK47E.EVRAKNO(1)
	Tarih = doc.StrtoNordate(STOK47E.TARIH(1))
	TalepEdenBolum = STOK47E.CODEDBOLUM(1)
	TalepEdenBolumAdi = doc.GetGecoValue("DEPT","AD",TalepEdenBolum ,-1)
	TalepEdenKisi = STOK47E.CODEDKISI(1)  
	TalepEden = Doc.GetDBFieldValue("PERS00","AD",TalepEdenKisi,"")

	res = pe1.SetBold(0)
	res = pe1.Write(UstMargin + 3, D2+1, 5,sagMargin - D2 - 2, "FORM KODU :FR048 ")
	res = pe1.Write(pe1.GetTop + 10.5, pe1.GetLeft, 5,sagMargin - pe1.GetLeft - 2, "REVÝZYON :00 ")

	res = pe1.SetBold(1)
	res = pe1.write(Y1 + 1 , SolMargin+1, 6, Genislik10, "ÝSTEÐÝ YAPAN")
	res = pe1.SetBold(0)
	res = pe1.write(pe1.getTop , pe1.getRight + 1, 6, D1 - pe1.getRight - 2, ": " & Trim(TalepEden))
	res = pe1.SetBold(1)
	res = pe1.write(pe1.getTop , D1 + 1, 6, Genislik20, "ÝSTEK NO")
	res = pe1.SetBold(0)
	res = pe1.write(pe1.getTop , pe1.getRight + 1, 6, sagMargin - pe1.getRight - 2, ": " & trim(FisNo))

	res = pe1.SetBold(1)
	res = pe1.write(Y2 + 1, SolMargin+1, 6, Genislik10, "BÖLÜM ADI")
	res = pe1.SetBold(0)
	res = pe1.write(pe1.getTop , pe1.getRight + 1, 6, D1 - pe1.getRight - 2, ": " & Trim(TalepEdenBolumAdi))		
	res = pe1.SetBold(1)
	res = pe1.write(pe1.getTop , D1 + 1, 6, Genislik20, "TARÝH")
	res = pe1.SetBold(0)
	res = pe1.write(pe1.getTop , pe1.getRight + 1, 6, sagMargin - pe1.getRight - 2, ": " & Tarih)				

	res = pe1.SetBold(1)
	res = pe1.write(Y3 + 1 , SolMargin+1, 6, Genislik10, "ONAY")
	res = pe1.SetBold(0)
	res = pe1.write(pe1.getTop , pe1.getRight + 1, 6, D1 - pe1.getRight - 2, ": ")
	res = pe1.SetBold(1)
	res = pe1.write(pe1.getTop , D1 + 1, 6, Genislik20, "ONAY")
	res = pe1.SetBold(0)
	res = pe1.write(pe1.getTop , pe1.getRight + 1, 6, sagMargin - pe1.getRight - 2, ": ")
	
'***********************		
	satirBaslangic = pe1.GetBottom + 18

	res = pe1.setalign("CENTER")
	res = pe1.selectfont("Arial", 10)
	Sol = solmargin + 1
	res = pe1.SetBold(1)
	For i = 0 To UBound(Grid,1)
		SutunGenisligi = Grid(i,1)
		Baslik = Grid(i,2)
		If SutunGenisligi > 0 Then	
			if i = 3 then
				Genislik = Grid(i,1) + Grid(i + 1,1)
				res = pe1.write(satirBaslangic - 14, Sol, 10, Genislik - 2, "ÝSTENÝLEN")
				res = pe1.Line(satirBaslangic - 8,sol - 1, 0, Genislik)
				Ust  = satirBaslangic - 7
			elseif i = 4 then
				Ust  = satirBaslangic - 7
			else
				Ust  = satirBaslangic - 14
			end if
			res = pe1.write(Ust, Sol, 10, SutunGenisligi - 2, Baslik)
			SutunGenisligi = Grid(i,1)
			Sol = Sol + SutunGenisligi
			xCizgi(uBound(xCizgi)) =	Sol - 1
			redim Preserve xCizgi(uBound(xCizgi)+1)
		End If
	Next

'yatay çizgiler
	res = pe1.pensize(0.3)
	res = pe1.Box(UstMargin, solmargin, Y4 - UstMargin, sagMargin - solMargin)
	res = pe1.Line(Y1, solmargin, 0, sagMargin - solMargin)
	res = pe1.Line(Y2, solmargin, 0, sagMargin - solMargin)
	res = pe1.Line(Y3, solmargin, 0, sagMargin - solMargin)
'dikey çizgiler
	res = pe1.Line(UstMargin, D2, Y1 - UstMargin, 0)
	res = pe1.Line(Y1, D1, Y4 - Y1, 0)

End Sub

Sub Print_form_satir(pe1, satirNo)
Dim satirpozr
	'res = pe1.SetTransparentMode(0)
	res = pe1.SetBkgColor(RGB(255,0,0))


	if trim(STOK47T.KOD(satirNo)) = "" then exit sub
	SiraNo = SiraNo + 1
	sonSatir = pe1.GetBottom()
	If SiraNo > sayfaSatirSayisi Then
		Call Print_form_sayfasonu(pe1)
		res = pe1.PageBreak()
		Call Print_form_sayfabasi(pe1)
		satirpozr = satirBaslangic
		SiraNo = 1
		sayfaNo = sayfaNo + 1
	Else
		If satirNo = 1 Then
			satirpozr = satirBaslangic
		Else
			satirpozr = satirBaslangic + (siraNo - 1) * satiryuksekligi
		End If
	End If
		
	res = pe1.StoreSet(1001)
	res = pe1.selectfont("Arial", 10)
	res = pe1.setbold(0)
	Sol = solMargin + 1
	res = pe1.setalign("LEFT")
	For i=0 To UBound(Grid,1)
		SutunGenisligi = Grid(i,1)
		DegiskenAdi = Grid(i,0)
		res = pe1.SetBold(0)
		if DegiskenAdi = "_CINS" then
			StokKodu = STOK47T.KOD(satirNo)
			Stokadi = Doc.GetDBFieldValue("STOK00","AD",StokKodu,"")
			Degisken = trim(StokAdi)
		Elseif DegiskenAdi = "_SN" then
			res = pe1.SetBold(1)
			Degisken = SiraNo + sayfaNo * sayfaSatirSayisi
		else
			Degisken = trim(cstr(STOK47T.GetFieldValue(DegiskenAdi,SatirNo)))
		end if
		
		res = pe1.SetAlign("LEFT")
		If Grid(i,4) = "N" Then
			res = pe1.SetAlign("RIGHT")
			Ondalik = Grid(i,6)
			Degisken = Doc.NumFormat(Degisken, 20, 2)
		ElseIf Grid(i,4) = "D" Then
			Degisken = Doc.StrtoNordate(Degisken)
		ElseIf Grid(i,4) = "CC" Then
			res = pe1.SetAlign("CENTER")
		End If
		
		If DegiskenAdi = "SF_MIKTAR" then
			Birim = Trim(cstr(STOK47T.GetFieldValue("SF_SF_UNIT", SatirNo)))
			Degisken = Doc.NumFormat(trim(cstr(STOK47T.GetFieldValue(DegiskenAdi,SatirNo))),20,2) & " " & Birim
		End If
		
		If SutunGenisligi > 0 Then
			res = pe1.write(satirpozr, Sol, satiryuksekligi, SutunGenisligi - 2, Degisken)
			Sol = Sol + SutunGenisligi
		End If	
		Next					 
	sonSatir = pe1.GetBottom()
End Sub
Sub Print_form_sayfasonu(pe1)

'dikey çizgiler
	cizgiAlt = satirBaslangic - 1 + sayfaSatirSayisi * satirYuksekligi
	res = pe1.pensize(0.3)
	For i = 0 To UBound(xCizgi)- 2
	  LineX = cint(xCizgi(i))
		if i = 3 then
		  res = pe1.Line(satirBaslangic - 8, LineX, cizgiAlt - satirBaslangic + 8, 0)
		else
		  res = pe1.Line(Y5, LineX, cizgiAlt - Y5, 0)
		end if
	Next
	res = pe1.Line(satirBaslangic - 1,solMargin,0,sagMargin - solMargin)
	res = pe1.Line(cizgiAlt,solMargin,0,sagMargin - solMargin)
'yatay çizgiler	
	res = pe1.pensize(0.1)
	for i = 1 to sayfaSatirSayisi
		res = pe1.Line(satirBaslangic - 1 + i * satirYuksekligi,solMargin,0,sagMargin - solMargin)	
	Next
	res = pe1.selectfont("Arial", 10)
	res = pe1.SetBold(1)
	Notlar1 = STOK47E.SIPLEILGILINOTLAR_1
	Notlar2 = STOK47E.SIPLEILGILINOTLAR_2
	res = pe1.Write(cizgiAlt + 1,solMargin + 1,6,35,"GENEL AÇIKLAMA :")
	res = pe1.SetBold(0)
	res = pe1.Write(pe1.GetTop, pe1.GetRight + 1,6,sagmargin - pe1.GetRight - 2,Notlar1)
	res = pe1.Write(pe1.GetTop + satirYuksekligi, pe1.GetLeft,6,sagmargin - pe1.GetLeft - 2,Notlar2)
   res = pe1.Write(AltMargin + 1,sagMargin - 45,6,45,"FR048_Rev.00_11.07.2013")

End Sub

Sub Alanlar()
	ReDim Grid(6,7)
	Grid(0,0) = "_SN"
	Grid(1,0) = "_CINS"
	Grid(2,0) = "KOD"
	Grid(3,0) = "SF_MIKTAR"
	Grid(4,0) = "RTALEPEDILENTESTARIH"
	Grid(5,0) = "_ONAYLANAN"
	Grid(6,0) = "_TAKIP"

	Grid(0,1) = 13
	Grid(1,1) = 67
	Grid(2,1) = 30
	Grid(3,1) = 18
	Grid(4,1) = 20
	Grid(5,1) = 27
	Grid(6,1) = 14

	Grid(0,2) = "SIRA NO"
	Grid(1,2) = "ÝSTENÝLEN MALZEMENÝN TANIMI"
	Grid(2,2) = "STOK KODU"
	Grid(3,2) = "MÝKTAR"
	Grid(4,2) = "TARÝH"
	Grid(5,2) = "ONAYLANAN ADET"
	Grid(6,2) = "Takip"
	
	Grid(0,4) = "CC"
	Grid(1,4) = "C"
	Grid(2,4) = "C"
	Grid(3,4) = "N"
	Grid(4,4) = "D"
	Grid(5,4) = "C"
	Grid(6,4) = "C"

	For i = 0 To UBound(Grid,1) 
		Degisken = Grid(i,0)
		If Grid(i,4)="N" And Left(degisken,1) <> "_" Then
			Degisken = Grid(i,0)
			ondalik = STOK47T.MaxSigDig(Degisken)	
			If Ondalik > 2 Then Ondalik = 2
			Grid(i,6) = Ondalik		
		End If
	Next	
End Sub
Sub Normal(pe1)
	res = pe1.setbold(0)
	res = pe1.setitalic(0)
	res = pe1.pensize(0.4)
	res = pe1.setbkgcolor(RGB(255, 255, 255))
	res = pe1.setrotation(0)
	res = pe1.settransparentmode(1)
	res = pe1.settextcolor(RGB(0, 0, 0))
	res = pe1.selectfont("Arial", 10)
	res = pe1.setalign("left")
End Sub

Form Kaynak Kodu

Public sayfaTuru, sayfaBoyu, sayfaEni, basla, bitir, sonSatir, satirSonu, ToplamEn , BasilabilirAlan
Public UstMargin, SolMargin, SagMargin, AltMargin
Public siraNo, satirBaslangic,Tip, satirAra
Public AlanSayisi(), ToplamTutar, ToplamProTutar
Public xCizgi()
Public Secilenler()
Public Grid()

Sub PrintForm()
	Dim satirSayisi,pe1
	sayfaBoyu = 297
	sayfaEni = 210

	ToplamTutar = 0
	ToplamProTutar = 0
	siraNo = 0
	satirBaslangic = 0
	UstMargin = 15
	AltMargin = SayfaBoyu - 15
	SolMargin = 13
	SagMargin = SayfaEni - 13
	ReDim xCizgi(1)
	
	Set pe1 = doc.CreatePrintEngineOutput()
	res = pe1.Open(0, "Satýþ Sözleþmesi", SayfaBoyu, SayfaEni, "")
	satirSonu = UstMargin + 172
	satirBaslangic = UstMargin + 55

	Call Alanlar()
	If doc.ErrorNumber Then
		MsgBox ("Hata main : " & doc.ErrorNumber)
		Exit Sub
	End If
	siraNo = 0        
	satirSayisi = STOK40T.GetRecCount()
	Call Print_form_sayfabasi(pe1)
	For i = 1 To satirSayisi
			Call Print_form_satir(pe1, i)
	Next
		
	Call Print_form_sayfasonu(pe1)

	res = pe1.Show()
	res = Doc.ActivateAfterMacro(1)
End Sub

Sub Print_form_sayfabasi(pe1)
		res = pe1.nopen()
        
'*******************
		res = pe1.selectfont("Arial", 16)
		res = pe1.setalign("CENTER")
		res = pe1.SetBold(1)
		res = pe1.Write(UstMargin + 5,SolMargin + 47,8,SagMargin - SolMargin - 87,"SATIÞ SÝPARÝÞÝ")
		res = pe1.SetBold(0)
		res = pe1.SetUnderlined(0)
		res = pe1.SelectFont("Arial", 10)

		res = pe1.setalign("LEFT")
		res = pe1.pensize(0.6)
		SiparisNo = STOK40E.EVRAKNO(1)
		Tarih = doc.StrtoNordate(STOK40E.TARIH(1))
		CHKodu = STOK40E.MUSTERIKODU(1)
		CHAdi = doc.GetKartValue("CARI00","AD",CHKodu,-1) 
		Adres1 = doc.GetKartValue("CARI00","ADRES_1",CHKodu,-1) 
		Adres2 = doc.GetKartValue("CARI00","ADRES_2",CHKodu,-1) 
		Adres3 = doc.GetKartValue("CARI00","ADRES_3",CHKodu,-1) 
		VergiDairesi = doc.GetKartValue("CARI00","VERGIDAIRESI",CHKodu,-1) 
		VergiNo = doc.GetKartValue("CARI00","VERGINO",CHKodu,-1) 
		Tel = doc.GetKartValue("CARI00","TELEFONNO_1",CHKodu,-1) 
		Faks = doc.GetKartValue("CARI00","FAXNO",CHKodu,-1) 

		sevkAdres1 = STOK40E.SEVKADRESI_1(1)
		sevkAdres2 = STOK40E.SEVKADRESI_2(1)
		sevkAdres3 = STOK40E.SEVKADRESI_3(1)


'		ilgili = STOK40E.SIPIALAN(1)

		res = pe1.SetBold(1)
		res = pe1.Write(UstMargin+16, SolMargin + 2, 6,26,"Sipariþ No")
		res = pe1.SetBold(0)
		res = pe1.Write(UstMargin+16, SolMargin + 29, 6,50,": " & trim(SiparisNo))

		res = pe1.SetBold(1)
		res = pe1.Write(UstMargin+16, SolMargin + 116, 6,15,"Tarih")
		res = pe1.SetBold(0)
		res = pe1.Write(UstMargin+16, SolMargin + 132, 6,50,": " & Tarih)

		res = pe1.SetBold(1)
		res = pe1.Write(UstMargin+22, SolMargin + 2, 6,28,"Ünvan ve Adres")
		res = pe1.SetBold(0)
		res = pe1.Write(UstMargin+22, SolMargin + 30, 6,60,": " & CHAdi)

		res = pe1.Write(UstMargin+27, SolMargin + 2, 6,87,Adres1)
		res = pe1.Write(UstMargin+32, SolMargin + 2, 6,87,Adres2)
		res = pe1.Write(UstMargin+37, SolMargin + 2, 6,87,Adres3)

		res = pe1.SetBold(1)
		res = pe1.Write(UstMargin+42, SolMargin + 2, 6,19,"V.D./V.No")
		res = pe1.SetBold(0)
		If trim(VergiDairesi) <> "" And trim(VergiNo) <> "" Then
			Bolu = " / "
		Else		
			Bolu = ""
		End If
		res = pe1.Write(UstMargin+42, SolMargin + 22, 6,67,": " & trim(VergiDairesi) & Bolu & trim(VergiNo))

		res = pe1.SetBold(1)
		res = pe1.Write(UstMargin+22, SolMargin + 92, 6,24,"Teslim Adresi")
		res = pe1.Write(UstMargin+22, SolMargin + 116, 6,10,":")
		res = pe1.SetBold(0)
		res = pe1.Write(UstMargin+27, SolMargin + 92, 6,87,sevkAdres1)
		res = pe1.Write(UstMargin+32, SolMargin + 92, 6,87,sevkAdres2)
		res = pe1.Write(UstMargin+37, SolMargin + 92, 6,87,sevkAdres3)
		res = pe1.SetBold(1)
		res = pe1.Write(UstMargin+42, SolMargin + 92, 6,23,"Tel/Faks")
		res = pe1.SetBold(0)
		res = pe1.Write(UstMargin+42, SolMargin + 116, 6,SagMargin - SolMargin - 118,": " & trim(Tel) & " / " & trim(Faks))


'***********************		
		res = pe1.SelectFont("Arial",10)
 		res = pe1.SetAlign("LEFT")
		Sol = SolMargin + 2
		res = pe1.SetBold(1)
		For i = 0 To UBound(Grid,1)
			SutunGenisligi = Grid(i,1)
			Baslik = Grid(i,2)
			res = pe1.write(satirBaslangic - 6, Sol, 10, SutunGenisligi - 4 , Baslik)
			Sol = Sol + SutunGenisligi
		Next
		res = pe1.pensize(0.6)
		Call Normal(pe1)
End Sub

Sub Print_form_satir(pe1, satirNo)
Dim satirpozr, satiryuksekligi
		If trim(STOK40T.KOD(satirNo)) = "" Then Exit Sub
		Degisken = ""
		satiryuksekligi = 6.7
		sonSatir = pe1.GetBottom()
		If sonSatir >= satirSonu Then
			Call Print_form_sayfasonu(pe1)
			siraNo = 1
			res = pe1.PageBreak()
			Call Print_form_sayfabasi(pe1)
			satirpozr = satirBaslangic
		Else
			If satirNo = 1 Then
					satirpozr = satirBaslangic
			Else
'					satirpozr = pe1.GetTop() + satiryuksekligi
					satirpozr = SatirBaslangic + siraNo * satiryuksekligi
			End If
			siraNo = siraNo + 1
		End If		
		res = pe1.selectfont("Arial", 10)
		res = pe1.setbold(0)
		Sol = SolMargin + 2
		res = pe1.setalign("LEFT")
			For i=0 To UBound(Grid,1)
				SutunGenisligi = Grid(i,1) 
				DegiskenAdi = Grid(i,0)
				Degisken = trim(cstr(STOK40T.GetFieldValue(DegiskenAdi,SatirNo)))
				res = pe1.SetAlign("LEFT")
				If Grid(i,4) = "N" Then
					res = pe1.SetAlign("RIGHT")
					Ondalik = Grid(i,6)
					Degisken = Doc.NumFormat(Degisken, 18,Ondalik)
				ElseIf Grid(i,4) = "D" Then
					Degisken = Doc.StrtoNordate(Degisken)
				End If
				res = pe1.write(satirpozr, Sol, satiryuksekligi, SutunGenisligi - 4, Degisken)
				Sol = Sol + SutunGenisligi
				If satirNo = 1 Then
					xCizgi(UBound(xCizgi)-1) = Sol - 2
					ReDim Preserve xCizgi(Ubound(xCizgi)+1)
				End If
			Next					 
		sonSatir = pe1.GetBottom()
End Sub
Sub Print_form_sayfasonu(pe1)

'dýþ çerçeve
	res = pe1.PenSize(0.5)
	res = pe1.box(UstMargin, SolMargin, AltMargin - UstMargin, SagMargin - SolMargin)

'üst çizgiler
	res = pe1.Line(UstMargin + 16, SolMargin, 0, SagMargin - SolMargin)		
	res = pe1.pensize(0.3)
'	res = pe1.Line(UstMargin, SolMargin + 47, 16, 0)
'	res = pe1.Line(UstMargin, SolMargin + 142, 16, 0)
	res = pe1.Line(UstMargin+21, SolMargin + 90, 26.5, 0)

'logolar
	logo = Doc.Getd7Directory_DataFirm() & trim(Doc.Customp_Gets("!LOGO"))

	res = pe1.NoPen()
	res = pe1.Picture(UstMargin + 1, SolMargin + 5, 14,45,logo, 114)

'dikey çizgiler
	res = pe1.pensize(0.3)
	For i = 0 To UBound(Grid,1) - 2
		LineX = xCizgi(i)		
		res = pe1.Line(UstMargin+47.5, LineX, 126, 0)
	Next
'yatay çizgiler
	res = pe1.Line(UstMargin + 21, SolMargin, 0, SagMargin - SolMargin)		
	res = pe1.pensize(0.5)
	res = pe1.Line(UstMargin + 47.5, SolMargin, 0, SagMargin - SolMargin)		
	res = pe1.pensize(0.3)
	res = pe1.Line(UstMargin + 53.5, SolMargin, 0, SagMargin - SolMargin)		
	res = pe1.Line(UstMargin + 173.5, SolMargin, 0, SagMargin - SolMargin)		
	
	res = pe1.SetPenStyle(1)
	res = pe1.PenSize(0.3)
	For k = 1 To 17
		res = pe1.line(UstMargin + 53.5 + k*6.7, SolMargin, 0, SagMargin - SolMargin)			
	Next
	res = pe1.SetPenStyle(0)

'toplam çizgileri
	res = pe1.PenSize(0.3)
	res = pe1.Line(UstMargin + 173.5,xCizgi(2),AltMargin - 40 - UstMargin - 173.5,0)
	res = pe1.Line(UstMargin + 173.5,xCizgi(3),30,0)
	For i = 1 To 5
		res = pe1.Line(UstMargin + 173.5 + i*6,xCizgi(2),0,xCizgi(4) - xCizgi(2))
	Next
	
	res = pe1.PenSize(0.5)
'alt yatay çizgiler
	res = pe1.Line(AltMargin - 40, SolMargin, 0, SagMargin - SolMargin)		
	res = pe1.pensize(0.3)
	res = pe1.Line(AltMargin - 20, SolMargin, 0, SagMargin - SolMargin)		
	res = pe1.Line(AltMargin - 15, SolMargin, 0, SagMargin - SolMargin)		
	res = pe1.Line(AltMargin - 34.5, SagMargin-42.5, 0, 42.5)		
'alt dikey çizgiler
	res = pe1.Line(AltMargin - 40, SolMargin+70, 20, 0)		
	res = pe1.Line(AltMargin - 40, SagMargin-42.5, 20, 0)		
	res = pe1.Line(AltMargin - 20, SolMargin+47.5, 20, 0)		
	res = pe1.Line(AltMargin - 20, SagMargin-75, 20, 0)		


	OdemeSekli = STOK40E.ODEMESEKLI(1)
	OdemeGunu = STOK40E.ODEMEGUNU(1)
	If trim(OdemeSekli) = "SP" Then
		str1 = "Sipariþ tarihinden itibaren"
	ElseIf trim(OdemeSekli) = "TS" Then
		str1 = "Teslim tarihinden itibaren"
	End If
	Odeme = Str1 & " " & trim(OdemeGunu) & " gün"

	Ondalik = Grid(4,6)
	Tutar = STOK40E.OR_ISKONTOLUTOPLAM(1)
	Tutar = Doc.NumFormat(Tutar, 18,Ondalik)
	KDVTutari = STOK40E.OR_KDVLERTOPLAM(1)
	KDVTutari  = Doc.NumFormat(KDVTutari, 18, Ondalik)
	KDVDahilTutar = STOK40E.OR_KDVDAHILTOPLAM(1)
	KDVDahilTutar = Doc.NumFormat(KDVDahilTutar, 18, Ondalik)

	TeslimTarihi = doc.StrToNorDate(STOK40T.RTESTARIH(1))
	Satinalma = STOK40E.SIPIVEREN(1)

	res = pe1.SelectFont("Arial",10)
	
	res = pe1.SetBold(1)
	res = pe1.Write(UstMargin + 187,xCizgi(2) + 2,6,xCizgi(3) - xCizgi(2) - 4 ,"TOPLAM")
	res = pe1.Write(UstMargin + 193,xCizgi(2) + 2,6,xCizgi(3) - xCizgi(2) - 4,"KDV %")
	res = pe1.Write(UstMargin + 199,xCizgi(2) + 2,6,xCizgi(3) - xCizgi(2) - 3,"GENEL TOP.")
	res = pe1.SetUnderlined(1)
	res = pe1.Write(UstMargin + 205,xCizgi(2) + 2,6,xCizgi(4) - xCizgi(2) - 4,"ÖDEME ÞEKLÝ")
	res = pe1.SetUnderlined(0)

	res = pe1.SetAlign("RIGHT")
	res = pe1.SetBold(0)
	res = pe1.Write(UstMargin + 187,xCizgi(3) + 2,6,SagMargin - xCizgi(3) - 4, Tutar)
	res = pe1.Write(UstMargin + 193,xCizgi(3) + 2,6,SagMargin - xCizgi(3) - 4, KDVTutari)
	res = pe1.Write(UstMargin + 199,xCizgi(3) + 2,6,SagMargin - xCizgi(3) - 4, KDVDahilTutar)
	res = pe1.SetAlign("LEFT")
	res = pe1.Write(UstMargin + 211,xCizgi(2) + 2,12,SagMargin - xCizgi(2) - 4, Odeme)

	res = pe1.SetAlign("JUSTIFIED")
	res = pe1.SelectFont("Arial",10)
	satirAra = 4.2
	sBasla = UstMArgin + 180
	res = pe1.SetBold(1)
	res = pe1.Write(sBasla - SatirAra, SolMargin + 2,6,40,"SÖZLEÞME ÞARTLARI :")
	res = pe1.Write(sBasla, SolMargin + 8,6,3,"1")

	res = pe1.SelectFont("Arial",10)
	res = pe1.SetUnderlined(1)
	res = pe1.SetBold(1)
	res = pe1.Write(AltMargin - 39, SolMargin + 2, 6,40,"SÖZLEÞME EKLERÝ")
	res = pe1.SetUnderlined(0)

'	res = pe1.Write(AltMargin - 35, SolMargin + 72, 6,31,"RENK")
	res = pe1.Write(AltMargin - 30, SolMargin + 72, 6,31,"TESLÝM TARÝHÝ")
	res = pe1.Write(AltMargin - 25, SolMargin + 72, 6,31,"TESLÝM ÞEKLÝ")

	TeslimTarihi = doc.StrToNorDate(STOK40T.RTESTARIH(1))
	TeslimSekli = STOK40E.GK_1(1)
	TeslimSekli = Doc.GetGecoValue("ST4GK1","AD",TeslimSekli,-1)
'	TeslimSekli = Doc.GetGecoValue("TK4GK1","AD",TeslimSekli,-1)

	res = pe1.SetBold(0)
'	res = pe1.Write(AltMargin - 35, SolMargin + 104, 6,33,":")
	res = pe1.Write(AltMargin - 30, SolMargin + 104, 6,33,": " & TeslimTarihi)
	res = pe1.Write(AltMargin - 25, SolMargin + 104, 6,33,": " & trim(TeslimSekli))

	res = pe1.SetBold(1)
	res = pe1.Write(AltMargin - 39, SagMargin - 40.5, 6,38,"PLANLAMA ONAYI")
	res = pe1.Write(AltMargin - 19.5, SolMargin + 2, 6,38,"SATIÞ MD. ONAYI")

	SiparisVeren = STOK40E.SIPIVEREN(1)
	SiparisAlan = STOK40E.SIPIALAN(1)

	res = pe1.Write(AltMargin - 19.5, SolMargin + 49.5, 6,38,"SATIÞ TEMSÝLCÝSÝ")
	res = pe1.Write(AltMargin - 14, SolMargin + 49.5, 6,20,"Adý-Soyadý")
	res = pe1.Write(AltMargin - 9.5, SolMargin + 49.5, 6,20,"Tarih")
	res = pe1.Write(AltMargin - 5, SolMargin + 49.5, 6,20,"Ýmza")
	res = pe1.SetBold(0)
	res = pe1.Write(AltMargin - 14, SolMargin + 70, 6,34,": " & trim(SiparisAlan))
	res = pe1.Write(AltMargin - 9.5, SolMargin + 70, 6,34,":")
	res = pe1.Write(AltMargin - 5, SolMargin + 70, 6,34,":")

	res = pe1.SetBold(1)
	res = pe1.Write(AltMargin - 19.5, SagMargin - 73, 6,70,"SÝPARÝÞÝ VEREN FÝRMA YETKÝLÝSÝ")
	res = pe1.Write(AltMargin - 14, SagMargin - 73, 6,20,"Adý-Soyadý")
	res = pe1.Write(AltMargin - 9.5, SagMargin - 73, 6,20,"Tarih")
	res = pe1.Write(AltMargin - 5, SagMargin - 73, 6,20,"Ýmza")
	res = pe1.SetBold(0)
	res = pe1.Write(AltMargin - 14, SagMargin - 52, 6,52,": " & trim(SiparisVeren))
	res = pe1.Write(AltMargin - 9.5, SagMargin - 52, 6,52,":")
	res = pe1.Write(AltMargin - 5, SagMargin - 52, 6,52,":")


	res = pe1.Write(AltMargin +1,SolMargin + 2,6,55,"FR058_Rev.00_11.07.2013")
End Sub

Sub Alanlar()
	ReDim Grid(5,7)
	Grid(0,0) = "KOD"
	Grid(1,0) = "KOD_STOK00_AD"
	Grid(2,0) = "SF_MIKTAR"
	Grid(3,0) = "OR_FIYAT"
	Grid(4,0) = "OR_TUTAR"
	Grid(0,1) = 37
	Grid(1,1) = 70
	Grid(2,1) = 20
	Grid(3,1) = 25
	Grid(4,1) = 30
	Grid(0,2) = "KOD"
	Grid(1,2) = "CÝNSÝ"
	Grid(2,2) = "MÝKTAR"
	Grid(3,2) = "BR. FÝYAT"
	Grid(4,2) = "TUTAR"
	Grid(0,4) = "C"
	Grid(1,4) = "C"
	Grid(2,4) = "N"
	Grid(3,4) = "N"
	Grid(4,4) = "N"
	For i = 0 To UBound(Grid,1)
		If Grid(i,4)="N" Then
			Degisken = Grid(i,0)
			ondalik = STOK40T.MaxSigDig(Degisken)	
			Grid(i,6) = Ondalik		
		End If
	Next	
	Grid(0,6) = 0
	Tutar = STOK40E.OR_ISKONTOLUTOPLAM(1)
	dTutar = Doc.SigDig(Tutar)
	KDVTutari = STOK40E.OR_KDVLERTOPLAM(1)
	dKDV = Doc.SigDig(KDVTutari)
	KDVDahilTutar = STOK40E.OR_KDVDAHILTOPLAM(1)
	dKDVDahil = Doc.SigDig(KDVDahilTutar)
	If dTutar > Grid(4,6) Then
		Grid(4,6) = dTutar
	End If
	If dKDV  > Grid(4,6) Then
		Grid(4,6) = dKDV 
	End If
	If dKDVDahil > Grid(4,6) Then
		Grid(4,6) = dKDVDahil
	End If
End Sub
Sub Normal(pe1)
		res = pe1.setbold(0)
		res = pe1.setitalic(0)
		res = pe1.pensize(0.4)
		res = pe1.setbkgcolor(RGB(255, 255, 255))
		res = pe1.setrotation(0)
		res = pe1.settransparentmode(1)
		res = pe1.settextcolor(RGB(0, 0, 0))
		res = pe1.selectfont("Arial", 10)
		res = pe1.setalign("LEFT")
End Sub
Function FontAyarla(BasilacakAlan,ToplamGen,Font)
	Oran = BasilacakAlan / ToplamGen
	If Oran >= 1 Then
		FontAyarla = Font
	Else
		If oran <=0.8 Then
			FontAyarla = Round(0.8 * Font,0)
		Else
			FontAyarla = Round(Oran * Font,0)
		End If
	End If
End Function

Public sayfaTuru, sayfaBoyu, sayfaEni, basla, bitir, sonSatir, satirSonu, ToplamEn , BasilabilirAlan
Public UstMargin, SolMargin, SagMargin, AltMargin, satirYuksekligi
Public siraNo, satirBaslangic,Tip, satirAra
Public AlanSayisi(), ToplamTutar, ToplamProTutar
Public xCizgi()
Public Secilenler()
Public Grid()

Sub PrintForm()
	Dim satirSayisi,pe1
	sayfaBoyu = 297
	sayfaEni = 210

	ToplamTutar = 0
	ToplamProTutar = 0
	siraNo = 0
	satirBaslangic = 0
	UstMargin = 0
	AltMargin = SayfaBoyu - 20
	SolMargin = 10
	SagMargin = SayfaEni - 13
	satiryuksekligi = 5.5 ' 7.6
	satirBaslangic = UstMargin + 77
'	satirSonu = satirBaslangic + 14 * SatirYuksekligi
	satirSonu = satirBaslangic + 11 * SatirYuksekligi
	
	Set pe1 = doc.CreatePrintEngineOutput()
	res = pe1.Open(0, "Purchase Order", SayfaBoyu, SayfaEni, "LANDSCAPE")

	Call Alanlar()
	If doc.ErrorNumber Then
		MsgBox ("Hata main : " & doc.ErrorNumber)
		Exit Sub
	End If
	siraNo = 0        
	satirSayisi = STOK46T.GetRecCount()
	Call Print_form_sayfabasi(pe1)
	logo2 = Doc.Customp_Gets("!LOGO2")
	logo2 = Doc.getd7Directory_Datafirm & trim(logo2)
	res = pe1.nopen()
	res = pe1.Picture(UstMargin + 173, SolMargin + 120, 30,120,logo2,94)
			res = pe1.Line(UstMargin ,  1 , 0, 0) ' hizalama
	For i = 1 To satirSayisi
			Call Print_form_satir(pe1, i)
	Next
		
	Call Print_form_sayfasonu(pe1)

	res = pe1.Show()
	res = Doc.ActivateAfterMacro(1)
End Sub

Sub Print_form_sayfabasi(pe1)
		res = pe1.nopen()
        
'*******************
		res = pe1.selectfont("arial", 12)
		res = pe1.setalign("CENTER")
		res = pe1.SetBold(1)
		res = pe1.write(UstMargin + 35,SolMargin + 44,8,SagMargin - SolMargin - 87,"PURCHASE ORDER FORM")
		res = pe1.SetBold(0)
		res = pe1.SetUnderlined(0)
		res = pe1.selectfont("Arial", 9)
		res = pe1.setalign("LEFT")
		res = pe1.SetBold(1)
		res = pe1.write(UstMargin+12, SolMargin + 175, 6,100,"BUDORA OTOMOTIV SAN. VE TIC A.S.")
		res = pe1.SetBold(0)
		res = pe1.write(pe1.GetTop + 5, SolMargin + 175, 6,100,"SULTAN ORHAN MAH 1174/2 SOK. NO.3 GEBZE / KOCAELI")
		res = pe1.write(pe1.GetTop + 5, SolMargin + 175, 6,50,"ULUCINAR V.D.188 049 4518")
		res = pe1.write(pe1.GetTop + 5, SolMargin + 175, 6,30,"Tel:0262 646 66 12")
		res = pe1.write(pe1.GetTop , pe1.GetRight, 6,30,"Fax:0262 646 66 92")
		res = pe1.write(pe1.GetTop , pe1.GetRight, 6,40," www.budora.com")
		res = pe1.pensize(0.4)
		SiparisNo = STOK46E.EVRAKNO(1)

		CHAdi = doc.GetKartValue("CARI00","AD",CHKodu,-1) 
		ilgili = doc.GetKartValue("CARI00","KONTAKTNAME_1",CHKodu,-1) 
		Adres1 = doc.GetKartValue("CARI00","ADRES_1",CHKodu,-1) 
		Adres2 = doc.GetKartValue("CARI00","ADRES_2",CHKodu,-1) 
		Adres3 = doc.GetKartValue("CARI00","ADRES_3",CHKodu,-1) 
		Tel = doc.GetKartValue("CARI00","TELEFONNO_1",CHKodu,-1) 
		Faks = doc.GetKartValue("CARI00","FAXNO",CHKodu,-1) 		
				
		res = pe1.SetBold(1)
		res = pe1.write(ustmargin+45,solmargin+5,6,20,"COMPANY")
		res = pe1.SetBold(0)
		res = pe1.write(ustmargin+45,solmargin+22,6,160,": "	& CHAdi)
		res = pe1.SetBold(1)
		res = pe1.write(ustmargin+50,solmargin+5,6,20,"ADDRESS")
		res = pe1.SetBold(0)
		res = pe1.write(ustmargin+50,solmargin+22,6,160,": " & trim(Adres1) & " " & trim(Adres2) & " " & trim(Adres3))
		res = pe1.SetBold(1)
		res = pe1.write(ustmargin+60,solmargin+5,6,25,"TEL.NO")
		res = pe1.SetBold(0)
		res = pe1.write(ustmargin+60,pe1.Getright,6,45,": " & Tel)
		res = pe1.SetBold(1)
		res = pe1.write(ustmargin+60,pe1.Getright,6,20,"FAX NO")
		res = pe1.SetBold(0)
		res = pe1.write(ustmargin+60,pe1.Getright,6,60,": " & Faks) 
		res = pe1.SetBold(1)
		res = pe1.write(ustmargin+45,solmargin+180,6,20,"ORDER NO")
		res = pe1.write(ustmargin+50,pe1.GetLeft,6,25,"ORDER DATE")
		res = pe1.write(ustmargin+55,pe1.GetLeft,6,20,"INQUIRY NO")

		Set A_STOK46T1 = Doc.Select1("__OLDD_re", "STOK46T1", "ARTNO = '"& STOK46T.ARTNO(1) &"'", "", -1)

		if A_STOK46T1.GetRecCount() > 0 then	talep_no = A_STOK46T1.TALEPNO(1)
		res = pe1.SetBold(0)
		res = pe1.write(ustmargin+45,solmargin+210,6,20,": " & trim(SiparisNo))
		res = pe1.write(ustmargin+50,pe1.GetLeft,6,20,": " & Doc.StrToNorDate(STOK46E.TARIH(1)))
		res = pe1.write(ustmargin+55,pe1.GetLeft,6,30,": " & trim(talep_no))

 		res = pe1.SetAlign("LEFT")
		Sol = SolMargin + 4
		res = pe1.SetBold(0)
		ReDim Preserve xCizgi(0)

		For i = 0 To UBound(Grid,1)

			SutunGenisligi = Grid(i,1)
			
			Baslik = Grid(i,2)
		res = pe1.SetBold(1)
			res = pe1.write(satirBaslangic - 7, Sol, 10, SutunGenisligi - 4 , Baslik)
			Sol = Sol + SutunGenisligi
			res = pe1.pensize(0.4)
			res = pe1.Line(pe1.GetTop - 2,  Sol - 2 , 7, 0)
			res = pe1.pensize(0.2)
			res = pe1.Line(pe1.GetTop ,  Sol - 2 , 72, 0)

		Next
			res = pe1.Line(pe1.GetTop - 75 ,  1 , 0, 0) ' hizalama

		res = pe1.SetBold(0)
		res = pe1.pensize(0.3)
		Call Normal(pe1)

End Sub

Sub Print_form_satir(pe1, satirNo)
Dim satirpozr
		If trim(STOK46T.KOD(satirNo)) = "" Then Exit Sub
		Degisken = ""
		sonSatir = pe1.GetBottom()
		If sonSatir >= satirSonu Then
			Call Print_form_sayfasonu(pe1)
			siraNo = 1
			res = pe1.PageBreak()
			Call Print_form_sayfabasi(pe1)
			satirpozr = satirBaslangic - 1.5
		Else
			If satirNo = 1 Then
					satirpozr = satirBaslangic - 1.5
			Else
					satirpozr = pe1.GetTop() + satiryuksekligi
			End If
			siraNo = siraNo + 1
		End If		
		res = pe1.selectfont("arial", 9)
		res = pe1.setbold(0)
		Sol = SolMargin + 2
		res = pe1.setalign("LEFT")
			For i=0 To UBound(Grid,1)
				SutunGenisligi = Grid(i,1) 
				DegiskenAdi = Grid(i,0)
				res = pe1.selectfont("arial", 9)

				If left(DegiskenAdi,1)<> "_" Then
					Degisken = trim(cstr(STOK46T.GetFieldValue(DegiskenAdi,SatirNo)))
				Else
					If DegiskenAdi = "_POS" Then
						Degisken = satirNo
					ElseIf DegiskenAdi = "_AD" Then
						res = pe1.selectfont("arial", 8)
						ad = STOK46T.KOD_STOK00_AD(satirNo)
						if trim(STOK46T.KOD_STOK00_AD(satirNo)) = "GÝDER FATURALARI" then ad = STOK46T.NONSTOK_AD(satirNo)
						Degisken = trim(ad)
			
					ElseIf DegiskenAdi = "_MUSTERI" Then
						res = pe1.selectfont("arial", 8)
						stokkodu=STOK46T.KOD(satirNo)
						chkodu=STOK46E.CH_KODU(1)
						'musterikodu1=doc.GetCHStokKodu(stokkodu,chkodu)
						musterikodu=doc.getdbfieldvalue("STOK00","NAME2",stokkodu,"")
						Degisken = trim(musterikodu)

					Else
						res = pe1.selectfont("arial", 9)
						Degisken = ""
					End If
				End If	
				res = pe1.SetAlign("LEFT")
				If Grid(i,4) = "N" Then
					res = pe1.SetAlign("RIGHT")
				ElseIf DegiskenAdi = "_POS" Then
					res = pe1.SetAlign("CENTER")
				Elseif Grid(i,4) = "D" Then
					Degisken = " "	 &	doc.StrtoNordate(Degisken)
				Else
					Degisken = " " & Degisken
				End If
				If Grid(i,5) > 0  Then
					Degisken = Doc.NumFormat(Degisken, 18,Grid(i,5))
				end if
				if DegiskenAdi =  "OR_TUTAR" OR  DegiskenAdi =  "OR_FIYAT" then 
					Degisken =  Degisken &  " " & trim(STOK46T.PRICEUNIT(satirno))
				end if	
				res = pe1.write(satirpozr, Sol, satiryuksekligi, SutunGenisligi - 4, Degisken)
				Sol = Sol + SutunGenisligi
				
			Next					 
		sonSatir = pe1.GetBottom()
End Sub

Sub Alanlar()
	ReDim Grid(9,7)
	Grid(0,0) = "_POS"
	Grid(1,0) = "KOD"
	Grid(2,0) = "_AD"
	Grid(3,0) = "_MUSTERI"
	Grid(4,0) = "SF_MIKTAR"
	Grid(5,0) = "SF_SF_UNIT"
	Grid(6,0) = "REV_RTESTARIH"
	Grid(7,0) = "OR_FIYAT"
	Grid(8,0) = "OR_TUTAR"		

	Grid(0,1) = 16
	Grid(1,1) = 36
	Grid(2,1) = 82
	Grid(3,1) = 26
	Grid(4,1) = 22
	Grid(5,1) = 16
	Grid(6,1) = 23
	Grid(7,1) = 26
	Grid(8,1) = 28
	
	Grid(0,2) = "No"
	Grid(1,2) = "Stock Code"
	Grid(2,2) = "Stock Name"
	Grid(3,2) = "Stock Name2"
	Grid(4,2) = "Quantity"
	Grid(5,2) = "Unit"
	Grid(6,2) = "Delivery"
	Grid(7,2) = "Price"
	Grid(8,2) = "Amount"	


	Grid(0,4) = "T"
	Grid(1,4) = "T"
	Grid(2,4) = "T"
	Grid(3,4) = "T"
	Grid(4,4) = "N"
	Grid(5,4) = "T"
	Grid(6,4) = "D"
	Grid(7,4) = "N"
	Grid(8,4) = "N"

	Grid(0,5) = 0
	Grid(1,5) = 0
	Grid(2,5) = 0
	Grid(3,5) = 0
	Grid(4,5) = 2
	Grid(5,5) = 0
	Grid(6,5) = 0
	Grid(7,5) = 2
	Grid(8,5) = 2

	For i = 0 To UBound(Grid,1)
		If Grid(i,4)="N" Then
			Degisken = Grid(i,0)
			if i=4 or i=5 then
				ondalik=2
			else
			ondalik = STOK46T.MaxSigDig(Degisken)	
			end if
			Grid(i,6) = Ondalik		
		End If
	Next	
	Grid(0,6) = 0
	dTutar = STOK46E.MaxSigDig("OR_ISKONTOLUTOPLAM")
	dKDV = STOK46E.MaxSigDig("OR_KDVLERTOPLAM")
	dKDVDahil = STOK46E.MaxSigDig("OR_KDVDAHILTOPLAM")
	
End Sub
Sub Normal(pe1)
		res = pe1.setbold(0)
		res = pe1.setitalic(0)
		res = pe1.pensize(0.4)
		res = pe1.setbkgcolor(RGB(255, 255, 255))
		res = pe1.setrotation(0)
		res = pe1.settransparentmode(1)
		res = pe1.settextcolor(RGB(0, 0, 0))
		res = pe1.selectfont("Arial", 10)
		res = pe1.setalign("LEFT")
End Sub

Public sayfaTuru, sayfaBoyu, sayfaEni, basla, bitir, sonSatir, satirSonu, ToplamEn , BasilabilirAlan
Public UstMargin, SolMargin, SagMargin, AltMargin, satirYuksekligi

Public Secilenler()
Public Grid()

Sub PrintForm()
	Dim satirSayisi,pe1
	sayfaBoyu = 297
	sayfaEni = 210

	ToplamTutar = 0
	ToplamProTutar = 0
	siraNo = 0
	satirBaslangic = 0
	UstMargin = 0
	AltMargin = SayfaBoyu - 20
	SolMargin = 10
	SagMargin = SayfaEni - 13
	satiryuksekligi = 5.5 ' 7.6
	satirBaslangic = UstMargin + 77
'	satirSonu = satirBaslangic + 14 * SatirYuksekligi
	satirSonu = satirBaslangic + 11 * SatirYuksekligi
	
	Set pe1 = doc.CreatePrintEngineOutput()
	res = pe1.Open(0, "Purchase Order", SayfaBoyu, SayfaEni, "LANDSCAPE")

	Call Alanlar()
	If doc.ErrorNumber Then
		MsgBox ("Hata main : " & doc.ErrorNumber)
		Exit Sub
	End If
	siraNo = 0        
	satirSayisi = STOK46T.GetRecCount()
	Call Print_form_sayfabasi(pe1)
	logo2 = Doc.Customp_Gets("!LOGO2")
	logo2 = Doc.getd7Directory_Datafirm & trim(logo2)
	res = pe1.nopen()
	res = pe1.Picture(UstMargin + 173, SolMargin + 120, 30,120,logo2,94)
			res = pe1.Line(UstMargin ,  1 , 0, 0) ' hizalama
	For i = 1 To satirSayisi
			Call Print_form_satir(pe1, i)
	Next
		
	Call Print_form_sayfasonu(pe1)

	res = pe1.Show()
	res = Doc.ActivateAfterMacro(1)
End Sub

		res = pe1.nopen()

		res = pe1.setalign("CENTER")

		res = pe1.SetBold(0)
		res = pe1.SetUnderlined(0)
		res = pe1.selectfont("Arial", 9)
		res = pe1.setalign("LEFT")
		res = pe1.SetBold(1)
		res = pe1.write(UstMargin+12, SolMargin + 175, 6,100,"BUDORA OTOMOTIV SAN. VE TIC A.S.")
		res = pe1.SetBold(0)
		res = pe1.write(pe1.GetTop + 5, SolMargin + 175, 6,100,"SULTAN ORHAN MAH 1174/2 SOK. NO.3 GEBZE / KOCAELI")
		res = pe1.write(pe1.GetTop + 5, SolMargin + 175, 6,50,"ULUCINAR V.D.188 049 4518")

		res = pe1.write(pe1.GetTop , pe1.GetRight, 6,30,"Fax:0262 646 66 92")
		res = pe1.write(pe1.GetTop , pe1.GetRight, 6,40," www.budora.com")

		res = pe1.SetBold(1)

		res = pe1.SetBold(1)

		res = pe1.write(ustmargin+60,pe1.Getright,6,45,": " & Tel)
		res = pe1.SetBold(1)

		res = pe1.SetBold(1)
		res = pe1.write(ustmargin+45,solmargin+180,6,20,"ORDER NO")
		res = pe1.write(ustmargin+50,pe1.GetLeft,6,25,"ORDER DATE")
		res = pe1.write(ustmargin+55,pe1.GetLeft,6,20,"INQUIRY NO")

		res = pe1.write(ustmargin+50,pe1.GetLeft,6,20,": " & Doc.StrToNorDate(STOK46E.TARIH(1)))

 		res = pe1.SetAlign("LEFT")

			Baslik = Grid(i,2)

		Next

		Call Normal(pe1)

End Sub

Sub Print_form_satir(pe1, satirNo)
Dim satirpozr
		If trim(STOK46T.KOD(satirNo)) = "" Then Exit Sub
		Degisken = ""
		sonSatir = pe1.GetBottom()
		If sonSatir >= satirSonu Then
			Call Print_form_sayfasonu(pe1)
			siraNo = 1
			res = pe1.PageBreak()
			Call Print_form_sayfabasi(pe1)
			satirpozr = satirBaslangic - 1.5
		Else
			If satirNo = 1 Then
					satirpozr = satirBaslangic - 1.5
			Else
					satirpozr = pe1.GetTop() + satiryuksekligi
			End If
			siraNo = siraNo + 1
		End If		
		res = pe1.selectfont("arial", 9)
		res = pe1.setbold(0)
		Sol = SolMargin + 2
		res = pe1.setalign("LEFT")
			For i=0 To UBound(Grid,1)
				SutunGenisligi = Grid(i,1) 
				DegiskenAdi = Grid(i,0)
				res = pe1.selectfont("arial", 9)

				If left(DegiskenAdi,1)<> "_" Then
					Degisken = trim(cstr(STOK46T.GetFieldValue(DegiskenAdi,SatirNo)))
				Else
					If DegiskenAdi = "_POS" Then
						Degisken = satirNo
					ElseIf DegiskenAdi = "_AD" Then
						res = pe1.selectfont("arial", 8)
						ad = STOK46T.KOD_STOK00_AD(satirNo)
						if trim(STOK46T.KOD_STOK00_AD(satirNo)) = "GÝDER FATURALARI" then ad = STOK46T.NONSTOK_AD(satirNo)
						Degisken = trim(ad)
			
					ElseIf DegiskenAdi = "_MUSTERI" Then
						res = pe1.selectfont("arial", 8)
						stokkodu=STOK46T.KOD(satirNo)
						chkodu=STOK46E.CH_KODU(1)
						'musterikodu1=doc.GetCHStokKodu(stokkodu,chkodu)
						musterikodu=doc.getdbfieldvalue("STOK00","NAME2",stokkodu,"")
						Degisken = trim(musterikodu)

					Else
						res = pe1.selectfont("arial", 9)
						Degisken = ""
					End If
				End If	
				res = pe1.SetAlign("LEFT")
				If Grid(i,4) = "N" Then
					res = pe1.SetAlign("RIGHT")
				ElseIf DegiskenAdi = "_POS" Then
					res = pe1.SetAlign("CENTER")
				Elseif Grid(i,4) = "D" Then
					Degisken = " "	 &	doc.StrtoNordate(Degisken)
				Else
					Degisken = " " & Degisken
				End If
				If Grid(i,5) > 0  Then
					Degisken = Doc.NumFormat(Degisken, 18,Grid(i,5))
				end if
				if DegiskenAdi =  "OR_TUTAR" OR  DegiskenAdi =  "OR_FIYAT" then 
					Degisken =  Degisken &  " " & trim(STOK46T.PRICEUNIT(satirno))
				end if	
				res = pe1.write(satirpozr, Sol, satiryuksekligi, SutunGenisligi - 4, Degisken)
				Sol = Sol + SutunGenisligi
				
			Next					 
		sonSatir = pe1.GetBottom()
End Sub
