Sub Alanlar()
	ReDim Grid(9,7)
	Grid(0,0) = "_POS"
	Grid(1,0) = "KOD"
	Grid(2,0) = "_AD"
	Grid(3,0) = "_MUSTERI"
	Grid(4,0) = "SF_MIKTAR"
	Grid(5,0) = "SF_SF_UNIT"
	Grid(6,0) = "REV_RTESTARIH"
	Grid(7,0) = "OR_FIYAT"
	Grid(8,0) = "OR_TUTAR"		

	Grid(0,1) = 16
	Grid(1,1) = 36
	Grid(2,1) = 82
	Grid(3,1) = 26
	Grid(4,1) = 22
	Grid(5,1) = 16
	Grid(6,1) = 23
	Grid(7,1) = 26
	Grid(8,1) = 28
	
	Grid(0,2) = "No"
	Grid(1,2) = "Stock Code"
	Grid(2,2) = "Stock Name"
	Grid(3,2) = "Stock Name2"
	Grid(4,2) = "Quantity"
	Grid(5,2) = "Unit"
	Grid(6,2) = "Delivery"
	Grid(7,2) = "Price"
	Grid(8,2) = "Amount"	


	Grid(0,4) = "T"
	Grid(1,4) = "T"
	Grid(2,4) = "T"
	Grid(3,4) = "T"
	Grid(4,4) = "N"
	Grid(5,4) = "T"
	Grid(6,4) = "D"
	Grid(7,4) = "N"
	Grid(8,4) = "N"

	Grid(0,5) = 0
	Grid(1,5) = 0
	Grid(2,5) = 0
	Grid(3,5) = 0
	Grid(4,5) = 2
	Grid(5,5) = 0
	Grid(6,5) = 0
	Grid(7,5) = 2
	Grid(8,5) = 2

	For i = 0 To UBound(Grid,1)
		If Grid(i,4)="N" Then
			Degisken = Grid(i,0)
			if i=4 or i=5 then
				ondalik=2
			else
			ondalik = STOK46T.MaxSigDig(Degisken)	
			end if
			Grid(i,6) = Ondalik		
		End If
	Next	
	Grid(0,6) = 0
	dTutar = STOK46E.MaxSigDig("OR_ISKONTOLUTOPLAM")
	dKDV = STOK46E.MaxSigDig("OR_KDVLERTOPLAM")
	dKDVDahil = STOK46E.MaxSigDig("OR_KDVDAHILTOPLAM")
	
End Sub
Sub Normal(pe1)
		res = pe1.setbold(0)
		res = pe1.setitalic(0)
		res = pe1.pensize(0.4)
		res = pe1.setbkgcolor(RGB(255, 255, 255))
		res = pe1.setrotation(0)
		res = pe1.settransparentmode(1)
		res = pe1.settextcolor(RGB(0, 0, 0))
		res = pe1.selectfont("Arial", 10)
		res = pe1.setalign("LEFT")
End Sub

Form Kaynak Kodu
Firma Kodu : BDORA_2014 / Uygulama : PFRM7S
Makro Kodu : STOK63-0
Hangi Tablo için : STOK63
Ylave Hangi Uygulamalar için :

Kod : 
Public sayfaTuru, sayfaBoyu, sayfaEni, sonSatir, satirSonu, sayfaSatirSayisi, ST60,SiparisFaturaNiteligi,sat_rty
Public siraNo, satirBaslangic, satirYuksekligi, SagMargin, solmargin, ustmargin, chkodu
Public Grid()
Sub PrintForm()
	Doc.CreateTableObject "XXX=STOK60T", "TMPCOPY"
	Set ST60 = Doc.GetTableObject("XXX")
	Set S60T = Doc.GetTableObject("STOK63T")
	ST60.CopyTable(S60T)
	ST60.GroupBy2("KOD")

	Dim satirSayisi,pe1
	sat_rty = 0
	siraNo = 0

	sayfaBoyu = 203
	sayfaEni = 271
	satiryuksekligi = 4.3

	Set pe1 = doc.CreatePrintEngineOutput()
	res = pe1.SetConsolePrintOpt(1)
	res = pe1.ConsoleOutput_SetLinesPerPage(66)
	res = pe1.ConsoleOutput_SetCharsPerInch(17)
	res = pe1.Open(0, "Irsaliye", SayfaBoyu, SayfaEni, "")
	ustmargin = 0
	satirBaslangic = ustmargin + 18 * satirYuksekligi
	sayfaSatirSayisi = 30
	solmargin = 0
	sagMargin = SayfaEni - 10

	Call Alanlar()
'	If doc.ErrorNumber Then
'		MsgBox ("Hata main : " & doc.ErrorNumber)
'		Exit Sub
'	End If

	satirSayisi = ST60.GetRecCount()
	Call Print_form_sayfabasi(pe1)
	For i = 1 To satirSayisi
		Call Print_form_satir(pe1, i)
	Next
	Call Print_form_sayfasonu(pe1)

	if Doc.GetDocType0 = "TRACK601" Then
		res = pe1.PrintDoc(1)
	Else
		res = pe1.Show()
	End if
End Sub

Sub Print_form_sayfabasi(pe1)
	res = pe1.selectfont("Courier New", 7)

	chkodu = STOK60E.CH_KODU(1)
	chadi = STOK60E.CH_KODU_CARI00_AD(1)
	if trim(STOK60E.SEVKADRESI_1) = "" then
		adres1 = 	STOK60E.CH_KODU_CARI00_ADRES_1(1)
		adres2 = 	STOK60E.CH_KODU_CARI00_ADRES_2(1)
		adres3 = 	STOK60E.CH_KODU_CARI00_ADRES_3(1)
	else
		adres1 = 	STOK60E.SEVKADRESI_1(1)
		adres2 = 	STOK60E.SEVKADRESI_2(1)
		adres3 = 	STOK60E.SEVKADRESI_3(1)
	end if


	vergidairesi = STOK60E.CH_KODU_CARI00_VERGIDAIRESI(1)
	vergino = STOK60E.CH_KODU_CARI00_VERGINO(1)
	tarih = Doc.StrtoNorDate(STOK60E.TARIH(1))
	if trim(STOK60E.RSEVKTARIH) = "" then
		sevktarih = Tarih
	else
		sevktarih = Doc.StrtoNorDate(STOK60E.RSEVKTARIH)
	end if

	res = pe1.Setalign("LEFT")
	res = pe1.write(ustmargin + 9* satirYuksekligi, solmargin + 165, 5,30, tarih)
	res = pe1.write(ustmargin + 10.5* satirYuksekligi, pe1.GetLeft, 5, 40, sevktarih)

	res = pe1.write(ustmargin + 7 * satirYuksekligi, solmargin + 22, 5, 80, chadi)
	res = pe1.write(ustmargin + 8* satirYuksekligi, solMargin + 22, 5, 80, adres1)
	res = pe1.write(ustmargin + 9 * satirYuksekligi, pe1.GetLeft, 5, 80, adres2)
	res = pe1.write(ustmargin + 10 * satirYuksekligi, pe1.GetLeft, 5, 80, adres3)
	res = pe1.write(ustmargin + 14 * satirYuksekligi, solmargin + 50, 5, 60, vergidairesi & " " & vergino)
End Sub

Sub Print_form_satir(pe1, satirNo)
Dim satirpozr
		If trim(ST60.KOD(satirNo)) = "" Then Exit Sub
		Degisken = ""
		sonSatir = pe1.GetBottom()
		If siraNo > sayfaSatirSayisi Then
			Call Print_form_sayfasonu(pe1)
			siraNo = 0
			res = pe1.PageBreak()
			Call Print_form_sayfabasi(pe1)
			satirpozr = satirBaslangic 

			sat_rty = 0
		End If		

		siraNo = siraNo + 1
		satirpozr = satirBaslangic + ((siraNo - 1) + sat_rty) * satiryuksekligi


'		sat_rty = 0
		res = pe1.selectfont("Courier New", 7)
		res = pe1.setbold(0)
		Sol = solMargin + 7
		For i = 0 To UBound(Grid,1)
			SutunGenisligi = Grid(i,1) 
			DegiskenAdi = Grid(i,0)
			stokkodu = ST60.KOD(satirno)						
			aciklama = ST60.NOTES(satirno)
			MStokKodu = Doc.GetCHStokKodu(stokkodu,chkodu)
			MStokAdi = Doc.GetCHStokAdi(stokkodu,chkodu)
			SipNo = ST60.SIPNO(satirno)
			ArtNo = ST60.SIPARTNO(satirno)
			MSipNo = doc.getdbfieldvalue("STOK40E", "CHSIPNO", sipno, "")
			SiparisFaturaNiteligi = Doc.GetDBFieldValue("STOK40E","MUHT_FATURA_NITELIK",STOK63T.SIPNO(satirno),"") 
			Select Case trim(degiskenAdi)
				case "_SIRA***"
					degisken = siraNo
				case "_AD"
					if trim(MStokKodu) = "" Then
						degisken = Doc.GetDBFieldValue("STOK00", "AD", StokKodu, "")
					Else
						degisken = MStokKodu
					end if
				case else
					Degisken = ST60.GetFieldValue(DegiskenAdi,SatirNo)
			end select
			res = pe1.setalign("LEFT")
			If Grid(i,4) = "N" Then
				res = pe1.SetAlign("RIGHT")
				Ondalik = Grid(i,6)
				Degisken = Doc.numformat(degisken, grid(i,1), grid(i,6))
			ElseIf Grid(i,4) = "D" Then
				Degisken = Doc.StrtoNordate(Degisken)
			End If

			if degiskenadi="SF_SF_UNIT" then
					degisken=Doc.GetDbFieldValue("STOK00","IUNIT",StokKodu,"")
			end if

			if trim(stokkodu) = "3-02-02-0554" then
				if degiskenadi="SF_MIKTAR" then
					degisken=ST60.SF_MIKTAR(satirno) * Doc.Stok00_toUnit(stokkodu,ST60.SF_SF_UNIT(satirno),"TAKIM",-1)
				elseif degiskenadi="SF_SF_UNIT" then
					degisken="TAKIM"
				end if
			end if

			res = pe1.write(satirpozr, Sol , satiryuksekligi, SutunGenisligi - 2, Degisken)

			if 	degiskenadi ="_AD" Then
				res = pe1.write(satirpozr + satiryuksekligi, Sol, satiryuksekligi, SutunGenisligi - 2, trim(Doc.GetDBFieldValue("STOK00", "NAME2", StokKodu, "")))
				sat_rty = sat_rty + 1
				if trim(aciklama) <> "" then
					res = pe1.write(satirpozr + 2 * satiryuksekligi, Sol, satiryuksekligi, SutunGenisligi - 2, trim(aciklama))
					sat_rty = sat_rty + 1
				end if
			end if

			Sol = Sol + SutunGenisligi
			j = j + 1
		Next					 
		sonSatir = pe1.GetBottom()
End Sub
Sub Print_form_sayfasonu(pe1)
	res = pe1.Setalign("LEFT")
	not1 = STOK60E.NOTES_1(1)
	not2 = STOK60E.NOTES_2(1)
	not3 = STOK60E.NOTES_3(1)
	Sofor = STOK60E.BOX1_2
	PlakaNo = STOK60E.BOX1_3

	if trim(SiparisFaturaNiteligi) = "NC" then
		res = pe1.write(UstMargin + 35 * satirYuksekligi, solmargin + 40, 5, 50, "FATURA EDÝLMEYECEKTÝR!....")
	Elseif trim(SiparisFaturaNiteligi) = "Z4" then
		res = pe1.write(UstMargin + 35 * satirYuksekligi, solmargin + 40, 5, 50, "REWORK ÝÞLEM UYGULANMIÞTIR!...")
	End if

	res = pe1.write(UstMargin + 38 * satirYuksekligi, solmargin + 40, 5, 50, Sofor)
	res = pe1.write(pe1.GetTop + 2 * satirYuksekligi, pe1.getLeft, 5, 30, PlakaNo)

	'res = pe1.write(UstMargin + 38 * satirYuksekligi, solmargin + 150, 5, 30, Doc.StrToNorDate(STOK60E.TARIH))
	'res = pe1.write(pe1.GetTop + 2 * satirYuksekligi, pe1.getLeft, 5, 30, Doc.Saat(5))


'	res = pe1.write(215, solmargin, 5, 250, not1)
'	res = pe1.write(220, solmargin, 5, 250, not2)
'	res = pe1.write(225, solmargin, 5, 250, not3)

'	res = pe1.write(240, solmargin + 125, 5, 65, Sofor)
'	res = pe1.write(244, solmargin + 125, 5, 65, PlakaNo)
End Sub

Sub Alanlar()
	ReDim Grid(6,7)
	Grid(0,0) = "_SIRA"
	Grid(1,0) = "KOD"
	Grid(2,0) = "_AD"
	Grid(3,0) = "SF_MIKTAR"
	Grid(4,0) = "_BOS"
	Grid(5,0) = "SF_SF_UNIT"
	Grid(6,0) = "SIPNO"
	
	Grid(0,1) = 9
	Grid(1,1) = 30
	Grid(2,1) = 53
	Grid(3,1) = 25
	Grid(4,1) = 8
	Grid(5,1) = 10
	Grid(6,1) = 26
	
	Grid(0,4) = "C"
	Grid(1,4) = "C"
	Grid(2,4) = "C"
	Grid(3,4) = "N"
	Grid(4,4) = "C"
	Grid(5,4) = "C"
	Grid(6,4) = "C"
	
	For i = 0 To UBound(Grid,1)
		If Grid(i,4) = "N" Then
			Degisken = Grid(i,0)
			ondalik = ST60.MaxSigDig(Degisken)	
			Grid(i,6) = Ondalik		
		End If
	Next	
End Sub

Sub PrintForm()
Dim pe1
	Set pe1 = doc.CreatePrintEngineOutput()
   res = pe1.Open(0,"Satýnalma Sipariþi",297,210,"LANDSCAPE")

'	Set pe1 = doc.CreatePrintEngineOutput()
'	res = pe1.Open(0,"Baþlýk",-1,-1,"")
	ust = 13
	yan = 11
'***
	satirbaslangic=80
	satirno=0
	eurotoplam=0
	tltoplam=0
	Call SayfaBasi(pe1)
	satirSayisi = STOK46T.GetRecCount()
	For i=1 To satirSayisi 
				satirno=satirno+1
		Call SatirYazdir(pe1,i)
	Next
	Call SayfaSonu(pe1)
	res = pe1.Show()
	res = doc.ActivateAfterMacro(0)
End Sub

Sub Satiryazdir(pe1, i)
	
	res = pe1.Setbold(0)

	res = pe1.Selectfont("Arial", 9)
	If satirno = 18 Then
	Call sayfasonu(pe1)
	res=pe1.pagebreak
	satirno=1
	Call sayfabasi(pe1)
	satirbaslangic=80 
End If	

	satirpozr= satirbaslangic + (satirno - 1) * 5
	
	stokkodu=STOK46T.KOD(i)
	res = pe1.write(satirpozr+1,yan+1,4,35,stokkodu)
	stokadi=doc.GetkartValue("STOK00","AD",stokkodu,-1) 
	res = pe1.write(satirpozr+1,yan+37,4,60,stokadi)
	not0=STOK46T.NOTE0(i)
	res = pe1.write(satirpozr+1,yan+100,4,40,not0)
	miktar=Doc.NumFormat_NLZ(STOK46T.SF_MIKTAR(i),25,2)
	res = pe1.setalign("RIGHT")
	res = pe1.write(satirpozr+1,yan+149,4,17,miktar)
	res = pe1.setalign("LEFT")
	miktarbirim=STOK46T.SF_SF_UNIT(i)
	res = pe1.write(satirpozr+1,yan+167,4,10,miktarbirim)
	fiyat=Doc.NumFormat_NLZ(STOK46T.OR_FIYAT(i),15,3)
	res = pe1.setalign("RIGHT")
	res = pe1.write(satirpozr+1,yan+175,4,15,fiyat)
	res = pe1.setalign("LEFT")
	parabirim=STOK46T.PRICEUNIT(i)
	res = pe1.write(satirpozr+1,yan+194,4,10,parabirim)
	tutar=Doc.NumFormat_NLZ(STOK46T.OR_TUTAR(i),15,3)
	res = pe1.setalign("RIGHT")
	res = pe1.write(satirpozr+1,yan+205,4,15,tutar)
	IskontoTutari = Doc.NumFormat_NLZ(STOK46T.OR_TISKONTO_1(i),15,3)
	res = pe1.setalign("RIGHT")
	res = pe1.write(satirpozr+1,yan+221,4,10,IskontoTutari)
	IndTutar=Doc.NumFormat_NLZ(STOK46T.OR_ISKONTOLU_TUTAR(i),15,3)
	res = pe1.setalign("RIGHT")
	res = pe1.write(satirpozr+1,yan+235,4,15,Indtutar)


	'tutar=Doc.NumFormat_NLZ(STOK46T.OR_TUTAR(i),15,3)
	'res = pe1.setalign("RIGHT")
	'res = pe1.write(satirpozr+1,yan+221,4,21,tutar)
	'res = pe1.setalign("LEFT")
	'res = pe1.write(satirpozr+1,yan+242.3,4,10,parabirim)
	testar=Doc.StrToNorDate(STOK46T.REV_RTESTARIH(i))
	res = pe1.setalign("LEFT")
	res = pe1.write(satirpozr+1,yan+257,4,19,testar)

End Sub


Sub SayfaSonu(pe1)

	res = pe1.write(175,yan+75,4,15,"ONAY")
	
	res = pe1.Setbold(1)
	res = pe1.write(185,50,4,125, "SÝPARÝÞÝ ONAYLADIKTAN SONRA LÜTFEN GERÝ FAKSLAYINIZ!")
	
	res = pe1.Selectfont("Arial",8)
	res = pe1.write(185,240,4,40,"Q.Form KSÝ-06-01.4")

	ToplamIskonto = STOK46E.OR_ISKONTOLARTOPLAMI(1)
	NetTutar = STOK46E.OR_ISKONTOLUTOPLAM(1)
		
	res = pe1.write(175,yan+180,4,30,"Ýskonto Toplamý")
	res = pe1.write(175,yan+205,4,10,":")
	res = pe1.setalign("right")

	res = pe1.setalign("left")
	res = pe1.write(180,yan+180,4,80,"Net Tutar")
	res = pe1.write(180,yan+205,4,10,":")
	res = pe1.setalign("right")
	res = pe1.write(180,yan+207,4,20,doc.numformat0(NetTutar,20,4))


End Sub

Public sayfaTuru, sayfaBoyu, sayfaEni, basla, bitir, sonSatir, satirSonu, ToplamEn , BasilabilirAlan
Public siraNo, satirBaslangic,UstMargin, AltMargin, sagMargin, solMargin, satirYuksekligi, not1
Public AlanSayisi()	
Public xCizgi()
Public Grid
Sub PrintForm()
	ToplamEn = 0
	BasilabilirAlan = 0
	siraNo = 0

	sayfaBoyu = 210
	sayfaEni = 280
	UstMargin = 28
	AltMargin = sayfaBoyu - 15
	solMargin = 8
	sagMargin = sayfaEni - 8
	satiryuksekligi = 6
	SiraNo = 0
	Set pe1 = doc.CreatePrintEngineOutput()

	Call Alanlar()
	res = pe1.Open(0, "Satýnalma Formu", SayfaEni ,SayfaBoyu,"LANDSCAPE")

	BasilabilirAlan = SayfaEni - 20
	satirSonu = sayfaBoyu - 87


	If doc.ErrorNumber Then
		MsgBox ("Hata main : " & doc.ErrorNumber)
		Exit Sub
	End If

	Dim satirSayisi_tableT
	satirSayisi_tableT = STOK46T.GetRecCount()
	Call Print_form_sayfabasi(pe1)
	For I = 1 To satirSayisi_tableT
		Call Print_form_satir(pe1, I)
	Next
		
	Call Print_form_sayfasonu(pe1)

	res = pe1.Show()
	res = Doc.ActivateAfterMacro(1)
End Sub

Sub Print_form_sayfabasi(pe1)
	ReDim xCizgi(0)

	res = pe1.StoreSet(1001)
	res = pe1.nopen()
	res = pe1.settransparentmode(0)
'	res = pe1.box(8, 8, 25, SayfaEni-110)
	res = pe1.UseSet(1001)
	res = pe1.RemoveSet(1001)
        
	res = pe1.selectfont("Arial", 10)
	res = pe1.nopen()
	logo = Doc.Getd7Directory_DataFirm() & trim(Doc.Customp_Gets("!LOGO"))
'	FNAMEL0
	res = pe1.selectfont("Arial", 14)
	res = pe1.setbold(1)
	res = pe1.setalign("CENTER")
	res = pe1.write(10, 10, 15, SayfaEni - 20, "SATINALMA SÝPARÝÞ FORMU")
	res = pe1.setalign("LEFT")
	If logo <> "" Then
		res = pe1.Picture(8, 10, 18, -2, logo, 18)
	End If
	res = pe1.selectfont("Helvetica", "12")
	res = pe1.setbold(1)
	res = pe1.setalign("CENTER")
	res = pe1.selectfont("Arial", 12)

	res = pe1.write(UstMargin, 10, 17, 140, "SÝPARÝÞÝ ALAN")
	res = pe1.write(UstMargin, 146, 17, 50, "SÝPARÝÞÝ VEREN")

	res = pe1.setalign("LEFT")
'*******************
	BBasla = UstMargin + 10
	SatirAra = 6
	Genislik10 = 40
	Genislik11 = 120
	Genislik20 = 30
	Genislik21 = 40


	FisNo = STOK46E.EVRAKNO(1)
	Tarih = doc.StrtoNordate(STOK46E.TARIH(1))

'	TalepEdenBolum = STOK46E.CODEDBOLUM(1)
'	TalepEdenBolumAdi = doc.GetGecoValue("DEPT","AD",TalepEdenBolum ,-1)
'	TalepEdenKisi = STOK46E.CODEDKISI(1)  
'	TalepEden = Doc.GetDBFieldValue("PERS00","AD",TalepEdenKisi,"")

	Firmakodu = STOK46E.CH_KODU
	Firmaadi = Doc.GetDBFieldValue("CARI00","AD",Firmakodu,"")
	Firmatel = Doc.GetDBFieldValue("CARI00","TELEFONNO_1",Firmakodu,"")
	Firmafax = Doc.GetDBFieldValue("CARI00","FAXNO",Firmakodu,"")
	Sipalan = STOK46E.SIPIALAN
'	Sipveren = STOK46E.SIPIVEREN
	Sipveren = "DÝLEK AKBUDAK"

	Firmakodu1 = "M000004"
	Firmaadi1 = Doc.GetDBFieldValue("CARI00","AD",Firmakodu1,"")
	Firmatel1 = Doc.GetDBFieldValue("CARI00","TELEFONNO_1",Firmakodu1,"")
	Firmafax1 = Doc.GetDBFieldValue("CARI00","FAXNO",Firmakodu1,"")

	res = pe1.selectfont("Arial", 10)
	res = pe1.SetBold(1)
	res = pe1.write(10, 200, 6, Genislik20, "SÝPARÝÞ NO")
	res = pe1.SetBold(0)
	res = pe1.StoreSet(1)
	res = pe1.SelectFont("Arial", 20)
	res = pe1.write(pe1.getTop - 2, pe1.getRight + 1, 12, Genislik21, ": " & trim(FisNo))
	res = pe1.UseSet(1)
	res = pe1.SetBold(1)
	res = pe1.write(pe1.getBottom+1, 200, satirAra, Genislik20, "SÝPARÝÞ TARÝHÝ")
	res = pe1.SetBold(0)
	res = pe1.write(pe1.getTop , pe1.getRight + 1, satirAra, Genislik21, ": " & Tarih)

	res = pe1.selectfont("Arial", 10)
	res = pe1.SetBold(1)
	res = pe1.write(BBasla , SolMargin+2, satirAra, Genislik10, "FÝRMA ADI")
	res = pe1.SetBold(0)
	res = pe1.write(pe1.getTop , pe1.getRight + 1, satirAra, Genislik11, ": " & Trim(Firmaadi))
	res = pe1.SetBold(1)
	res = pe1.write(pe1.getTop + satirAra, SolMargin+2, satirAra, Genislik10, "ÝLGÝLÝ")
	res = pe1.SetBold(0)
	res = pe1.write(pe1.getTop , pe1.getRight + 1, satirAra, Genislik11, ": " & Trim(Sipalan))
	res = pe1.SetBold(1)
	res = pe1.write(pe1.getTop + satirAra, SolMargin+2, satirAra, Genislik10, "TEL")
	res = pe1.SetBold(0)
	res = pe1.write(pe1.getTop , pe1.getRight + 1, satirAra, Genislik11, ": " & Trim(Firmatel))
	res = pe1.SetBold(1)
	res = pe1.write(pe1.getTop + satirAra, SolMargin+2, satirAra, Genislik10, "FAX")
	res = pe1.SetBold(0)
	res = pe1.write(pe1.getTop , pe1.getRight + 1, satirAra, Genislik11, ": " & Trim(Firmafax))
	res = pe1.SetBold(1)

	res = pe1.SetBold(1)
	res = pe1.write(BBasla, 150, satirAra, Genislik10, "FÝRMA ADI")
	res = pe1.SetBold(0)
	res = pe1.write(pe1.getTop , pe1.getRight + 1, satirAra, sagMargin - pe1.getRight - 1, ": " & Trim(Firmaadi1))
	res = pe1.SetBold(1)
	res = pe1.write(pe1.getTop + satirAra , 150, satirAra, Genislik10, "ÝLGÝLÝ")
	res = pe1.SetBold(0)
	res = pe1.write(pe1.getTop , pe1.getRight + 1, satirAra, sagMargin - pe1.getRight - 1, ": " & Trim(Sipveren))
	res = pe1.SetBold(1)
	res = pe1.write(pe1.getTop + satirAra, 150, satirAra, Genislik10, "TEL")
	res = pe1.SetBold(0)
	res = pe1.write(pe1.getTop , pe1.getRight + 1, satirAra, sagMargin - pe1.getRight - 1, ": " & Trim(Firmatel1))
	res = pe1.SetBold(1)
	res = pe1.write(pe1.getTop + satirAra, 150, satirAra, Genislik10, "FAX")
	res = pe1.SetBold(0)
	res = pe1.write(pe1.getTop , pe1.getRight + 1, satirAra, sagMargin - pe1.getRight - 1, ": " & Trim(Firmafax1))
	res = pe1.SetBold(1)


'***********************		
	satirBaslangic = pe1.GetBottom + 12

	res = pe1.setalign("CENTER")
	res = pe1.selectfont("Arial", 10)
	Sol = solmargin + 2
	For i = 0 To UBound(Grid,1)
		SutunGenisligi = Grid(i,1)
		Baslik = Grid(i,2)
		If SutunGenisligi > 0 Then	
'			if i = 2 then
'				SutunGenisligi = Grid(i,1) + Grid(i+1,1)
'			end if
			res = pe1.write(satirBaslangic - 11, Sol, 10, SutunGenisligi - 4 , Baslik)
				SutunGenisligi = Grid(i,1)
			Sol = Sol + SutunGenisligi
			xCizgi(uBound(xCizgi)) =	Sol - 2
			redim Preserve xCizgi(uBound(xCizgi)+1)
		End If
	Next

	res = pe1.pensize(0.6)
'yatay çizgiler
	res = pe1.pensize(0.4)
'	res = pe1.Line(25, 10, 0, SayfaEni-20)
	res = pe1.Line(satirBaslangic - 12, solmargin, 0, sagMargin - solmargin)
	res = pe1.Line(satirBaslangic - 1, solmargin, 0, sagMargin - solmargin)
	res = pe1.StoreSet(1001)
	res = pe1.PenColor(RGB(0, 0, 0))        
	res = pe1.pensize(0.5)

	Call Normal(pe1)
End Sub

Sub Print_form_satir(pe1, satirNo)
Dim satirpozr
	if trim(STOK46T.KOD(satirNo)) = "" then exit sub
	SiraNo = SiraNo + 1
	sonSatir = pe1.GetBottom()
	If sonSatir >= satirSonu Then
		Call Print_form_sayfasonu(pe1)
		res = pe1.PageBreak()
		Call Print_form_sayfabasi(pe1)
		satirpozr = satirBaslangic
	Else
		If satirNo = 1 Then
			satirpozr = satirBaslangic
		Else
			satirpozr = pe1.GetTop() + satiryuksekligi
		End If
	End If
		
	res = pe1.StoreSet(1001)
	res = pe1.selectfont("Arial Narrow", 9)
	res = pe1.setbold(0)
	Sol = solMargin + 1
	res = pe1.setalign("LEFT")
BOS = ""
	For i=0 To UBound(Grid,1)
		SutunGenisligi = Grid(i,1)
		DegiskenAdi = Grid(i,0)
		res = pe1.SetBold(0)
		if DegiskenAdi = "_CINS" then
			if trim(STOK46T.STOK_NONSTOK(satirNo)) = "NS" Then
				Degisken = trim(STOK46T.NONSTOK_AD(satirNo))
			Else
				StokKodu = STOK46T.KOD(satirNo)
				Stokadi = Doc.GetDBFieldValue("STOK00","AD",StokKodu,"")
				Degisken = trim(StokAdi)
			End if
		Elseif DegiskenAdi = "_TALEPNO" Then
			Set S46T1 = Doc.Select3("sdgfw34gsdh", "STOK46T1", "OR_TRNUM='" & STOK46T.TRNUM(satirNo) & "'", "", -1)
			if S46T1.getRowCount() > 0 Then
				Degisken = trim(S46T1.TALEPNO(1))
			Else
				Degisken = ""
			End if
		Elseif DegiskenAdi = "RTALEPEDILENTESTARIH" then
			if trim(STOK46T.RTALEPEDILENTESTARIH(satirNo)) = bos Then Degisken = trim(STOK46T.RTESTARIH(satirNo))
				Degisken = trim(STOK46T.RTALEPEDILENTESTARIH(satirNo))
		Elseif DegiskenAdi = "_SN" then
			res = pe1.SetBold(1)
			Degisken = SiraNo & "."
		else
			Degisken = STOK46T.GetFieldValue(DegiskenAdi,SatirNo)
		end if

		res = pe1.SetAlign("LEFT")
		If Grid(i,4) = "N" Then
			res = pe1.SetAlign("RIGHT")
			Ondalik = Grid(i,6)
			Degisken = Doc.NumFormat(Degisken, 20,Ondalik)
		ElseIf Grid(i,4) = "D" Then
			Degisken = Doc.StrtoNordate(Degisken)
		End If
		If SutunGenisligi > 0 Then
			res = pe1.write(satirpozr, Sol, satiryuksekligi, SutunGenisligi - 2, Degisken)
			Sol = Sol + SutunGenisligi
		End If	
		Next					 
	sonSatir = pe1.GetBottom()
End Sub
Sub Print_form_sayfasonu(pe1)
	res = pe1.pensize(0.5)
'dýþ çerçeve
	res = pe1.box(UstMargin, solMargin, AltMargin - UstMargin, sagMargin - solMargin)

'dikey çizgiler
	res = pe1.pensize(0.3)
	For i = 0 To UBound(xCizgi)- 2
	  LineX = cint(xCizgi(i))
		If LineX = 0 Then Exit For
'		if i <> 0 and i <> 2 then
		if i <> 0 and i <> 3 and i <> 5 then
		  res = pe1.Line(satirBaslangic - 12, LineX, satirSonu - satirBaslangic + 15, 0)
		end if
	Next
'	res = pe1.Line(pe1.GetBottom,solMargin,0,sagMargin-solMargin)
	res = pe1.Line(UstMargin + 6,solMargin,0,sagMargin-solMargin)
	res = pe1.SetBold(1)
	For i = 1 to 9
		res = pe1.Line(satirBaslangic + i * satirYuksekligi -2 ,solMargin,0,sagMargin-solMargin)		
	Next
	res = pe1.setbold(1)

	not1 = STOK46E.SIPLEILGILINOTLAR_1
	Not1 = trim(not1) & " " & STOK46E.SIPLEILGILINOTLAR_2
	Not1 = trim(not1) & " " & STOK46E.SIPLEILGILINOTLAR_3
	Not1 = trim(not1) & " " & STOK46E.SIPLEILGILINOTLAR_4

	res = pe1.Write(satirSonu+3,solMargin + 1,6,15,"NOT")
	res = pe1.setbold(0)
	res = pe1.Write(pe1.getTop, pe1.GetRight + 1,10,sagMargin - pe1.getRight - 2,": " & trim(Not1))

	res = pe1.setbold(1)
	res = pe1.Line(satirSonu+12 ,solMargin,0,sagMargin-solMargin)		
	res = pe1.selectfont("Arial", 10)
	res = pe1.Write(satirSonu + 12,solMargin + 1,6,sagMargin-solMargin,"DÝKKAT !!!!!!")
	res = pe1.Write(pe1.GetBottom,solMargin + 1,4.5,sagMargin-solMargin,"FATURA ADRESÝ : ÖZNUR KABLO SAN. VE TÝC. A.Þ.")
	res = pe1.Write(pe1.GetBottom,solMargin + 1,4.5,sagMargin-solMargin,"ÞAÝR ZÝYA PAÞA CAD.NO:41/KAT3 KARAKÖY/ÝSTANBUL")
	res = pe1.Write(pe1.GetBottom,solMargin + 1,4.5,sagMargin-solMargin,"BÜYÜK MÜKELLEFLER VD.703 029 15 48")
	res = pe1.Write(pe1.GetBottom,solMargin + 1,4.5,sagMargin-solMargin,"Sipariþ teyidi ayný gün içinde yapýlmalýdýr.")
	res = pe1.setbold(0)
	res = pe1.Write(pe1.GetBottom,solMargin + 1,4.5,sagMargin-solMargin,"Teslimde gecikilmesi halinde mal tutarýnýn %3 ü kadar günlük gecikme cezasý uygulanýr.")
	res = pe1.Write(pe1.GetBottom,solMargin + 1,4.5,sagMargin-solMargin,"Kaþe ve imzalý fax teyidi ile sipariþin kabul olduðu ve sözleþmenin kurulduðu kabul edilir.")
	res = pe1.Write(pe1.GetBottom,solMargin + 1,4.5,sagMargin-solMargin,"Mal gereði gibi ve süresinde teslim edilmediðinde uðranýlacak zarar tazmin edilecektir.")
	res = pe1.Write(pe1.GetBottom,solMargin + 1,4.5,sagMargin-solMargin,"Ödeme:Gelen malzemenin kalite kontrol onayýyla kabulunü müteakip, her hafta Cuma akþamýna kadar Satýnalma bölümüne ulaþan faturalar, takip eden")
	res = pe1.Write(pe1.GetBottom,solMargin + 1,4.5,sagMargin-solMargin,"Salý günkü ödeme listesine alýnýr, aksi durumda bir sonraki haftaya kalýr.")
	res = pe1.Write(pe1.GetBottom+6,solMargin + 1,4.5,sagMargin-solMargin,"NOT : MAL TESLÝMÝ HAFTA ÝÇÝ 16:30 CUMARTESÝ 12:00'e KADARDIR.")
	res = pe1.selectfont("Arial", 11)
	res = pe1.SetAlign("CENTER")
	res = pe1.SetUnderLine(1)
	res = pe1.SetBold(1)
	res = pe1.Write(AltMargin + 1,SolMargin,6,sagMargin-solMargin,"LÜTFEN TEYÝD EDÝNÝZ")
End Sub

Sub Alanlar()
	ReDim Grid(9,7)
	Grid(0,0) = "_SN"
	Grid(1,0) = "KOD"
	Grid(2,0) = "_CINS"
	Grid(3,0) = "SF_MIKTAR"
	Grid(4,0) = "SF_SF_UNIT"
	Grid(5,0) = "OR_FIYAT"
	Grid(6,0) = "PRICEUNIT"
	Grid(7,0) = "REV_RTESTARIH"
	Grid(8,0) = "NOTE0"
	Grid(9,0) = "_TALEPNO"


	Grid(0,1) = 5
	Grid(1,1) = 30
	Grid(2,1) = 80
	Grid(3,1) = 23
	Grid(4,1) = 10
	Grid(5,1) = 20
	Grid(6,1) = 10
	Grid(7,1) = 20
	Grid(8,1) = 45
	Grid(9,1) = 20

	Grid(0,2) = ""
	Grid(1,2) = "STOK KODU"
	Grid(2,2) = "MALZEME CÝNSÝ"
	Grid(3,2) = "MÝKTAR"
	Grid(4,2) = ""
	Grid(5,2) = "BÝRÝM FÝYAT"
	Grid(6,2) = ""
	Grid(7,2) = "TESLÝM TARÝHÝ"
	Grid(8,2) = "SEVK YERÝ"
	Grid(9,2) = "TALEP NO"

	
	Grid(0,4) = "C"
	Grid(1,4) = "C"
	Grid(2,4) = "C"
	Grid(3,4) = "N"
	Grid(4,4) = "C"
	Grid(5,4) = "N"
	Grid(6,4) = "C"
	Grid(7,4) = "D"
	Grid(8,4) = "C"
	Grid(9,4) = "C"

	For i = 0 To UBound(Grid,1) 
		Degisken = Grid(i,0)
		If Grid(i,4)="N" And Left(degisken,1) <> "_" Then
			Degisken = Grid(i,0)
			ondalik = STOK46T.MaxSigDig(Degisken)	
			If Ondalik > 2 Then Ondalik = 2
			Grid(i,6) = Ondalik		
		End If
	Next	
End Sub

Sub Normal(pe1)
		res = pe1.setbold(0)
		res = pe1.setitalic(0)
		res = pe1.pensize(0.4)
		res = pe1.setbkgcolor(RGB(255, 255, 255))
		res = pe1.setrotation(0)
		res = pe1.settransparentmode(1)
		res = pe1.settextcolor(RGB(0, 0, 0))
		res = pe1.selectfont("Arial", 10)
		res = pe1.setalign("left")
End Sub

Public satirbaslangic, satirSayisi, chkodu, satirno, eurotoplam, tltoplam,ustmargin,solmargin,sirano
Sub PrintForm()
Dim pe1
	
	Set pe1 = doc.CreatePrintEngineOutput()
	res = pe1.Open(0,"Baþlýk",-1,-1,"")
	satirbaslangic=100
	satirno=0
	eurotoplam=0
	ustmargin=0
	solmargin=10
	tltoplam=0
	sirano=0
	Call SayfaBasi(pe1)
	satirSayisi = STOK46T.GetRecCount()
	For i=1 To satirSayisi
		satirno=satirno+1
		Call SatirYazdir(pe1,i)
	Next
	Call SayfaSonu(pe1)
	res = pe1.Show()
	res = doc.ActivateAfterMacro(0)
End Sub

Sub Satiryazdir(pe1, i)

	res = pe1.Selectfont("Arial", 9)

	If satirno = 20 Then
	Call sayfasonu(pe1)
	res=pe1.pagebreak
	satirno=1
	Call sayfabasi(pe1)
	satirbaslangic=100
	End If	

	satirpozr= satirbaslangic + (satirno - 1) * 7
	sirano=sirano+1

	stokkodu=STOK46T.KOD(i)
	stokadi=doc.GetkartValue("STOK00","AD",stokkodu,-1) 
	stokv1=STOK46T.SF_VRI_VR_R1(i)
	musteristokkodu=Doc.getdbfieldvalue("STOK0GT", "MSTOKCODE", chkodu & stokkodu, "KOD+STOKCODE")
	miktarbirim=STOK46T.SF_SF_UNIT(i)
	parabirim=STOK46T.PRICEUNIT(i)
	miktar=STOK46T.SF_MIKTAR(i)
	fiyat=STOK46T.OR_FIYAT(i)
	tutar=STOK46T.OR_TUTAR(i)
	Iskontoorani=STOK46T.YISKONTO_1(i)
	testarihi=Doc.StrtonorDate(STOK46T.RTESTARIH(i))
	not1=STOK46T.NOTE0(i)
'satýr bilgileri yazdýr
	res=pe1.Setalign("LEFT")

	res=pe1.write(satirpozr,solmargin+3, 5, 10, sirano)

	res = pe1.Selectfont("Arial", 7)
	res=pe1.write(satirpozr,solmargin+8.5, 5, 31, trim(stokkodu))
	res=pe1.write(satirpozr,solmargin+38, 5, 58, trim(stokadi))
	res=pe1.write(satirpozr,solmargin+95, 5, 35, trim(stokv1))
	res = pe1.Selectfont("Arial", 9)

	res=pe1.write(satirpozr,solmargin+ 174, 5, 20,testarihi)
	res=pe1.write(satirpozr,solmargin+143, 5, 12, miktarbirim)
	res=pe1.write(satirpozr,solmargin+164, 5, 10, parabirim)
	res=pe1.Setalign("RIGHT")
	res=pe1.write(satirpozr,solmargin+ 130, 5, 12, formatnumber(miktar, 2,,,-1))
	res=pe1.write(satirpozr,solmargin+ 146, 5, 17, formatnumber(fiyat, 2, , , -1))
	


	res=pe1.Setalign("LEFT")
	
	Select Case trim(parabirim)
		Case "EURO"
	eurotoplam=eurotoplam+tutar
		Case "TL"
	
	tltoplam=tltoplam+tutar
	End Select


End Sub


Sub SayfaSonu(pe1) 
	res = pe1.Selectfont("Arial", 9)
	res=pe1.Setalign("RIGHT")
	sipveren=STOK46E.SIPIVEREN(1)
	

	geneltoplam=STOK46E.OR_ISKONTOLUTOPLAM(1)

	If trim(eurotoplam) <> 0 Then
	res=pe1.Setalign("LEFT")

	'res=pe1.write(217, 115, 5, 45, "GENEL TOPLAM (EURO)")
	res=pe1.Setalign("RIGHT")

	'res=pe1.write(217, 161, 5, 35, formatnumber(eurotoplam,2, , , -1))
	End If
	If trim(tltoplam)<>0 Then
	res=pe1.Setalign("LEFT")

	'res=pe1.write(212, 115, 5, 45, "GENEL TOPLAM (TL)")
	res=pe1.Setalign("RIGHT")

	'res=pe1.write(212, 161, 5, 35, formatnumber(tltoplam,2, , , -1))
	End If
	res=pe1.Setalign("LEFT")
	res = pe1.Selectfont("Arial", 9)
	not1=STOK46E.SIPLEILGILINOTLAR_1
	not2=STOK46E.SIPLEILGILINOTLAR_2
	not3=STOK46E.SIPLEILGILINOTLAR_3
	not4=STOK46E.SIPLEILGILINOTLAR_4
	res = pe1.write(ustmargin+248,solmargin+30,6,100,not1)
	res = pe1.write(ustmargin+253,solmargin+2,6,100,not2)
	res = pe1.write(ustmargin+258,solmargin+2,6,100,not3)
	res = pe1.write(ustmargin+263,solmargin+2,6,100,not4)
	toplam=STOK46E.OR_NEWTOPLAM(1)
	toplam=Doc.Numformat_NLZ(toplam,18,2)
	parabirimi=STOK46T.PRICEUNIT(1)
	res = pe1.write(ustmargin+233,solmargin+150,6,40,toplam & " " & parabirimi)
	'satýr sonu baþlýk yazdýr
	
	res = pe1.setunderline(1)
	res = pe1.write(ustmargin+233,solmargin+2,6,40,"TEDARÝKÇÝ FÝRMA ONAYI")
	res = pe1.write(ustmargin+233,solmargin+120,6,40,"TOPLAM TUTAR")
	res = pe1.write(ustmargin+248,solmargin+2,6,40,"AÇIKLAMALAR :")
	
	res = pe1.setunderline(0)
'	res = pe1.write(283,solmargin+2, 5, 90,"F/7.4/2-G")
	res=pe1.pensize(0.1)
	res = pe1.setbold(1)
	res = pe1.Selectfont("Arial", 9)
	res = pe1.write(270, solmargin+2, 5, 150, "1-Lütfen Sipariþ tarih ve numaramýzý irsaliyenizin ve faturanýzýn üzerinde belirtiniz.")
	res = pe1.write(274, solmargin+2, 5, 150, "2-Sipariþlerin tarafýmýza teyid edilmesi gerekmektedir.Teyit edilmemiþ sipariþler tarafýnýzca") 
	res = pe1.setunderline(1)
	res = pe1.write(274, solmargin+140, 5, 20, "kabul")
	res = pe1.write(274, solmargin+150, 5, 20, "edilmiþ")
	res = pe1.setunderline(0)

Kod : 
Public sayfaTuru, sayfaBoyu, sayfaEni, basla, bitir, sonSatir, satirSonu, ToplamEn , BasilabilirAlan
Public UstMargin, SolMargin, SagMargin, AltMargin, satirYuksekligi
Public siraNo, satirBaslangic,Tip, satirAra
Public AlanSayisi(), ToplamTutar, ToplamProTutar
Public xCizgi()
Public Secilenler()
Public Grid()

Sub PrintForm()
	Dim satirSayisi,pe1
	sayfaBoyu = 297
	sayfaEni = 210

	ToplamTutar = 0
	ToplamProTutar = 0
	siraNo = 0
	satirBaslangic = 0
	UstMargin = 0
	AltMargin = SayfaBoyu - 20
	SolMargin = 10
	SagMargin = SayfaEni - 13
	satiryuksekligi = 7.6
	satirBaslangic = UstMargin + 84
	satirSonu = satirBaslangic + 14 * SatirYuksekligi
	
	Set pe1 = doc.CreatePrintEngineOutput()
	res = pe1.Open(0, "Satýnalma Sipariþi", SayfaBoyu, SayfaEni, "")

	Call Alanlar()
	If doc.ErrorNumber Then
		MsgBox ("Hata main : " & doc.ErrorNumber)
		Exit Sub
	End If
	siraNo = 0        
	satirSayisi = STOK46T.GetRecCount()
	Call Print_form_sayfabasi(pe1)
	For i = 1 To satirSayisi
			Call Print_form_satir(pe1, i)
	Next
		
	Call Print_form_sayfasonu(pe1)

	res = pe1.Show()
	res = Doc.ActivateAfterMacro(1)
End Sub

Sub Print_form_sayfabasi(pe1)
		res = pe1.nopen()
        
'*******************
		res = pe1.selectfont("arial", 12)
		res = pe1.setalign("CENTER")
		res = pe1.SetBold(1)
		res = pe1.Write(UstMargin + 35,SolMargin + 44,8,SagMargin - SolMargin - 87,"SATINALMA SÝPARÝÞ FORMU")
		res = pe1.SetBold(0)
		res = pe1.SetUnderlined(0)
		res = pe1.selectfont("Arial", 9)
		res = pe1.setalign("LEFT")
		res = pe1.Write(UstMargin+12, SolMargin + 94, 6,100,"SULTAN ORHAN MAH 1174/2 SOK. NO.3 GEBZE / KOCAELÝ")
		res = pe1.Write(UstMargin+17, SolMargin + 94, 6,50,"ULUÇINAR V.D.188 049 4518")
		res = pe1.Write(UstMargin+22, SolMargin + 94, 6,35,"Tel    :0262 646 66 12")
		res = pe1.Write(UstMargin+22, SolMargin + 130, 6,35,"Fax    :0262 646 66 92")
		res = pe1.Write(UstMargin+27, SolMargin + 94, 6,40,"E-mail :info@budora.com")

		res = pe1.pensize(0.6)
		SiparisNo = STOK46E.EVRAKNO(1)
		Tarih = doc.StrtoNordate(STOK46E.TARIH(1))
		CHKodu = STOK46E.CH_KODU(1)
		CHAdi = doc.GetKartValue("CARI00","AD",CHKodu,-1) 
		ilgili = doc.GetKartValue("CARI00","KONTAKTNAME_1",CHKodu,-1) 
		Adres1 = doc.GetKartValue("CARI00","ADRES_1",CHKodu,-1) 
		Adres2 = doc.GetKartValue("CARI00","ADRES_2",CHKodu,-1) 
		Adres3 = doc.GetKartValue("CARI00","ADRES_3",CHKodu,-1) 
		Tel = doc.GetKartValue("CARI00","TELEFONNO_1",CHKodu,-1) 
		Faks = doc.GetKartValue("CARI00","FAXNO",CHKodu,-1) 		
				
		res = pe1.SetBold(0)
		res = pe1.write(ustmargin+45,solmargin+5,6,20,"FÝRMA ADI")
		res = pe1.write(ustmargin+45,solmargin+22,6,60,": "	& CHAdi)
		res = pe1.write(ustmargin+50,solmargin+5,6,20,"ADRESÝ")
		res = pe1.write(ustmargin+50,solmargin+22,6,60,": " & trim(Adres1))
		res = pe1.write(ustmargin+55,solmargin+22,6,60,trim(Adres2))
		res = pe1.write(ustmargin+60,solmargin+22,6,60,trim(Adres3))

		res = pe1.write(ustmargin+45,solmargin+95,6,20,"SÝPARÝÞ NO.")
		res = pe1.write(ustmargin+50,solmargin+95,6,25,"SÝPARÝÞ TARÝHÝ")
		res = pe1.write(ustmargin+55,solmargin+95,6,20,"ÝLGÝLÝ")
		res = pe1.write(ustmargin+60,solmargin+95,6,25,"TELEFON NO.")
		res = pe1.write(ustmargin+65,solmargin+95,6,20,"FAX NO.")

		res = pe1.write(ustmargin+45,solmargin+120,6,20,": " & trim(SiparisNo))
		res = pe1.write(ustmargin+50,solmargin+120,6,20,": " & Doc.StrToNorDate(STOK46E.TARIH(1)))
		res = pe1.write(ustmargin+55,solmargin+120,6,60,": " & trim(ilgili))
		res = pe1.write(ustmargin+60,solmargin+120,6,25,": " & Tel)
		res = pe1.write(ustmargin+65,solmargin+120,6,25,": " & Faks)

'***********************		
 		res = pe1.SetAlign("LEFT")
		Sol = SolMargin + 4
		res = pe1.SetBold(0)
		ReDim Preserve xCizgi(0)

		For i = 0 To UBound(Grid,1)
			SutunGenisligi = Grid(i,1)
			Baslik = Grid(i,2)
			res = pe1.write(satirBaslangic - 10, Sol, 10, SutunGenisligi - 4 , Baslik)
			Sol = Sol + SutunGenisligi
			xCizgi(UBound(xCizgi)) = Sol - 2
				ReDim Preserve xCizgi(Ubound(xCizgi)+1)
		Next
		res = pe1.SetBold(0)
		res = pe1.pensize(0.6)
		Call Normal(pe1)
End Sub

Sub Print_form_satir(pe1, satirNo)
Dim satirpozr
		If trim(STOK46T.KOD(satirNo)) = "" Then Exit Sub
		Degisken = ""
		sonSatir = pe1.GetBottom()
		If sonSatir >= satirSonu Then
			Call Print_form_sayfasonu(pe1)
			siraNo = 1
			res = pe1.PageBreak()
			Call Print_form_sayfabasi(pe1)
			satirpozr = satirBaslangic - 1
		Else
			If satirNo = 1 Then
					satirpozr = satirBaslangic - 1
			Else
					satirpozr = pe1.GetTop() + satiryuksekligi
			End If
			siraNo = siraNo + 1
		End If		
		res = pe1.selectfont("arial", 9)
		res = pe1.setbold(0)
		Sol = SolMargin + 2
		res = pe1.setalign("LEFT")
			For i=0 To UBound(Grid,1)
				SutunGenisligi = Grid(i,1) 
				DegiskenAdi = Grid(i,0)
				res = pe1.selectfont("arial", 9)
				If left(DegiskenAdi,1)<> "_" Then
					Degisken = trim(cstr(STOK46T.GetFieldValue(DegiskenAdi,SatirNo)))

				Else
					If DegiskenAdi = "_POS" Then
						Degisken = satirNo
					ElseIf DegiskenAdi = "_AD" Then
						res = pe1.selectfont("arial", 8)
						ad=STOK46T.KOD_STOK00_AD(satirNo)
						Degisken = trim(ad)
					ElseIf DegiskenAdi = "_MUSTERI" Then
						res = pe1.selectfont("arial", 8)
						stokkodu=STOK46T.KOD(satirNo)
						chkodu=STOK46E.CH_KODU(1)
						'musterikodu1=doc.GetCHStokKodu(stokkodu,chkodu)
						musterikodu=doc.getdbfieldvalue("STOK00","NAME2",stokkodu,"")
						Degisken = trim(musterikodu)
					Else
						res = pe1.selectfont("arial", 9)
						Degisken = ""
					End If
				End If	
				If DegiskenAdi = "PRICEUNIT" Then
					if STOK46T.OR_FIYAT(satirNo) > 0 then
						Degisken = STOK46T.PRICEUNIT(satirNo)
					else
						Degisken = ""
					end if
				end if

				res = pe1.SetAlign("LEFT")
				'If Grid(i,4) = "N" And left(DegiskenAdi,1)<> "_" Then
				'	res = pe1.SetAlign("RIGHT")
				'	Ondalik = Grid(i,6)
				'	Degisken = Doc.NumFormat(Degisken, 18,2)
				If trim(DegiskenAdi) = "OR_FIYAT" or trim(DegiskenAdi) = "OR_TUTAR" then
					res = pe1.SetAlign("RIGHT")
					Ondalik = Grid(i,6)
					Degisken = Doc.NumFormat(Degisken, 18,2)
				ElseIf trim(DegiskenAdi) = "SF_MIKTAR" then
					res = pe1.SetAlign("RIGHT")
					Ondalik = Grid(i,6)
					Degisken = Doc.NumFormat(Degisken, 18,2)
				ElseIf Grid(i,4) = "D" And left(DegiskenAdi,1)<> "_" Then
					Degisken = Doc.StrtoNordate(Degisken)
				ElseIf DegiskenAdi = "_POS" Then
					res = pe1.SetAlign("CENTER")
				End If
				res = pe1.write(satirpozr, Sol, satiryuksekligi, SutunGenisligi - 4, Degisken)
				Sol = Sol + SutunGenisligi
				
			Next					 
		sonSatir = pe1.GetBottom()
End Sub

	Notlar1 = STOK46E.SIPLEILGILINOTLAR_1(1)
	Notlar2 = STOK46E.SIPLEILGILINOTLAR_2(1)
	
End Sub

Sub Alanlar()
	ReDim Grid(10,7)
	Grid(0,0) = "_POS"
	Grid(1,0) = "KOD"
	Grid(2,0) = "_AD"
	Grid(3,0) = "_MUSTERI"
	Grid(4,0) = "SF_MIKTAR"
	Grid(5,0) = "SF_SF_UNIT"
	Grid(6,0) = "REV_RTESTARIH"
	Grid(7,0) = "OR_FIYAT"
	Grid(8,0) = "PRICEUNIT"
	Grid(9,0) = "OR_TUTAR"		

	Grid(0,1) = 10
	Grid(1,1) = 31
	Grid(2,1) = 30
	Grid(3,1) = 29
	Grid(4,1) = 18
	Grid(5,1) = 11
	Grid(6,1) = 20
	Grid(7,1) = 13
	Grid(8,1) = 12
	Grid(9,1) = 15
	
	Grid(0,2) = "Sýra No"
	Grid(1,2) = "STOK KODU"
	Grid(2,2) = "STOK ADI"
	Grid(3,2) = "STOK ADI2"
	Grid(4,2) = "MÝKTAR"
	Grid(5,2) = "BR"
	Grid(6,2) = "TERMÝN"
	Grid(7,2) = "FÝYAT"
	Grid(8,2) = ""
	Grid(9,2) = "TUTAR"	

	Grid(0,4) = "C"
	Grid(1,4) = "C"
	Grid(2,4) = "C"
	Grid(3,4) = "C"
	Grid(4,4) = "N"
	Grid(5,4) = "C"
	Grid(6,4) = "D"
	Grid(7,4) = "N"
	Grid(8,4) = "C"
	Grid(9,4) = "N"
	Grid(10,4) = "C"

	For i = 0 To UBound(Grid,1)
		If Grid(i,4)="N" Then
			Degisken = Grid(i,0)
			if i=4 or i=5 then
				ondalik=2
			else
			ondalik = STOK46T.MaxSigDig(Degisken)	
			end if
			Grid(i,6) = Ondalik		
		End If
	Next	
	Grid(0,6) = 0
	dTutar = STOK46E.MaxSigDig("OR_ISKONTOLUTOPLAM")
	dKDV = STOK46E.MaxSigDig("OR_KDVLERTOPLAM")
	dKDVDahil = STOK46E.MaxSigDig("OR_KDVDAHILTOPLAM")
	
End Sub
Sub Normal(pe1)
		res = pe1.setbold(0)
		res = pe1.setitalic(0)
		res = pe1.pensize(0.4)
		res = pe1.setbkgcolor(RGB(255, 255, 255))
		res = pe1.setrotation(0)
		res = pe1.settransparentmode(1)
		res = pe1.settextcolor(RGB(0, 0, 0))
		res = pe1.selectfont("Arial", 10)
		res = pe1.setalign("LEFT")
End Sub

Public sayfaTuru, sayfaBoyu, sayfaEni, basla, bitir, sonSatir, satirSonu, ToplamEn , BasilabilirAlan
Public UstMargin, SolMargin, SagMargin, AltMargin, satirYuksekligi
Public siraNo, satirBaslangic,Tip, satirAra
Public AlanSayisi(), ToplamTutar, ToplamProTutar
Public xCizgi()
Public Secilenler()
Public Grid()

Sub PrintForm()
	Dim satirSayisi,pe1
	sayfaBoyu = 297
	sayfaEni = 210

	ToplamTutar = 0
	ToplamProTutar = 0
	siraNo = 0
	satirBaslangic = 0
	UstMargin = 0
	AltMargin = SayfaBoyu - 20
	SolMargin = 10
	SagMargin = SayfaEni - 13
	satiryuksekligi = 7.6
	satirBaslangic = UstMargin + 84
'	satirSonu = satirBaslangic + 14 * SatirYuksekligi
	satirSonu = satirBaslangic + 11 * SatirYuksekligi
	
	Set pe1 = doc.CreatePrintEngineOutput()
	res = pe1.Open(0, "Satýnalma Sipariþi", SayfaBoyu, SayfaEni, "")

	Call Alanlar()
	If doc.ErrorNumber Then
		MsgBox ("Hata main : " & doc.ErrorNumber)
		Exit Sub
	End If
	siraNo = 0        
	satirSayisi = STOK46T.GetRecCount()
	Call Print_form_sayfabasi(pe1)
	For i = 1 To satirSayisi
			Call Print_form_satir(pe1, i)
	Next
		
	Call Print_form_sayfasonu(pe1)

	res = pe1.Show()
	res = Doc.ActivateAfterMacro(1)
End Sub

Sub Print_form_sayfabasi(pe1)
		res = pe1.nopen()
        
'*******************
		res = pe1.selectfont("arial", 12)
		res = pe1.setalign("CENTER")
		res = pe1.SetBold(1)
		res = pe1.Write(UstMargin + 35,SolMargin + 44,8,SagMargin - SolMargin - 87,"SATINALMA SÝPARÝÞ FORMU")
		res = pe1.SetBold(0)
		res = pe1.SetUnderlined(0)
		res = pe1.selectfont("Arial", 9)
		res = pe1.setalign("LEFT")
		res = pe1.Write(UstMargin+12, SolMargin + 94, 6,100,"SULTAN ORHAN MAH 1174/2 SOK. NO.3 GEBZE / KOCAELÝ")
		res = pe1.Write(UstMargin+17, SolMargin + 94, 6,50,"ULUÇINAR V.D.188 049 4518")
		res = pe1.Write(UstMargin+22, SolMargin + 94, 6,35,"Tel    :0262 646 66 12")
		res = pe1.Write(UstMargin+22, SolMargin + 130, 6,35,"Fax    :0262 646 66 92")
		res = pe1.Write(UstMargin+27, SolMargin + 94, 6,40,"E-mail :info@budora.com")
		res = pe1.pensize(0.6)
		SiparisNo = STOK46E.EVRAKNO(1)
		Tarih = doc.StrtoNordate(STOK46E.TARIH(1))
		CHKodu = STOK46E.CH_KODU(1)
		CHAdi = doc.GetKartValue("CARI00","AD",CHKodu,-1) 
		ilgili = doc.GetKartValue("CARI00","KONTAKTNAME_1",CHKodu,-1) 
		Adres1 = doc.GetKartValue("CARI00","ADRES_1",CHKodu,-1) 
		Adres2 = doc.GetKartValue("CARI00","ADRES_2",CHKodu,-1) 
		Adres3 = doc.GetKartValue("CARI00","ADRES_3",CHKodu,-1) 
		Tel = doc.GetKartValue("CARI00","TELEFONNO_1",CHKodu,-1) 
		Faks = doc.GetKartValue("CARI00","FAXNO",CHKodu,-1) 		
				
		res = pe1.SetBold(0)
		res = pe1.write(ustmargin+45,solmargin+5,6,20,"FÝRMA ADI")
		res = pe1.write(ustmargin+45,solmargin+22,6,60,": "	& CHAdi)
		res = pe1.write(ustmargin+50,solmargin+5,6,20,"ADRESÝ")
		res = pe1.write(ustmargin+50,solmargin+22,6,60,": " & trim(Adres1))
		res = pe1.write(ustmargin+55,solmargin+22,6,60,trim(Adres2))
		res = pe1.write(ustmargin+60,solmargin+22,6,60,trim(Adres3))

		res = pe1.write(ustmargin+45,solmargin+95,6,20,"SÝPARÝÞ NO.")
		res = pe1.write(ustmargin+50,solmargin+95,6,25,"SÝPARÝÞ TARÝHÝ")
		res = pe1.write(ustmargin+55,solmargin+95,6,20,"ÝLGÝLÝ")
		res = pe1.write(ustmargin+60,solmargin+95,6,25,"TELEFON NO.")
		res = pe1.write(ustmargin+65,solmargin+95,6,20,"FAX NO.")

		res = pe1.write(ustmargin+45,solmargin+120,6,20,": " & trim(SiparisNo))
		res = pe1.write(ustmargin+50,solmargin+120,6,20,": " & Doc.StrToNorDate(STOK46E.TARIH(1)))
		res = pe1.write(ustmargin+55,solmargin+120,6,60,": " & trim(ilgili))
		res = pe1.write(ustmargin+60,solmargin+120,6,25,": " & Tel)
		res = pe1.write(ustmargin+65,solmargin+120,6,25,": " & Faks)

'***********************		
 		res = pe1.SetAlign("LEFT")
		Sol = SolMargin + 4
		res = pe1.SetBold(0)
		ReDim Preserve xCizgi(0)

		For i = 0 To UBound(Grid,1)
			SutunGenisligi = Grid(i,1)
			Baslik = Grid(i,2)
			res = pe1.write(satirBaslangic - 10, Sol, 10, SutunGenisligi - 4 , Baslik)
			Sol = Sol + SutunGenisligi
			xCizgi(UBound(xCizgi)) = Sol - 2
				ReDim Preserve xCizgi(Ubound(xCizgi)+1)
		Next
		res = pe1.SetBold(0)
		res = pe1.pensize(0.6)
		Call Normal(pe1)
End Sub

Sub Print_form_satir(pe1, satirNo)
Dim satirpozr
		If trim(STOK46T.KOD(satirNo)) = "" Then Exit Sub
		Degisken = ""
		sonSatir = pe1.GetBottom()
		If sonSatir >= satirSonu Then
			Call Print_form_sayfasonu(pe1)
			siraNo = 1
			res = pe1.PageBreak()
			Call Print_form_sayfabasi(pe1)
			satirpozr = satirBaslangic - 1
		Else
			If satirNo = 1 Then
					satirpozr = satirBaslangic - 1
			Else
					satirpozr = pe1.GetTop() + satiryuksekligi
			End If
			siraNo = siraNo + 1
		End If		
		res = pe1.selectfont("arial", 9)
		res = pe1.setbold(0)
		Sol = SolMargin + 2
		res = pe1.setalign("LEFT")
			For i=0 To UBound(Grid,1)
				SutunGenisligi = Grid(i,1) 
				DegiskenAdi = Grid(i,0)
				res = pe1.selectfont("arial", 9)
				If left(DegiskenAdi,1)<> "_" Then
					Degisken = trim(cstr(STOK46T.GetFieldValue(DegiskenAdi,SatirNo)))
				Else
					If DegiskenAdi = "_POS" Then
						Degisken = satirNo
					ElseIf DegiskenAdi = "_AD" Then
						res = pe1.selectfont("arial", 8)
						ad=STOK46T.NONSTOK_AD(satirNo)
						Degisken = trim(ad)
					ElseIf DegiskenAdi = "_MUSTERI" Then
						res = pe1.selectfont("arial", 8)
						notlar=STOK46T.NOTE0(satirNo)						
						Degisken = trim(notlar)
	
					Else
						res = pe1.selectfont("arial", 9)
						Degisken = ""
					End If
				End If	
				res = pe1.SetAlign("LEFT")
				'If Grid(i,4) = "N" And left(DegiskenAdi,1)<> "_" Then
				'	res = pe1.SetAlign("RIGHT")
				'	Ondalik = Grid(i,6)
				'	Degisken = Doc.NumFormat(Degisken, 18,2)
				If trim(DegiskenAdi) = "OR_FIYAT" or trim(DegiskenAdi) = "OR_TUTAR" then
					res = pe1.SetAlign("RIGHT")
					Ondalik = Grid(i,6)
					Degisken = Doc.NumFormat(Degisken, 18,2)
				ElseIf trim(DegiskenAdi) = "SF_MIKTAR" then
					res = pe1.SetAlign("RIGHT")
					Ondalik = Grid(i,6)
					Degisken = Doc.NumFormat(Degisken, 18,2)
				ElseIf Grid(i,4) = "D" And left(DegiskenAdi,1)<> "_" Then
					Degisken = Doc.StrtoNordate(Degisken)
				ElseIf DegiskenAdi = "_POS" Then
					res = pe1.SetAlign("CENTER")
				End If
				res = pe1.write(satirpozr, Sol, satiryuksekligi, SutunGenisligi - 4, Degisken)
				Sol = Sol + SutunGenisligi
				
			Next					 
		sonSatir = pe1.GetBottom()
End Sub

	res = pe1.SetAlign("LEFT")

	res = pe1.Write(sBaslangic ,solmargin + 175,6,10, STOK46E.PRICEUNIT(1))
	res = pe1.Write(sBaslangic +sYuksekligi ,solmargin + 175,6,10, STOK46E.PRICEUNIT(1))
	res = pe1.Write(sBaslangic + 2 * sYuksekligi ,solmargin + 175,6,10, STOK46E.PRICEUNIT(1))
	res = pe1.Write(sBaslangic + 3 * sYuksekligi ,solmargin + 175,6,10,STOK46E.PRICEUNIT(1))
	
	Notlar1 = STOK46E.SIPLEILGILINOTLAR_1(1)
	Notlar2 = STOK46E.SIPLEILGILINOTLAR_2(1)
	
End Sub

Sub Alanlar()
	ReDim Grid(9,7)
	Grid(0,0) = "_POS"
	Grid(1,0) = "_AD"
	Grid(2,0) = "_MUSTERI"
	Grid(3,0) = "SF_MIKTAR"
	Grid(4,0) = "SF_SF_UNIT"
	Grid(5,0) = "REV_RTESTARIH"
	Grid(6,0) = "OR_FIYAT"
	Grid(7,0) = "PRICEUNIT"
	Grid(8,0) = "OR_TUTAR"		

	Grid(0,1) = 10
	Grid(1,1) = 41
	Grid(2,1) = 41
	Grid(3,1) = 18
	Grid(4,1) = 11
	Grid(5,1) = 20
	Grid(6,1) = 18
	Grid(7,1) = 12
	Grid(8,1) = 18
	
	Grid(0,2) = "Sýra No"
	Grid(1,2) = "MALZEME TANIMI"
	Grid(2,2) = "AÇIKLAMA"
	Grid(3,2) = "MÝKTAR"
	Grid(4,2) = "BR"
	Grid(5,2) = "TERMÝN"
	Grid(6,2) = "FÝYAT"
	Grid(7,2) = ""
	Grid(8,2) = "TUTAR"	

	Grid(0,4) = "C"
	Grid(1,4) = "C"
	Grid(2,4) = "C"
	Grid(3,4) = "N"
	Grid(4,4) = "C"
	Grid(5,4) = "D"
	Grid(6,4) = "N"
	Grid(7,4) = "C"
	Grid(8,4) = "N"
	Grid(9,4) = "C"

	For i = 0 To UBound(Grid,1)
		If Grid(i,4)="N" Then
			Degisken = Grid(i,0)
			if i=4 or i=5 then
				ondalik=2
			else
			ondalik = STOK46T.MaxSigDig(Degisken)	
			end if
			Grid(i,6) = Ondalik		
		End If
	Next	
	Grid(0,6) = 0
	dTutar = STOK46E.MaxSigDig("OR_ISKONTOLUTOPLAM")
	dKDV = STOK46E.MaxSigDig("OR_KDVLERTOPLAM")
	dKDVDahil = STOK46E.MaxSigDig("OR_KDVDAHILTOPLAM")
	
End Sub
Sub Normal(pe1)
		res = pe1.setbold(0)
		res = pe1.setitalic(0)
		res = pe1.pensize(0.4)
		res = pe1.setbkgcolor(RGB(255, 255, 255))
		res = pe1.setrotation(0)
		res = pe1.settransparentmode(1)
		res = pe1.settextcolor(RGB(0, 0, 0))
		res = pe1.selectfont("Arial", 10)
		res = pe1.setalign("LEFT")
End Sub

Form Kaynak Kodu

Public sayfaTuru, sayfaBoyu, sayfaEni, basla, bitir, sonSatir, satirSonu, ToplamEn , BasilabilirAlan
Public UstMargin, SolMargin, SagMargin, AltMargin, satirYuksekligi
Public siraNo, satirBaslangic,Tip, satirAra
Public AlanSayisi(), ToplamTutar, ToplamProTutar
Public xCizgi()
Public Secilenler()
Public Grid()

Sub PrintForm()
	Dim satirSayisi,pe1
	sayfaBoyu = 297
	sayfaEni = 210

	ToplamTutar = 0
	ToplamProTutar = 0
	siraNo = 0
	satirBaslangic = 0
	UstMargin = 0
	AltMargin = SayfaBoyu - 20
	SolMargin = 10
	SagMargin = SayfaEni - 13
	satiryuksekligi = 7.6
	satirBaslangic = UstMargin + 84
'	satirSonu = satirBaslangic + 14 * SatirYuksekligi
	satirSonu = satirBaslangic + 11 * SatirYuksekligi
	
	Set pe1 = doc.CreatePrintEngineOutput()
	res = pe1.Open(0, "Satýnalma Sipariþi", SayfaBoyu, SayfaEni, "")

	Call Alanlar()
	If doc.ErrorNumber Then
		MsgBox ("Hata main : " & doc.ErrorNumber)
		Exit Sub
	End If
	siraNo = 0        
	satirSayisi = STOK46T.GetRecCount()
	Call Print_form_sayfabasi(pe1)
	For i = 1 To satirSayisi
			Call Print_form_satir(pe1, i)
	Next
		
	Call Print_form_sayfasonu(pe1)

	res = pe1.Show()
	res = Doc.ActivateAfterMacro(1)
End Sub

Sub Print_form_sayfabasi(pe1)
		res = pe1.nopen()
        
'*******************
		res = pe1.selectfont("arial", 12)
		res = pe1.setalign("CENTER")
		res = pe1.SetBold(1)
		res = pe1.Write(UstMargin + 35,SolMargin + 44,8,SagMargin - SolMargin - 87,"SATINALMA SÝPARÝÞ FORMU")
		res = pe1.SetBold(0)
		res = pe1.SetUnderlined(0)
		res = pe1.selectfont("Arial", 9)
		res = pe1.setalign("LEFT")
		res = pe1.Write(UstMargin+12, SolMargin + 94, 6,100,"SULTAN ORHAN MAH 1174/2 SOK. NO.3 GEBZE / KOCAELÝ")
		res = pe1.Write(UstMargin+17, SolMargin + 94, 6,50,"ULUÇINAR V.D.188 049 4518")
		res = pe1.Write(UstMargin+22, SolMargin + 94, 6,35,"Tel    :0262 646 66 12")
		res = pe1.Write(UstMargin+22, SolMargin + 130, 6,35,"Fax    :0262 646 66 92")
		res = pe1.Write(UstMargin+27, SolMargin + 94, 6,40,"E-mail :info@budora.com")
		res = pe1.pensize(0.6)
		SiparisNo = STOK46E.EVRAKNO(1)
		Tarih = doc.StrtoNordate(STOK46E.TARIH(1))
		CHKodu = STOK46E.CH_KODU(1)
		CHAdi = doc.GetKartValue("CARI00","AD",CHKodu,-1) 
		ilgili = doc.GetKartValue("CARI00","KONTAKTNAME_1",CHKodu,-1) 
		Adres1 = doc.GetKartValue("CARI00","ADRES_1",CHKodu,-1) 
		Adres2 = doc.GetKartValue("CARI00","ADRES_2",CHKodu,-1) 
		Adres3 = doc.GetKartValue("CARI00","ADRES_3",CHKodu,-1) 
		Tel = doc.GetKartValue("CARI00","TELEFONNO_1",CHKodu,-1) 
		Faks = doc.GetKartValue("CARI00","FAXNO",CHKodu,-1) 		
				
		res = pe1.SetBold(0)
		res = pe1.write(ustmargin+45,solmargin+5,6,20,"FÝRMA ADI")
		res = pe1.write(ustmargin+45,solmargin+22,6,60,": "	& CHAdi)
		res = pe1.write(ustmargin+50,solmargin+5,6,20,"ADRESÝ")
		res = pe1.write(ustmargin+50,solmargin+22,6,60,": " & trim(Adres1))
		res = pe1.write(ustmargin+55,solmargin+22,6,60,trim(Adres2))
		res = pe1.write(ustmargin+60,solmargin+22,6,60,trim(Adres3))

		res = pe1.write(ustmargin+45,solmargin+95,6,20,"SÝPARÝÞ NO.")
		res = pe1.write(ustmargin+50,solmargin+95,6,25,"SÝPARÝÞ TARÝHÝ")
		res = pe1.write(ustmargin+55,solmargin+95,6,20,"ÝLGÝLÝ")
		res = pe1.write(ustmargin+60,solmargin+95,6,25,"TELEFON NO.")
		res = pe1.write(ustmargin+65,solmargin+95,6,20,"FAX NO.")

		res = pe1.write(ustmargin+45,solmargin+120,6,20,": " & trim(SiparisNo))
		res = pe1.write(ustmargin+50,solmargin+120,6,20,": " & Doc.StrToNorDate(STOK46E.TARIH(1)))
		res = pe1.write(ustmargin+55,solmargin+120,6,60,": " & trim(ilgili))
		res = pe1.write(ustmargin+60,solmargin+120,6,25,": " & Tel)
		res = pe1.write(ustmargin+65,solmargin+120,6,25,": " & Faks)

'***********************		
 		res = pe1.SetAlign("LEFT")
		Sol = SolMargin + 4
		res = pe1.SetBold(0)
		ReDim Preserve xCizgi(0)

		For i = 0 To UBound(Grid,1)
			SutunGenisligi = Grid(i,1)
			Baslik = Grid(i,2)
			res = pe1.write(satirBaslangic - 10, Sol, 10, SutunGenisligi - 4 , Baslik)
			Sol = Sol + SutunGenisligi
			xCizgi(UBound(xCizgi)) = Sol - 2
				ReDim Preserve xCizgi(Ubound(xCizgi)+1)
		Next
		res = pe1.SetBold(0)
		res = pe1.pensize(0.6)
		Call Normal(pe1)
End Sub

Sub Print_form_satir(pe1, satirNo)
Dim satirpozr
		If trim(STOK46T.KOD(satirNo)) = "" Then Exit Sub
		Degisken = ""
		sonSatir = pe1.GetBottom()
		If sonSatir >= satirSonu Then
			Call Print_form_sayfasonu(pe1)
			siraNo = 1
			res = pe1.PageBreak()
			Call Print_form_sayfabasi(pe1)
			satirpozr = satirBaslangic - 1
		Else
			If satirNo = 1 Then
					satirpozr = satirBaslangic - 1
			Else
					satirpozr = pe1.GetTop() + satiryuksekligi
			End If
			siraNo = siraNo + 1
		End If		
		res = pe1.selectfont("arial", 9)
		res = pe1.setbold(0)
		Sol = SolMargin + 2
		res = pe1.setalign("LEFT")
			For i=0 To UBound(Grid,1)
				SutunGenisligi = Grid(i,1) 
				DegiskenAdi = Grid(i,0)
				res = pe1.selectfont("arial", 9)
				If left(DegiskenAdi,1)<> "_" Then
					Degisken = trim(cstr(STOK46T.GetFieldValue(DegiskenAdi,SatirNo)))
				Else
					If DegiskenAdi = "_POS" Then
						Degisken = satirNo
					ElseIf DegiskenAdi = "_AD" Then
						res = pe1.selectfont("arial", 8)
						ad=STOK46T.NONSTOK_AD(satirNo)
						Degisken = trim(ad)
					ElseIf DegiskenAdi = "_MUSTERI" Then
						res = pe1.selectfont("arial", 8)
						notlar=STOK46T.NOTE0(satirNo)						
						Degisken = trim(notlar)
	
					Else
						res = pe1.selectfont("arial", 9)
						Degisken = ""
					End If
				End If	
				res = pe1.SetAlign("LEFT")
				'If Grid(i,4) = "N" And left(DegiskenAdi,1)<> "_" Then
				'	res = pe1.SetAlign("RIGHT")
				'	Ondalik = Grid(i,6)
				'	Degisken = Doc.NumFormat(Degisken, 18,2)
				If trim(DegiskenAdi) = "OR_FIYAT" or trim(DegiskenAdi) = "OR_TUTAR" then
					res = pe1.SetAlign("RIGHT")
					Ondalik = Grid(i,6)
					Degisken = Doc.NumFormat(Degisken, 18,2)
				ElseIf trim(DegiskenAdi) = "SF_MIKTAR" then
					res = pe1.SetAlign("RIGHT")
					Ondalik = Grid(i,6)
					Degisken = Doc.NumFormat(Degisken, 18,2)
				ElseIf Grid(i,4) = "D" And left(DegiskenAdi,1)<> "_" Then
					Degisken = Doc.StrtoNordate(Degisken)
				ElseIf DegiskenAdi = "_POS" Then
					res = pe1.SetAlign("CENTER")
				End If
				res = pe1.write(satirpozr, Sol, satiryuksekligi, SutunGenisligi - 4, Degisken)
				Sol = Sol + SutunGenisligi
				
			Next					 
		sonSatir = pe1.GetBottom()
End Sub

	Notlar1 = STOK46E.SIPLEILGILINOTLAR_1(1)
	Notlar2 = STOK46E.SIPLEILGILINOTLAR_2(1)
	
End Sub

Sub Alanlar()
	ReDim Grid(9,7)
	Grid(0,0) = "_POS"
	Grid(1,0) = "_AD"
	Grid(2,0) = "_MUSTERI"
	Grid(3,0) = "SF_MIKTAR"
	Grid(4,0) = "SF_SF_UNIT"
	Grid(5,0) = "REV_RTESTARIH"
	Grid(6,0) = "OR_FIYAT"
	Grid(7,0) = "PRICEUNIT"
	Grid(8,0) = "OR_TUTAR"		

	Grid(0,1) = 10
	Grid(1,1) = 51
	Grid(2,1) = 31
	Grid(3,1) = 18
	Grid(4,1) = 10
	Grid(5,1) = 20
	Grid(6,1) = 21
	Grid(7,1) = 9
	Grid(8,1) = 21
	
	Grid(0,2) = "Sýra No"
	Grid(1,2) = "MALZEME TANIMI"
	Grid(2,2) = "AÇIKLAMA"
	Grid(3,2) = "MÝKTAR"
	Grid(4,2) = "BR"
	Grid(5,2) = "TERMÝN"
	Grid(6,2) = "FÝYAT"
	Grid(7,2) = ""
	Grid(8,2) = "TUTAR"	

	Grid(0,4) = "C"
	Grid(1,4) = "C"
	Grid(2,4) = "C"
	Grid(3,4) = "N"
	Grid(4,4) = "C"
	Grid(5,4) = "D"
	Grid(6,4) = "N"
	Grid(7,4) = "C"
	Grid(8,4) = "N"
	Grid(9,4) = "C"

	For i = 0 To UBound(Grid,1)
		If Grid(i,4)="N" Then
			Degisken = Grid(i,0)
			if i=4 or i=5 then
				ondalik=2
			else
			ondalik = STOK46T.MaxSigDig(Degisken)	
			end if
			Grid(i,6) = Ondalik		
		End If
	Next	
	Grid(0,6) = 0
	dTutar = STOK46E.MaxSigDig("OR_ISKONTOLUTOPLAM")
	dKDV = STOK46E.MaxSigDig("OR_KDVLERTOPLAM")
	dKDVDahil = STOK46E.MaxSigDig("OR_KDVDAHILTOPLAM")
	
End Sub
Sub Normal(pe1)
		res = pe1.setbold(0)
		res = pe1.setitalic(0)
		res = pe1.pensize(0.4)
		res = pe1.setbkgcolor(RGB(255, 255, 255))
		res = pe1.setrotation(0)
		res = pe1.settransparentmode(1)
		res = pe1.settextcolor(RGB(0, 0, 0))
		res = pe1.selectfont("Arial", 10)
		res = pe1.setalign("LEFT")
End Sub

Form Kaynak Kodu
Firma Kodu : BDORA_2014 / Uygulama : PFRM7S

Ylave Hangi Uygulamalar için : 
Açyklama : Satýnalma Talebi
Kod : 
Public sayfaTuru, sayfaBoyu, sayfaEni, basla, bitir, sonSatir, satirSonu, ToplamEn , BasilabilirAlan
Public siraNo, satirBaslangic,UstMargin, AltMargin, sagMargin, solMargin, satirYuksekligi
Public AlanSayisi()	
Public xCizgi()
Public Grid
Sub PrintForm()
	ToplamEn = 0
	BasilabilirAlan = 0
	siraNo = 0

	sayfaBoyu = 210
	sayfaEni = 297
	UstMargin = 28
	AltMargin = sayfaBoyu - 15
	solMargin = 10
	sagMargin = sayfaEni - 10
	satiryuksekligi = 9
	SiraNo = 0
	Set pe1 = doc.CreatePrintEngineOutput()

	Call Alanlar()
	res = pe1.Open(0, "Satýnalma Talebi", SayfaEni ,SayfaBoyu,"LANDSCAPE")

	BasilabilirAlan = SayfaEni - 20
	satirSonu = sayfaBoyu - 50


	If doc.ErrorNumber Then
		MsgBox ("Hata main : " & doc.ErrorNumber)
		Exit Sub
	End If
	Dim satirSayisi_tableT
	satirSayisi_tableT = STOK47T.GetRecCount()
	Call Print_form_sayfabasi(pe1)
	For I = 1 To satirSayisi_tableT
		Call Print_form_satir(pe1, I)
	Next
		
	Call Print_form_sayfasonu(pe1)

	res = pe1.Show()
	res = Doc.ActivateAfterMacro(1)
End Sub

Sub Print_form_sayfabasi(pe1)
	ReDim xCizgi(0)

	res = pe1.StoreSet(1001)
	res = pe1.nopen()
	res = pe1.settransparentmode(0)
	res = pe1.box(8, 8, 30, SayfaEni-110)
	res = pe1.UseSet(1001)
	res = pe1.RemoveSet(1001)
        
	res = pe1.selectfont("Arial", 10)
	res = pe1.nopen()
	logo = Doc.Getd7Directory_DataFirm() & trim(Doc.Customp_Gets("!LOGO"))
	res = pe1.selectfont("Arial", 14)
	res = pe1.setbold(1)
	res = pe1.setalign("CENTER")
	res = pe1.write(UstMargin + 3, 10, 15, SayfaEni - 10, "SATINALMA TALEP FORMU")
	res = pe1.setalign("LEFT")
	If logo <> "" Then
		res = pe1.Picture(12, 10, 15, 55, logo, 18)
	End If
	res = pe1.selectfont("Helvetica", "12")
	res = pe1.setbold(1)
	res = pe1.setalign("CENTER")
	res = pe1.selectfont("Arial", "10")

	res = pe1.setalign("LEFT")
'*******************
	BBasla = UstMargin + 15
	SatirAra = 6
	Genislik10 = 40
	Genislik11 = 120
	Genislik20 = 25
	Genislik21 = 80	


	FisNo = STOK47E.EVRAKNO(1)
	Tarih = doc.StrtoNordate(STOK47E.TARIH(1))
	TalepEdenBolum = STOK47E.CODEDBOLUM(1)
	TalepEdenBolumAdi = doc.GetGecoValue("DEPT","AD",TalepEdenBolum ,-1)
	TalepEdenKisi = STOK47E.CODEDKISI(1)  
	TalepEden = Doc.GetDBFieldValue("PERS00","AD",TalepEdenKisi,"")

	res = pe1.SetBold(1)
	res = pe1.write(BBasla , SolMargin+2, 6, Genislik10, "TALEP EDEN BÖLÜM")
	res = pe1.SetBold(0)
	res = pe1.write(pe1.getTop , pe1.getRight + 1, 6, Genislik11, ": " & Trim(TalepEdenBolumAdi))
	res = pe1.SetBold(1)
	res = pe1.write(pe1.getTop , pe1.getRight + 1, 6, Genislik20, "TARÝH")
	res = pe1.SetBold(0)
	res = pe1.write(pe1.getTop , pe1.getRight + 1, 6, Genislik21, ": " & Tarih)
	res = pe1.SetBold(1)
	res = pe1.write(pe1.getTop + satirAra, SolMargin+2, 6, Genislik10, "TALEP EDEN KÝÞÝ")
	res = pe1.SetBold(0)
	res = pe1.write(pe1.getTop , pe1.getRight + 1, 6, Genislik11, ": " & Trim(TalepEden))		
	res = pe1.SetBold(1)
	res = pe1.write(pe1.getTop , pe1.getRight + 1, 6, Genislik20, "TALEP NO")
	res = pe1.SetBold(0)
	res = pe1.write(pe1.getTop , pe1.getRight + 1, 6, Genislik21, ": " & trim(FisNo))				
	res = pe1.SetBold(1)

'***********************		
	satirBaslangic = pe1.GetBottom + 20

	res = pe1.setalign("CENTER")
	res = pe1.selectfont("Arial", 10)
	Sol = solmargin + 2
	For i = 0 To UBound(Grid,1)
		SutunGenisligi = Grid(i,1)
		Baslik = Grid(i,2)
		If SutunGenisligi > 0 Then	
			if i = 2 then
				SutunGenisligi = Grid(i,1) + Grid(i+1,1)
			end if
			res = pe1.write(satirBaslangic - 15, Sol, 10, SutunGenisligi - 4 , Baslik)
			SutunGenisligi = Grid(i,1)
			Sol = Sol + SutunGenisligi
			xCizgi(uBound(xCizgi)) =	Sol - 2
			redim Preserve xCizgi(uBound(xCizgi)+1)
		End If
	Next

	res = pe1.pensize(0.6)
'yatay çizgiler
	res = pe1.pensize(0.4)
	res = pe1.Line(28, 10, 0, SayfaEni-20)
	res = pe1.Line(satirBaslangic - 17, 10, 0, sayfaEni -20)
	res = pe1.Line(satirBaslangic - 4, 10, 0, sayfaEni -20)
	res = pe1.StoreSet(1001)
	res = pe1.PenColor(RGB(0, 0, 0))        
	res = pe1.pensize(0.5)

	Call Normal(pe1)
End Sub

Sub Print_form_satir(pe1, satirNo)
Dim satirpozr
	if trim(STOK47T.KOD(satirNo)) = "" then exit sub
	SiraNo = SiraNo + 1
	sonSatir = pe1.GetBottom()
	If sonSatir >= satirSonu Then
		Call Print_form_sayfasonu(pe1)
		res = pe1.PageBreak()
		Call Print_form_sayfabasi(pe1)
		satirpozr = satirBaslangic
	Else
		If satirNo = 1 Then
			satirpozr = satirBaslangic
		Else
			satirpozr = pe1.GetTop() + satiryuksekligi
		End If
	End If
		
	res = pe1.StoreSet(1001)
	res = pe1.selectfont("Arial", 10)
	res = pe1.setbold(0)
	Sol = solMargin + 2
	res = pe1.setalign("LEFT")
	For i=0 To UBound(Grid,1)
		SutunGenisligi = Grid(i,1)
		DegiskenAdi = Grid(i,0)
		res = pe1.SetBold(0)
		if DegiskenAdi = "_CINS" then
			StokKodu = STOK47T.KOD(satirNo)
			Stokadi = Doc.GetDBFieldValue("STOK00","AD",StokKodu,"")
			if trim(StokKodu) =  "ZZ-GÝDER" then 	Stokadi = STOK47T.NONSTOK_AD(satirNo)
'			Degisken = trim(StokKodu) & "  -  " & trim(StokAdi)
			Degisken = trim(StokAdi)
		Elseif DegiskenAdi = "_SN" then
			res = pe1.SetBold(1)
			Degisken = SiraNo & "."
		else
			Degisken = trim(cstr(STOK47T.GetFieldValue(DegiskenAdi,SatirNo)))
		end if
		res = pe1.SetAlign("LEFT")
		If Grid(i,4) = "N" Then
			res = pe1.SetAlign("RIGHT")
			Ondalik = Grid(i,6)
			Degisken = Doc.NumFormat(Degisken, 20,Ondalik)
		ElseIf Grid(i,4) = "D" Then
			Degisken = Doc.StrtoNordate(Degisken)
		End If
		If SutunGenisligi > 0 Then
			res = pe1.write(satirpozr, Sol, satiryuksekligi, SutunGenisligi - 4, Degisken)
			Sol = Sol + SutunGenisligi
		End If	
		Next					 
	sonSatir = pe1.GetBottom()
End Sub
Sub Print_form_sayfasonu(pe1)
	res = pe1.pensize(0.5)
'dýþ çerçeve
	res = pe1.box(UstMargin, 10, AltMargin - UstMargin, SayfaEni - 20)

'dikey çizgiler
	res = pe1.pensize(0.3)
	For i = 0 To UBound(xCizgi)- 1
	  LineX = cint(xCizgi(i))
		If LineX = 0 Then Exit For
		if i <> 0 and i <> 2 then
		  res = pe1.Line(satirBaslangic - 17, LineX, satirSonu - satirBaslangic + 21, 0)
		end if
	Next
	res = pe1.Line(pe1.GetBottom,solMargin,0,sagMargin-solMargin)
	res = pe1.Line(UstMargin + 11,solMargin,0,sagMargin-solMargin)
	res = pe1.SetBold(1)
	For i = 1 to 9
		res = pe1.Line(satirBaslangic + i * satirYuksekligi - 2,solMargin,0,sagMargin-solMargin)		
	Next
	res = pe1.selectfont("Arial", 12)
	res = pe1.Write(satirSonu + 10,solMargin + 1,6,25,"NOTLAR :")
	res = pe1.Write(pe1.GetTop + 13,solMargin + 1,6,25,"ÝMZA         :")
	res = pe1.selectfont("Arial", 8)
	res = pe1.Write(AltMargin + 1,sagMargin - 20,6,20,"FR SA 001 / 2")
End Sub

Sub Alanlar()
	ReDim Grid(5,7)
	Grid(0,0) = "_SN"
	Grid(1,0) = "_CINS"
	Grid(2,0) = "SF_MIKTAR"
	Grid(3,0) = "SF_SF_UNIT"
	Grid(4,0) = "RTALEPEDILENTESTARIH"
	Grid(5,0) = "_FIYAT"


	Grid(0,1) = 10
	Grid(1,1) = 130
	Grid(2,1) = 23
	Grid(3,1) = 14
	Grid(4,1) = 23
	Grid(5,1) = 77

	Grid(0,2) = ""
	Grid(1,2) = "MALZEME CÝNSÝ"
	Grid(2,2) = "MÝKTAR"
	Grid(3,2) = ""
	Grid(4,2) = "TESLÝM TARÝHÝ"
	Grid(5,2) = "BÝRÝM FÝYAT"

	
	Grid(0,4) = "C"
	Grid(1,4) = "C"
	Grid(2,4) = "N"
	Grid(3,4) = "C"
	Grid(4,4) = "D"
	Grid(5,4) = "C"

	For i = 0 To UBound(Grid,1) 
		Degisken = Grid(i,0)
		If Grid(i,4)="N" And Left(degisken,1) <> "_" Then
			Degisken = Grid(i,0)
			ondalik = STOK47T.MaxSigDig(Degisken)	
			If Ondalik > 2 Then Ondalik = 2
			Grid(i,6) = Ondalik		
		End If
	Next	
End Sub
Sub Normal(pe1)
		res = pe1.setbold(0)
		res = pe1.setitalic(0)
		res = pe1.pensize(0.4)
		res = pe1.setbkgcolor(RGB(255, 255, 255))
		res = pe1.setrotation(0)
		res = pe1.settransparentmode(1)
		res = pe1.settextcolor(RGB(0, 0, 0))
		res = pe1.selectfont("Arial", 10)
		res = pe1.setalign("left")
End Sub                                                                                                                                                                                     


----------------------------------------------------------------

Public ustmargin, solmargin, satirbaslangic, satirYuksekligi, satirpozr, sirano, S70T, ST70, TopTutar
Dim sayfaSatirsayisi, satirsolmargin, AraToplam, Grid, satirSonu, charCpi, sonSayfasatirSonu
Public solTutar, GenTutar, solfiyat, genfiyat,satirYuksekligi2, solTutar1, genTutar1, solPB, GenPB
Sub PrintForm()
		
	STOK70T.SafePack
	if STOK70T.GetRowCount = 0 Then Exit Sub
	charCpi = 17
	Set pe1 = Doc.CreatePrintEngineOutput()
	'res = pe1.SetConsolePrintOpt(1)
	'res = pe1.ConsoleOutput_SetLinesPerPage(66)
	'res = pe1.ConsoleOutput_SetCharsPerInch(charCpi)
	res = pe1.Open(0, "Fatura", 295, 215, "")

	satirYuksekligi = 4.3
	satirYuksekligi2 = 21.5
	DipToplamSatirSayisi = DipToplamSatirSayisiHesapla()	

	TopTutar = 0
	Ustmargin = 0
	satirSonu = 180
	sonSayfasatirSonu = satirSonu - DipToplamSatirSayisi * satirYuksekligi
	satirBaslangic = UstMargin + 102
	SOLTUTAR=0
	GENTUTAR=0
	solmargin = 0
	satirpozr = 0
	satirsolmargin = 7
	AraToplam = 0
	Doc.CreateTableObject "XXX=STOK70T", "TMPCOPY"
	Set S70T = Doc.GetTableObject("XXX")
	Set S70 = Doc.GetTableObject("STOK70T")
	S70T.CopyTable(S70)
	S70T.SafePack
'	S70T.GroupBy("KOD")

	sayfaSatirsayisi = 5

	Doc.CreateTableObject "YYY=STOK70T", "TMPCOPY"
	Set ST70 = Doc.GetTableObject("YYY")
	Set SS70 = Doc.GetTableObject("STOK70T")
	ST70.CopyTable(SS70)
	ST70.GroupBy("IRSALIYENO")

	satirSayisi = S70T.GetRecCount()
	sirano = 0
	
	Call Alanlar()
	Call Print_form_sayfabasi(pe1)
	For i = 1 To satirSayisi
		Call Print_form_satir(pe1, i)
	Next
	Call EnSonSayfa(pe1)
	
	res = pe1.Show()
	res = Doc.ActivateAfterMacro(1)
End Sub

Sub Print_form_sayfabasi(pe1)
	res = pe1.SetAlign("LEFT")
	res = pe1.Selectfont("Courier New", 7)
	CHKodu = STOK70E.CH_KODU
	CHAdi = Doc.GetDBFieldValue("CARI00", "AD", CHKodu, "")
	CHAdi2 = Doc.GetDBFieldValue("CARI00", "NAME2", CHKodu, "")
	CHAdres1 = Doc.GetDBFieldValue("CARI00", "ADRES_1", CHKodu, "")
	CHAdres2 = Doc.GetDBFieldValue("CARI00", "ADRES_2", CHKodu, "")
	CHAdres3 = Doc.GetDBFieldValue("CARI00", "ADRES_3", CHKodu, "")
	VergiD = Doc.GetDBFieldValue("CARI00", "VERGIDAIRESI", CHKodu, "")
	VergiNo = Doc.GetDBFieldValue("CARI00", "VERGINO", CHKodu, "")
		
	faturaTarihi = Doc.StrToNorDate(STOK70E.TARIH)
	sevkTarihi = Doc.StrToNorDate(STOK70E.S60_RSEVKTARIH)
	irsaliye=STOK70E.S60_EVRAKNO
	IrsNolar = IrsStr(ST70)
	IrsTarihleri = IrsTarih(ST70)

	'res = pe1.write(UstMargin+satiryuksekligi*17 , solmargin+15, 5, 95, trim(CHKODU))
	res = pe1.write(UstMargin+58, solmargin+25, 5, 95, trim(CHAdi))
	res = pe1.write(pe1.GetTop + satirYuksekligi, solmargin+10, 5, 95, CHAdres1)
	res = pe1.write(pe1.GetTop + satirYuksekligi, pe1.GetLeft, 5, 95, CHAdres2)
	res = pe1.write(pe1.GetTop + satirYuksekligi, pe1.GetLeft, 5, 95, CHAdres3)

	res = pe1.write(UstMargin + 82, SolMargin + 30, 6, 50, trim(VergiD))
	res = pe1.write(UstMargin + 82, SolMargin + 70, 6, 65, trim(VergiNo))

	res = pe1.write(UstMargin + 62, SolMargin + 150, 4, 20,trim(STOK70E.EVRAKNO))
	res = pe1.write(pe1.GetTop + satirYuksekligi+3, SolMargin + 152, 4, 20,trim(faturaTarihi))
	res = pe1.write(pe1.GetTop + satirYuksekligi+3, SolMargin + 152, 4, 15,trim(sevkTarihi))
	res = pe1.write(pe1.GetTop + satirYuksekligi+3, SolMargin + 152, 4, 15,trim(irsaliye))
	
	if pe1.GetPageCount() > 1 Then
		res = pe1.SetAlign("LEFT")
		res = pe1.Write(satirBaslangic - 2 * satirYuksekligi,solFiyat-5,5,genFiyat+10,"SUB TOTAL PAGE UP")
		res = pe1.SetAlign("RIGHT")
		res = pe1.Write(pe1.GetTop,solTutar1,5,genTutar1 - 2,DOC.NUMFORMAT(TopTutar,20,3))	
		res = pe1.SetAlign("LEFT")
		res = pe1.Write(pe1.GetTop,solPB,5,genPB, STOK70T.PRICEUNIT(1))
	End if

End Sub

Sub Print_form_satir(pe1, satirNo)
	ToplamTutar = ToplamTutar + S70T.OR_TUTAR(satirNo)
	Dim satirpozr
		If trim(S70T.KOD(satirNo)) = "" Then Exit Sub
		Degisken = ""
		sonSatir = pe1.GetBottom()
		If sonSatir >=  satirSonu Then
			Call AraSayfaSonu(pe1)
			siraNo = 1
			res = pe1.PageBreak()
			Call Print_form_sayfabasi(pe1)
			satirpozr = satirBaslangic
		Else
			If satirNo = 1 Then
				satirpozr = satirBaslangic
			Else
				satirpozr = SatirBaslangic + siraNo * satiryuksekligi2
			End If
			siraNo = siraNo + 1
		End If		
		res = pe1.setbold(0)
		TopTutar =TopTutar +S70T.OR_TUTAR(satirno)
		Sol = SolMargin + 1
		res = pe1.setalign("LEFT")
		For i = 0 To UBound(Grid, 1)
			SutunGenisligi = Grid(i, 1) 
			DegiskenAdi = Grid(i, 0)		

	
			if degiskenAdi = "D_ID" then
					'sipno=S70T.SERINO(satirNo)	
				sipNo1 = S70T.SIPNO(satirno)

				artno = S70T.S60_SIPARTNO(satirno)
				sipno = Doc.Getdbfieldvalue("STOK40E","CHSIPNO",sipNo1,"")
				artno = Doc.Getdbfieldvalue("STOK40T","CHSIPANO",artno,"ARTNO")
				not1 = Doc.GetUDFValue("STOK70T",STOK70T.ARTNO(satirno),"NOT1",0)
				not2 = Doc.GetUDFValue("STOK70T",STOK70T.ARTNO(satirno),"NOT2",0)
				not3 = Doc.GetUDFValue("STOK70T",STOK70T.ARTNO(satirno),"NOT3",0)
				not4 = Doc.GetUDFValue("STOK70T",STOK70T.ARTNO(satirno),"NOT4",0)
	
				res = pe1.write(satirpozr, Sol+115, satiryuksekligi2, 10,"PCE")
				res = pe1.write(satirpozr+4.3, Sol+25, satiryuksekligi2, 80,"P.O. " & trim(sipno) & " " & trim(artno))				
				
				res = pe1.write(pe1.gettop+satiryuksekligi, Sol+25, satiryuksekligi2, 80,"Country Of Origin : TR ")
				res = pe1.write(pe1.gettop+satiryuksekligi, Sol+25, satiryuksekligi2, 120,Trim(not1))
				res = pe1.write(pe1.gettop+satiryuksekligi, Sol+25, satiryuksekligi2, 120,Trim(not2))
		
			
			
	
			end if
				Degisken = S70T.GetFieldValue(DegiskenAdi, SatirNo)
			if DegiskenAdi = "OR_TUTAR" Then
				solTutar1 = Sol
				genTutar1 = SutunGenisligi
			Elseif DegiskenAdi = "PRICEUNIT" Then
				solPB = sol
				GenPB = sutunGenisligi
			End if
				res = pe1.SetAlign("LEFT")
				If Grid(i, 4) = "N" Then
					res = pe1.SetAlign("RIGHT")
					Ondalik = Grid(i, 6)
					Degisken = DOC.NUMFORMAT(Degisken, 20, 2)
				ElseIf Grid(i, 4) = "D" Then
					Degisken = Doc.StrtoNordate(Degisken)
				End If		
				
				res = pe1.write(satirpozr, Sol, satiryuksekligi2, SutunGenisligi - 2, Degisken)
				Sol = Sol + SutunGenisligi	
	Next	
	sonSatir = pe1.GetBottom()

End Sub
Sub AraSayfaSonu(pe1)
	sol1 = SolMargin + 1
	
	res = pe1.SetAlign("LEFT")
	'res = pe1.Write(pe1.GetTop + satirYuksekligi,solFiyat,5,genFiyat,"ARA TOPLAM")
	res = pe1.SetAlign("RIGHT")
	'res = pe1.Write(pe1.GetTop,solTutar,5,genTutar,DOC.NUMFORMAT_NLZ(AraToplam,20,2))	
End Sub
Sub EnSonSayfa(pe1)	
	sol1 = SolMargin + 1
	
		

	Toplam = STOK70E.TL_NEWTOPLAM
	IskontoTutari = STOK70E.TL_ISKONTOLARTOPLAMI
	IskontoluTutar = STOK70E.TL_ISKONTOLUTOPLAM
	OTVTutari = STOK70E.TL_VERART1TOPLAMI
	OTVDahilTutar = STOK70E.TL_IVD_KH_TOPLAM
	OTVOrani = Doc.Round(Doc.Div(OTVTutari,IskontoluTutar) * 100,2)
	KDVTutari =	STOK70E.TL_KDVLERTOPLAM
	KDVOrani = Doc.Round(Doc.Div(KDVTutari,OTVDahilTutar) * 100,2)
	KDVDahilTutar = STOK70E.TL_KDVDAHILTOPLAM
	OdemeSekli = STOK70E.ODEMEKOSULLARI_1


	Iskonto1 = STOK70E.TL_ISKONTO1TOPLAMI
	Iskonto2 = STOK70E.TL_ISKONTO2TOPLAMI
	Iskonto3 = STOK70E.TL_ISKONTO3TOPLAMI
	Iskonto4 = STOK70E.TL_ISKONTO4TOPLAMI
	IskontoOrani1 = Doc.Round(Doc.Div(Iskonto1,Toplam) * 100,2)
	IskontoOrani2 = Doc.Round(Doc.Div(Iskonto2,Toplam) * 100,2)
	IskontoOrani3 = Doc.Round(Doc.Div(Iskonto3,Toplam) * 100,2)
	IskontoOrani4 = Doc.Round(Doc.Div(Iskonto4,Toplam) * 100,2)
	IskontoOndalik1 = 1
	IskontoOndalik2 = 1
	IskontoOndalik3 = 1
	IskontoOndalik4 = 1
	Iskonto1 = DOC.NUMFORMAT_NLZ(Iskonto1,6,IskontoOndalik1)
	Iskonto2 = DOC.NUMFORMAT_NLZ(Iskonto2,6,IskontoOndalik2)
	Iskonto3 = DOC.NUMFORMAT_NLZ(Iskonto3,6,IskontoOndalik3)
	Iskonto4 = DOC.NUMFORMAT_NLZ(Iskonto4,6,IskontoOndalik4)


'***********

	Toplam = DOC.NUMFORMAT(Toplam,20,3)	
	KDVTutari = DOC.NUMFORMAT(KDVTutari,20,3)
	IskontoluTutar = DOC.NUMFORMAT_NLZ(IskontoluTutar,20,3)
	IskontoTutari = DOC.NUMFORMAT_NLZ(IskontoTutari,20,3)
	OTVTutari = DOC.NUMFORMAT_NLZ(OTVTutari,20,3)
	OTVDahilTutar = DOC.NUMFORMAT_NLZ(OTVDahilTutar,20,3)
	KDVDahilTutar = DOC.NUMFORMAT(KDVDahilTutar,20,3)
	kdvOndalik = 1
	KDVOrani = DOC.NUMFORMAT_NLZ(KDVOrani,6,kdvOndalik)
	OTVOndalik = Doc.SigDig(OTVOrani)
	OTVOrani = DOC.NUMFORMAT_NLZ(OTVOrani,6,OTVOndalik)
	Iskonto1 = DOC.NUMFORMAT_NLZ(Iskonto1,20,3)
	Iskonto2 = DOC.NUMFORMAT_NLZ(Iskonto2,20,3)
	Iskonto3 = DOC.NUMFORMAT_NLZ(Iskonto3,20,3)
	Iskonto4 = DOC.NUMFORMAT_NLZ(Iskonto4,20,3)

	res = pe1.Setalign("RIGHT")

	Yalniz = Doc.NumToText3(KDVDahilTutar,2,"B")
	'call yazi(yalniz)
	
	res = pe1.Setalign("RIGHT")
	res = pe1.write(ustmargin+224, solmargin+145, 5, genTutar, Toplam)
	res=pe1.Setalign("LEFT")
	res=pe1.write(ustmargin+229, solmargin+135, 5, genFiyat, trim(KDVOrani))
	res=pe1.Setalign("RIGHT")
	res=pe1.write(ustmargin+229 , solmargin+145, 5, genTutar, KDVTutari)
	res=pe1.write(ustmargin+240, solmargin+145, 5, genTutar, KDVDahilTutar)
	res=pe1.Setalign("LEFT")



	res = pe1.write(ustmargin+170, solmargin+10, satirYuksekligi, 115, STOK70E.ODEMEKOSULLARI_1)
	res = pe1.write(pe1.gettop+satiryuksekligi, solmargin+10, satirYuksekligi, 115, STOK70E.ODEMEKOSULLARI_2)
	res = pe1.write(pe1.gettop+satiryuksekligi, solmargin+10, satirYuksekligi, 115, STOK70E.S60_NOTES_1)
	res = pe1.write(pe1.gettop+satiryuksekligi, solmargin+10, satirYuksekligi, 115, STOK70E.S60_NOTES_2)
	res = pe1.write(pe1.gettop+satiryuksekligi, solmargin+10, satirYuksekligi, 115, STOK70E.S60_NOTES_3)
	res = pe1.write(pe1.gettop+satiryuksekligi, solmargin+10, satirYuksekligi, 115, STOK70E.S60_NOTES_4)
	res = pe1.write(pe1.gettop+satiryuksekligi, solmargin+10, satirYuksekligi, 115, STOK70E.S60_BOX1_1)
	res = pe1.write(pe1.gettop+satiryuksekligi, solmargin+10, satirYuksekligi, 115, STOK70E.S60_BOX1_2)
	res = pe1.write(pe1.gettop+satiryuksekligi, solmargin+10, satirYuksekligi, 115, STOK70E.S60_BOX1_3)

	res=pe1.write(ustmargin+215, solmargin+20, satiryuksekligi, 90,"Say : " & yalniz & STOK70T.PRICEUNIT)
	

End Sub
Function IrsStr(ST70)
	For i = 1 to ST70.GetRecCount
		irsaliyeNo = ST70.IRSALIYENO(i)
		if trim(irsaliyeNo) <> "" then
			Istr = Istr & trim(irsaliyeNo) & ", "		
		end if
	Next
	if trim(Istr) <> "" then
		Istr = left(Istr, len(Istr)-2)
	end if
	IrsStr = Istr
End Function
Function IrsTarih(ST70)
	For i = 1 to ST70.GetRecCount
		irsaliyeNo = ST70.IRSALIYENO(i)
		if trim(irsaliyeNo) <> "" then
			Tarih = Doc.GetDBFieldValue("STOK60E", "TARIH", irsaliyeNo, "")
			Tarih = Doc.StrToNorDate(Tarih)
			Itarih = Itarih & trim(Tarih) & ", "		
		end if
	Next
	if trim(Itarih) <> "" then
		Itarih = left(Itarih, len(Itarih)-2)
	end if
	Irstarih = Itarih
End Function
Sub Alanlar()
	ReDim Grid(8, 7)

	Grid(0, 0) = "KOD"
	Grid(1, 0) = "KOD_STOK00_AD"
	Grid(2, 0) = "SF_MIKTAR"
	Grid(3, 0) = "SF_SF_UNIT"
	Grid(4, 0) = "TL_FIYAT"
	'Grid(5, 0) = "PRICEUNIT"
	Grid(6, 0) = "TL_TUTAR"
	'Grid(7, 0) = "PRICEUNIT"

	Grid(0, 1) = 25
	Grid(1, 1) = 65
	Grid(2, 1) = 25
	Grid(3, 1) = 17
	Grid(4, 1) = 22
	'Grid(5, 1) = 10
	Grid(6, 1) = 28
	'Grid(7, 1) = 10

	Grid(0, 4) = "C"
	Grid(1, 4) = "C"
	Grid(2, 4) = "N"
	Grid(3, 4) = "C"
	Grid(4, 4) = "N"
	'Grid(5, 4) = "C"
	Grid(6, 4) = "N"
	'Grid(7, 4) = "C"
		soltutar=solmargin+1
		solfiyat=solmargin+1
		for i=0 to 4
			soltutar=soltutar+grid(i,1)
		next
		
		for i=0 to 3
			solfiyat=solfiyat+grid(i,1)
		next

			gentutar=grid(5,1)-2
			genfiyat=grid(4,1)	
	
	For i = 0 To UBound(Grid, 1) 
		Degisken = Grid(i, 0)
		If Grid(i, 4) = "N" Then
			ondalik = ST70.MaxSigDig(Degisken)	
			Grid(i, 6) = Ondalik		
		End If
	Next	
End Sub
Function StringSayisi(Genislik_mm)
	StringSayisi = Doc.Floor(Genislik_mm * charCpi / 25.4)-8
End Function
Function DipToplamSatirSayisiHesapla()

	satirSayisi = 7

	Iskonto1 = STOK70E.TL_ISKONTO1TOPLAMI
	Iskonto2 = STOK70E.TL_ISKONTO2TOPLAMI
	Iskonto3 = STOK70E.TL_ISKONTO3TOPLAMI
	Iskonto4 = STOK70E.TL_ISKONTO4TOPLAMI

	IskontoTutari = STOK70E.TL_ISKONTOLARTOPLAMI
	OTVTutari = STOK70E.TL_VERART1TOPLAMI

'***********

	if trim(Iskonto1) > 0 then
		satirSayisi = satirSayisi + 1
	end if
	if trim(Iskonto2) > 0 then
		satirSayisi = satirSayisi + 1
	end if
	if trim(Iskonto3) > 0 then
		satirSayisi = satirSayisi + 1
	end if
	if trim(Iskonto4) > 0 then
		satirSayisi = satirSayisi + 1
	end if

	if trim(IskontoTutari) > 0 then
		satirSayisi = satirSayisi + 1
	end if

	if trim(OTVTutari) > 0 then
		satirSayisi = satirSayisi + 2
	end if
	DipToplamSatirSayisiHesapla = satirSayisi
End Function
Function yazi(geneltoplamyazi)
	if instr(1, geneltoplamyazi, "%")=0 then
	geneltoplamyazi=geneltoplamyazi&" "&doc.defaultpb
	Else
	geneltoplamyazi=replace(geneltoplamyazi, "%"," "&"YTL"&" ",1)&" "&"Ykr"
	END if
end function

Public sayfaTuru, sayfaBoyu, sayfaEni, basla, bitir, sonSatir, satirSonu, ToplamEn , BasilabilirAlan

Sub PrintForm()
	Dim satirSayisi,pe1
	sayfaBoyu = 297
	sayfaEni = 210

	ToplamTutar = 0
	ToplamProTutar = 0
	siraNo = 0
	satirBaslangic = 0
	UstMargin = 0
	AltMargin = SayfaBoyu - 20
	SolMargin = 10
	SagMargin = SayfaEni - 13
	satiryuksekligi = 5.5 ' 7.6
	satirBaslangic = UstMargin + 77
'	satirSonu = satirBaslangic + 14 * SatirYuksekligi
	satirSonu = satirBaslangic + 11 * SatirYuksekligi
	
	Set pe1 = doc.CreatePrintEngineOutput()
	res = pe1.Open(0, "Satýnalma Sipariþi", SayfaBoyu, SayfaEni, "LANDSCAPE")

	Call Alanlar()
	If doc.ErrorNumber Then
		MsgBox ("Hata main : " & doc.ErrorNumber)
		Exit Sub
	End If
	siraNo = 0        
	satirSayisi = STOK46T.GetRecCount()
	Call Print_form_sayfabasi(pe1)
	logo2 = Doc.Customp_Gets("!LOGO2")
	logo2 = Doc.getd7Directory_Datafirm & trim(logo2)
	res = pe1.nopen()
	res = pe1.Picture(UstMargin + 173, SolMargin + 120, 30,120,logo2,94)
			res = pe1.Line(UstMargin ,  1 , 0, 0) ' hizalama
	For i = 1 To satirSayisi
			Call Print_form_satir(pe1, i)
	Next
		
	Call Print_form_sayfasonu(pe1)

	res = pe1.Show()
	res = Doc.ActivateAfterMacro(1)
End Sub
