Hangi Tablo için : STOK60
Ylave Hangi Uygulamalar için : STOK60I
Açyklama : Sevk Ýrsaliyesi - Budora - Kod Ýcmalli
Kod : 
Public sayfaTuru, sayfaBoyu, sayfaEni, sonSatir, satirSonu, sayfaSatirSayisi, ST60,SiparisFaturaNiteligi,sat_rty
Public siraNo, satirBaslangic, satirYuksekligi, SagMargin, solmargin, ustmargin, chkodu, eksatir
Public Grid()

Sub PrintForm()
	Doc.CreateTableObject "XXX=STOK60T", "TMPCOPY"
	Set ST60 = Doc.GetTableObject("XXX")
	Set S60T = Doc.GetTableObject("STOK60T")
	ST60.CopyTable(S60T)
	ST60.GroupBy2("KOD")

	Dim satirSayisi,pe1

	siraNo = 0
	sat_rty = 0
	sayfaBoyu = 203
	sayfaEni = 271
	satiryuksekligi = 4.3
	eksatir=0
	
	Set pe1 = doc.CreatePrintEngineOutput()
	'res = pe1.SetConsolePrintOpt(1)
	'res = pe1.ConsoleOutput_SetLinesPerPage(66)
	'res = pe1.ConsoleOutput_SetCharsPerInch(17)
	res = pe1.Open(0, "Irsaliye", SayfaBoyu, SayfaEni, "")
	ustmargin = 2
	satirBaslangic = ustmargin + 19*satirYuksekligi
	sayfaSatirSayisi = 30
	solmargin = 5
	sagMargin = SayfaEni - 10

	Call Alanlar()
'	If doc.ErrorNumber Then
'		MsgBox ("Hata main : " & doc.ErrorNumber)
'		Exit Sub
'	End If

	satirSayisi = ST60.GetRecCount()
	Call Print_form_sayfabasi(pe1)
	For i = 1 To satirSayisi
		satiryuksekligi = 4.3
		Call Print_form_satir(pe1, i)
	Next
	satiryuksekligi = 4.3
	Call Print_form_sayfasonu(pe1)

	if Doc.GetDocType0 = "TRACK601" Then
		res = pe1.PrintDoc(1)
	Else
		res = pe1.Show()
	End if
End Sub
'----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Sub Print_form_sayfabasi(pe1)
	res = pe1.selectfont("Arial", 9)

	chkodu = STOK60E.CH_KODU(1)
	chadi = STOK60E.CH_KODU_CARI00_AD(1)
	if trim(STOK60E.SEVKADRESI_1) = "" then
		adres1 = 	STOK60E.CH_KODU_CARI00_ADRES_1(1)
		adres2 = 	STOK60E.CH_KODU_CARI00_ADRES_2(1)
		adres3 = 	STOK60E.CH_KODU_CARI00_ADRES_3(1)
	else
		adres1 = 	STOK60E.SEVKADRESI_1(1)
		adres2 = 	STOK60E.SEVKADRESI_2(1)
		adres3 = 	STOK60E.SEVKADRESI_3(1)
	end if

	vergidairesi = STOK60E.CH_KODU_CARI00_VERGIDAIRESI(1)
	vergino = STOK60E.CH_KODU_CARI00_VERGINO(1)
	tarih = Doc.StrtoNorDate(STOK60E.TARIH(1))
	if trim(STOK60E.RSEVKTARIH) = "" then
		sevktarih = Tarih
	else
		sevktarih = Doc.StrtoNorDate(STOK60E.RSEVKTARIH)
	end if

	res = pe1.Setalign("LEFT")
	res = pe1.write(ustmargin + 9* satirYuksekligi, solmargin + 165, 5,30, tarih)
	res = pe1.write(ustmargin + 10.5* satirYuksekligi, pe1.GetLeft, 5, 40, sevktarih)

	res = pe1.write(ustmargin + 7 * satirYuksekligi, solmargin + 22, 5, 80, chadi)
	res = pe1.write(ustmargin + 8* satirYuksekligi, solMargin + 22, 5, 80, adres1)
	res = pe1.write(ustmargin + 9 * satirYuksekligi, pe1.GetLeft, 5, 80, adres2)
	res = pe1.write(ustmargin + 10 * satirYuksekligi, pe1.GetLeft, 5, 80, adres3)
	res = pe1.write(ustmargin + 14 * satirYuksekligi, solmargin + 50, 5, 30, vergidairesi)
	res = pe1.write(ustmargin + 14 * satirYuksekligi, solmargin + 75, 5, 15, vergino)
End Sub
'--------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Sub Print_form_satir(pe1, satirNo)
	'res = pe1.SetTransparentMode(0)
	res = pe1.SetBkgColor(RGB(255,0,0))

		Dim satirpozr
		If Trim(ST60.KOD(satirNo)) = "" Then Exit Sub
		Degisken = ""
		sonSatir = pe1.GetBottom()
		
		If siraNo > sayfaSatirSayisi Then
			Call Print_form_sayfasonu(pe1)
			siraNo = 0
			res = pe1.PageBreak()
			Call Print_form_sayfabasi(pe1)
			satirpozr = satirBaslangic * eksatir
			sat_rty = 0
		End If		

		siraNo = siraNo+1
		
		If eksatir = "0" Then
			satirpozr = satirBaslangic+((siraNo-1))*satiryuksekligi
		Else
			satirpozr = sonSatir+0.5
		end if		

		res = pe1.selectfont("Arial", 9)
		res = pe1.setbold(0)
		Sol = solMargin + 1
		eksatir = 0
		
		For i = 0 To UBound(Grid,1)
			SutunGenisligi = Grid(i,1) 
			DegiskenAdi = Grid(i,0)
			stokkodu = Trim(ST60.KOD(satirno))
			aciklama = Trim(ST60.NOTES(satirno))
			StokAdi2 = Trim(Doc.GetDBFieldValue("STOK00", "NAME2", StokKodu, ""))
			MStokKodu = Trim(Doc.GetCHStokKodu(stokkodu,chkodu))
			MStokAdi = Trim(Doc.GetCHStokAdi(stokkodu,chkodu))
			SipNo = Trim(ST60.SIPNO(satirno))
			ArtNo = Trim(ST60.SIPARTNO(satirno))
			
			MSipNo = Trim(Doc.getdbfieldvalue("STOK40E", "CHSIPNO", SipNo, ""))
			MSipArtNo = Trim(Doc.getdbfieldvalue("STOK40T", "CHSIPANO", ArtNo, "ARTNO"))
			SiparisFaturaNiteligi = Doc.GetDBFieldValue("STOK40E","MUHT_FATURA_NITELIK", SipNo, "") 

			Select Case Trim(degiskenAdi)
				Case "_SIRA***"
					degisken = siraNo
				Case "_AD"
					If Trim(MStokKodu) = "" Then
						Degisken = Trim(Doc.GetDBFieldValue("STOK00", "AD", StokKodu, ""))
						If StokAdi2 <> "" Then
							Degisken = Degisken & vbcrlf & StokAdi2
							eksatir = eksatir+1
						End If
						
						If Aciklama <> "" Then
							Degisken = Degisken & " - " & Aciklama & " - SipNo:" & MSipArtNo
							'eksatir = eksatir+1
						Else
							If MSipArtNo <> "" Then 
								Degisken = Degisken & " - " & "SipNo:" & MSipArtNo
								'eksatir = eksatir+1
							End If
						End If
					End If		
				Case Else
				Degisken = ST60.GetFieldValue(DegiskenAdi, SatirNo)
			End Select
			
			res = pe1.setalign("LEFT")
			If Grid(i,4) = "N" Then
				res = pe1.SetAlign("RIGHT")
				Ondalik = Grid(i,6)
				Degisken = Doc.numformat(degisken, grid(i,1), grid(i,6))
			ElseIf Grid(i,4) = "D" Then
				Degisken = Doc.StrtoNordate(Degisken)
			End If
			
			If Trim(stokkodu) = "3-02-02-0554" Then
				If degiskenadi="SF_MIKTAR" Then
					degisken=ST60.SF_MIKTAR(satirno) * Doc.Stok00_toUnit(stokkodu,ST60.SF_SF_UNIT(satirno),"TAKIM",-1)
				Elseif degiskenadi="SF_SF_UNIT" Then
					degisken="TAKIM"
				End If
			End If
			'If degiskenadi="_AD" Then msgbox Degisken
 			res = pe1.write(satirpozr, Sol, (eksatir*satiryuksekligi)+satiryuksekligi, SutunGenisligi-2, Trim(Degisken))
			Sol = Sol + SutunGenisligi
			j = j + 1
		Next					 
		sonSatir = pe1.GetBottom()

End Sub
'-------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Sub Print_form_sayfasonu(pe1)
	res = pe1.Setalign("LEFT")
	not1 = STOK60E.NOTES_1(1)
	not2 = STOK60E.NOTES_2(1)
	not3 = STOK60E.NOTES_3(1)
	Sofor = STOK60E.BOX1_2
	PlakaNo = STOK60E.BOX1_3

	if trim(SiparisFaturaNiteligi) = "NC" then
		res = pe1.write(UstMargin + 35 * satirYuksekligi, solmargin + 40, 5, 50, "FATURA EDÝLMEYECEKTÝR!....")
	Elseif trim(SiparisFaturaNiteligi) = "Z4" then
		res = pe1.write(UstMargin + 35 * satirYuksekligi, solmargin + 40, 5, 50, "REWORK ÝÞLEM UYGULANMIÞTIR!...")
	End if

	res = pe1.write(UstMargin + 38 * satirYuksekligi, solmargin + 40, 5, 50, Sofor)
	res = pe1.write(pe1.GetTop + 2 * satirYuksekligi, pe1.getLeft, 5, 30, PlakaNo)

	'res = pe1.write(UstMargin + 38 * satirYuksekligi, solmargin + 150, 5, 30, Doc.StrToNorDate(STOK60E.TARIH))
	'res = pe1.write(pe1.GetTop + 2 * satirYuksekligi, pe1.getLeft, 5, 30, Doc.Saat(5))


'	res = pe1.write(215, solmargin, 5, 250, not1)
'	res = pe1.write(220, solmargin, 5, 250, not2)
'	res = pe1.write(225, solmargin, 5, 250, not3)

'	res = pe1.write(240, solmargin + 125, 5, 65, Sofor)
'	res = pe1.write(244, solmargin + 125, 5, 65, PlakaNo)
End Sub

Sub Alanlar()
	ReDim Grid(6,7)
	Grid(0,0) = "_SIRA"
	Grid(1,0) = "KOD"
	Grid(2,0) = "_AD"
	Grid(3,0) = "SF_MIKTAR"
	Grid(4,0) = "_BOS"
	Grid(5,0) = "SF_SF_UNIT"
		
	Grid(0,1) = 9
	Grid(1,1) = 30
	Grid(2,1) = 57
	Grid(3,1) = 25
	Grid(4,1) = 4
	Grid(5,1) = 10
	Grid(6,1) = 26
	
	Grid(0,4) = "C"
	Grid(1,4) = "C"
	Grid(2,4) = "C"
	Grid(3,4) = "N"
	Grid(4,4) = "C"
	Grid(5,4) = "C"
	Grid(6,4) = "C"
	
	For i = 0 To UBound(Grid,1)
		If Grid(i,4) = "N" Then
			Degisken = Grid(i,0)
			ondalik = ST60.MaxSigDig(Degisken)	
			Grid(i,6) = Ondalik		
		End If
	Next	
End Sub

Form Kaynak Kodu
Firma Kodu : BDORA_2014 / Uygulama : PFRM7S

Public sayfaTuru, sayfaBoyu, sayfaEni, sonSatir, satirSonu, sayfaSatirSayisi, ST60,SiparisFaturaNiteligi,sat_rty
Public siraNo, satirBaslangic, satirYuksekligi, SagMargin, solmargin, ustmargin, chkodu
Public Grid()
Sub PrintForm()
	Doc.CreateTableObject "XXX=STOK60T", "TMPCOPY"
	Set ST60 = Doc.GetTableObject("XXX")
	Set S60T = Doc.GetTableObject("STOK63T")
	ST60.CopyTable(S60T)
	'ST60.GroupBy2("KOD")

	Dim satirSayisi,pe1
	sat_rty = 0
	siraNo = 0

	sayfaBoyu = 203
	sayfaEni = 271
	satiryuksekligi = 4.3

	Set pe1 = doc.CreatePrintEngineOutput()
	res = pe1.SetConsolePrintOpt(1)
	res = pe1.ConsoleOutput_SetLinesPerPage(66)
	res = pe1.ConsoleOutput_SetCharsPerInch(17)
	res = pe1.Open(0, "Irsaliye", SayfaBoyu, SayfaEni, "")
	ustmargin = 0
	satirBaslangic = ustmargin + 18 * satirYuksekligi
	sayfaSatirSayisi = 30
	solmargin = 0
	sagMargin = SayfaEni - 10

	Call Alanlar()
'	If doc.ErrorNumber Then
'		MsgBox ("Hata main : " & doc.ErrorNumber)
'		Exit Sub
'	End If

	satirSayisi = ST60.GetRecCount()
	Call Print_form_sayfabasi(pe1)
	For i = 1 To satirSayisi
		satiryuksekligi = 3.5
		Call Print_form_satir(pe1, i)
		satiryuksekligi = 4.3
	Next
	Call Print_form_sayfasonu(pe1)

	if Doc.GetDocType0 = "TRACK601" Then
		res = pe1.PrintDoc(1)
	Else
		res = pe1.Show()
	End if
End Sub
'-----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Sub Print_form_sayfabasi(pe1)
	res = pe1.selectfont("Courier New", 7)

	chkodu = STOK60E.CH_KODU(1)
	chadi = STOK60E.CH_KODU_CARI00_AD(1)
	if trim(STOK60E.SEVKADRESI_1) = "" then
		adres1 = 	STOK60E.CH_KODU_CARI00_ADRES_1(1)
		adres2 = 	STOK60E.CH_KODU_CARI00_ADRES_2(1)
		adres3 = 	STOK60E.CH_KODU_CARI00_ADRES_3(1)
	else
		adres1 = 	STOK60E.SEVKADRESI_1(1)
		adres2 = 	STOK60E.SEVKADRESI_2(1)
		adres3 = 	STOK60E.SEVKADRESI_3(1)
	end if

	MusteriAd2 = Trim(Doc.GetDbFieldValue("CARI00","NAME2",chkodu,""))
	VergiDairesi = Trim(Doc.GetDbFieldValue("CARI00","VERGIDAIRESI",chkodu,""))
	VergiNo = Trim(Doc.GetDbFieldValue("CARI00","VERGINO",chkodu,""))

	tarih = Doc.StrtoNorDate(STOK60E.TARIH(1))
	if trim(STOK60E.RSEVKTARIH) = "" then
		sevktarih = Tarih
	else
		sevktarih = Doc.StrtoNorDate(STOK60E.RSEVKTARIH)
	end if

	res = pe1.Setalign("LEFT")
	res = pe1.write(ustmargin + 9* satirYuksekligi, solmargin + 165, 5,30, tarih)
	res = pe1.write(ustmargin + 10.5* satirYuksekligi, pe1.GetLeft, 5, 40, sevktarih)

	res = pe1.write(ustmargin + 7 * satirYuksekligi, solmargin + 22, 5, 80, chadi)
	res = pe1.write(ustmargin + 8* satirYuksekligi, solMargin + 22, 5, 80, adres1)
	res = pe1.write(ustmargin + 9 * satirYuksekligi, pe1.GetLeft, 5, 80, adres2)
	res = pe1.write(ustmargin + 10 * satirYuksekligi, pe1.GetLeft, 5, 80, adres3)
	res = pe1.write(ustmargin + 14 * satirYuksekligi, solmargin + 50, 5, 60, VergiDairesi & " " & vergino)
End Sub
'---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Sub Print_form_satir(pe1, satirNo)
	'res = pe1.SetTransparentMode(0)
	res = pe1.SetBkgColor(RGB(255,0,0))
	
	Dim satirpozr
	If trim(ST60.KOD(satirNo)) = "" Then Exit Sub
	Degisken = ""
	sonSatir = pe1.GetBottom()
	If siraNo > sayfaSatirSayisi Then
		Call Print_form_sayfasonu(pe1)
		siraNo = 0
		res = pe1.PageBreak()
		Call Print_form_sayfabasi(pe1)
		satirpozr = satirBaslangic 
		eksatir= 0
	End If		

	siraNo = siraNo + 1
	
	If eksatir = "0" Then
		satirpozr = satirBaslangic+((siraNo-1))*satiryuksekligi
	Else
		satirpozr = sonSatir+0.5
	end if	
	
	res = pe1.selectfont("Courier New", 7)
	res = pe1.setbold(0)
	Sol = solMargin + 7
	
	eksatir = 0
	
	For i = 0 To UBound(Grid,1)
		SutunGenisligi = Grid(i,1) 
		DegiskenAdi = Grid(i,0)
		stokkodu = Trim(ST60.KOD(satirno))						
		aciklama = ST60.NOTES(satirno)
		MStokKodu = Doc.GetCHStokKodu(stokkodu,chkodu)
		MStokAdi = Doc.GetCHStokAdi(stokkodu,chkodu)
		SipNo = ST60.SIPNO(satirno)
		ArtNo = ST60.SIPARTNO(satirno)
		MSipNo = doc.getdbfieldvalue("STOK40E", "CHSIPNO", sipno, "")
		SiparisFaturaNiteligi = Doc.GetDBFieldValue("STOK40E","MUHT_FATURA_NITELIK",STOK63T.SIPNO(satirno),"") 
		
		Select Case Trim(degiskenAdi)
			Case "_SIRA***"
				degisken = siraNo
			Case "_AD"
			
				If Trim(MStokKodu) = "" Then
					If Left(stokkodu,2)="ZZ" Then
						Degisken = Aciklama
					Else
						Degisken = Trim(Doc.GetDBFieldValue("STOK00", "AD", StokKodu, ""))
						'eksatir = eksatir+1
					End If
				End If		
			Case Else
			Degisken = ST60.GetFieldValue(DegiskenAdi, SatirNo)
		End Select
		
		res = pe1.setalign("LEFT")
		If Grid(i,4) = "N" Then
			res = pe1.SetAlign("RIGHT")
			Ondalik = Grid(i,6)
			Degisken = Doc.numformat(degisken, grid(i,1), grid(i,6))
		ElseIf Grid(i,4) = "D" Then
			Degisken = Doc.StrtoNordate(Degisken)
		End If

		if degiskenadi="SF_SF_UNIT" then
				degisken=Doc.GetDbFieldValue("STOK00","IUNIT",StokKodu,"")
		end if

		if trim(stokkodu) = "3-02-02-0554" then
			if degiskenadi="SF_MIKTAR" then
				degisken=ST60.SF_MIKTAR(satirno) * Doc.Stok00_toUnit(stokkodu,ST60.SF_SF_UNIT(satirno),"TAKIM",-1)
			elseif degiskenadi="SF_SF_UNIT" then
				degisken="TAKIM"
			end if
		end if

		res = pe1.write(satirpozr, Sol, (eksatir*satiryuksekligi)+satiryuksekligi, SutunGenisligi-2, Trim(Degisken))
		Sol = Sol + SutunGenisligi
		j = j + 1
	Next					 
	sonSatir = pe1.GetBottom()
End Sub
'------------------------------------------------------------------------------

	not1 = STOK60E.NOTES_1(1)

	not3 = STOK60E.NOTES_3(1)

	PlakaNo = STOK60E.BOX1_3

	if trim(SiparisFaturaNiteligi) = "NC" then
		res = pe1.write(UstMargin + 35 * satirYuksekligi, solmargin + 40, 5, 50, "FATURA EDÝLMEYECEKTÝR!....")
	Elseif trim(SiparisFaturaNiteligi) = "Z4" then
		res = pe1.write(UstMargin + 35 * satirYuksekligi, solmargin + 40, 5, 50, "REWORK ÝÞLEM UYGULANMIÞTIR!...")
	End if

	res = pe1.write(UstMargin + 38 * satirYuksekligi, solmargin + 40, 5, 50, Sofor)
	res = pe1.write(pe1.GetTop + 2 * satirYuksekligi, pe1.getLeft, 5, 30, PlakaNo)

	'res = pe1.write(UstMargin + 38 * satirYuksekligi, solmargin + 150, 5, 30, Doc.StrToNorDate(STOK60E.TARIH))
	'res = pe1.write(pe1.GetTop + 2 * satirYuksekligi, pe1.getLeft, 5, 30, Doc.Saat(5))


'	res = pe1.write(215, solmargin, 5, 250, not1)

Sub Alanlar()
	ReDim Grid(6,7)
	Grid(0,0) = "_SIRA"
	Grid(1,0) = "KOD"
	Grid(2,0) = "_AD"
	Grid(3,0) = "SF_MIKTAR"
	Grid(4,0) = "_BOS"
	Grid(5,0) = "SF_SF_UNIT"
	Grid(6,0) = "SIPNO"
	
	Grid(0,1) = 9
	Grid(1,1) = 30
	Grid(2,1) = 53
	Grid(3,1) = 25
	Grid(4,1) = 8
	Grid(5,1) = 10
	Grid(6,1) = 26
	
	Grid(0,4) = "C"
	Grid(1,4) = "C"
	Grid(2,4) = "C"
	Grid(3,4) = "N"
	Grid(4,4) = "C"
	Grid(5,4) = "C"
	Grid(6,4) = "C"
	
	For i = 0 To UBound(Grid,1)
		If Grid(i,4) = "N" Then
			Degisken = Grid(i,0)
			ondalik = ST60.MaxSigDig(Degisken)	
			Grid(i,6) = Ondalik		
		End If
	Next	
End Sub                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                           


----------------------------------------------------------------
Form Kaynak Kodu
Firma Kodu : BDORA_2014 / Uygulama : PFRM7S
Makro Kodu : TOP-TLP
Hangi Tablo için : PERSM11
Ylave Hangi Uygulamalar için : 
Açyklama : Budora - Toplu Eðirim Talep Formu
Kod : 
Public sayfaBoyu, sayfaEni, sonSatir, satirSonu
Public siraNo, satirBaslangic,UstMargin, AltMargin, sagMargin, solMargin, satirYuksekligi, Y5, sayfaSatirSayisi
Public Grid, xCizgi, sayfaNo, Genislik, KatilimciSayisi, ekleneceksatirsayisi

Sub PrintForm()
	siraNo = 0

	sayfaBoyu = 297
	sayfaEni = 210
	UstMargin = 10
	AltMargin = sayfaBoyu - 10 
	solMargin = 10
	sagMargin = sayfaEni - 10
	Genislik = sagMargin-SolMargin
	satiryuksekligi = 8
	SiraNo = 0
	sayfaSatirsayisi = 26
	sayfaNo = 1
	Set pe1 = doc.CreatePrintEngineOutput()
	
	res = pe1.Open(0, "Toplu Eðitim Talebi", SayfaBoyu, SayfaEni, "")

	If doc.ErrorNumber Then
		MsgBox ("Hata main : " & doc.ErrorNumber)
		Exit Sub
	End If

	KatilimciSayisi = PERSM0_LIST.GetRecCount()
	If KatilimciSayisi = 0 Then
		ekleneceksatirsayisi = 31
	Else
		ekleneceksatirsayisi = sayfaSatirsayisi-KatilimciSayisi
	End If
	
	Call Print_form_sayfabasi(pe1)
	For I = 1 To KatilimciSayisi
		Call Print_form_satir(pe1, I)
	Next
		
	Call Print_form_sayfasonu(pe1)

	res = pe1.Show()
	res = Doc.ActivateAfterMacro(1)
End Sub
'------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Sub Print_form_sayfabasi(pe1)
	'res = pe1.SetTransparentMode(0)
	res = pe1.SetBkgColor(RGB(255,0,0))
	
	ReDim xCizgi(0)
	D1 = solMargin + 120
	D2 = solMargin + 145
	Y1 = UstMargin + 21
	Y2 = UstMargin + 28
	Y3 = UstMargin + 35
	Y4 = UstMargin + 42
	Y5 = UstMargin + 44
 
	res = pe1.Box(UstMargin, solMargin, AltMargin-10, sagMargin-solMargin)
	
	res = pe1.nopen()
	logo = Doc.Getd7Directory_DataFirm() & trim(Doc.Customp_Gets("!LOGO"))

	res = pe1.setalign("LEFT")
	res = pe1.Picture(Ustmargin + 1, solMargin + 1, Y1-UstMargin-2, 45, logo, 114)

	res = pe1.selectfont("Arial", 18)
	res = pe1.setbold(1)
	res = pe1.setalign("CENTER")
	res = pe1.write(pe1.GetTop+3, pe1.GetRight+1, 10, sagMargin-solMargin-60, "EÐÝTÝME KATILIM FORMU")
	
	res = pe1.Line(pe1.GetBottom+3, solMargin, 0, Genislik)

	res = pe1.setalign("LEFT")
	res = pe1.selectfont("Arial", "12")

	BBasla = UstMargin + 15
	SatirAra = 7
	Genislik10 = 27
	Genislik20 = 30
	
	EgitimAdi = Trim(PERSM11_INPUT.TALEPEDILENEGITIMT_A)
	EgitimKodu = Trim(PERSM11_INPUT.TALEPEDILENEGITIMT)
	EgitimIcerigi= Trim(Doc.GetDBFieldValue("PERSM08", "ICERIK", EgitimKodu, "KOD"))
	EgitimiVerenKod = Trim(Doc.GetDBFieldValue("PERSM08", "TERC_EDILEN_EGITIMCI", EgitimKodu, "KOD")) 
	EgitimiVerenAdi = Trim(Doc.GetDBFieldValue("PERSM06", "AD_SOYAD", EgitimiVerenKod, "KOD")) 
	EgitimYerKodu = Trim(Doc.GetDBFieldValue("PERSM08", "TERC_EDILEN_YER", EgitimKodu, "KOD")) 
	EgitimYerAdi = Trim(Doc.GetDBFieldValue("PERSM09", "AD", EgitimYerKodu, "KOD"))
	EgitimTarihi = Doc.StrToNorDate(PERSM11_INPUT.UYGUN_TARIH_ARALIGI1)
	
	EgitimSuresiSaat = Trim(Doc.GetDBFieldValue("PERSM08", "SAAT", EgitimKodu, "KOD"))  
	EgitimSuresiGun = Trim(Doc.GetDBFieldValue("PERSM08", "GUN", EgitimKodu, "KOD")) 
	EgitimSuresi = EgitimSuresiGun & " Gün, " &  EgitimSuresiSaat & " Saat"
	
	ek = 3
	res = pe1.SetBold(1)
	res = pe1.write(pe1.GetBottom+1, SolMargin+1, satiryuksekligi-ek, Genislik10, "EÐÝTÝM ADI")
	res = pe1.SetBold(0)
	res = pe1.write(pe1.GetTop, pe1.GetRight+1, satiryuksekligi-ek, Genislik-Genislik10-3, ": " & EgitimAdi)
	res = pe1.Line(pe1.GetBottom+1, SolMargin, 0, Genislik)
	
	res = pe1.SetBold(1)
	res = pe1.write(pe1.GetBottom+1, SolMargin+1, 3*satiryuksekligi-3.5, Genislik10, "EÐÝTÝMÝN ÝÇERÝÐÝ")
	res = pe1.SetBold(0)
	res = pe1.write(pe1.GetTop, pe1.GetRight+1, 3*satiryuksekligi-3.5, Genislik-Genislik10-3, ": " & EgitimIcerigi)
	res = pe1.Line(pe1.GetBottom+1, SolMargin, 0, Genislik)
	
	res = pe1.selectfont("Arial", "10")
	res = pe1.SetBold(1)
	res = pe1.write(pe1.GetBottom+2, SolMargin+1, satiryuksekligi-ek, Genislik10, "EÐÝTÝMÝ VEREN")
	res = pe1.SetBold(0)
	res = pe1.write(pe1.GetTop, pe1.GetRight+1, satiryuksekligi-ek, Genislik-Genislik10-105, ": " & EgitimiVerenAdi)
	
	res = pe1.SetBold(1)
	res = pe1.write(pe1.GetTop, pe1.GetRight+1, satiryuksekligi-ek, Genislik10, "EÐÝTÝM YERÝ")
	res = pe1.SetBold(0)
	res = pe1.write(pe1.GetTop, pe1.GetRight+1, satiryuksekligi-ek, Genislik-Genislik10-90, ": " & EgitimYerAdi)
	res = pe1.Line(pe1.GetBottom+1, SolMargin, 0, Genislik)
	
	res = pe1.SetBold(1)
	res = pe1.write(pe1.GetBottom+2, SolMargin+1, satiryuksekligi-ek, Genislik10, "EÐÝTÝMÝ NO")
	res = pe1.SetBold(0)
	res = pe1.write(pe1.GetTop, pe1.GetRight+1, satiryuksekligi-ek, Genislik-Genislik10-115, ": " & EgitimKodu)
	
	res = pe1.SetBold(1)
	res = pe1.write(pe1.GetTop, pe1.GetRight+1, satiryuksekligi-ek, Genislik10, "EÐÝTÝM TARÝHÝ")
	res = pe1.SetBold(0)
	res = pe1.write(pe1.GetTop, pe1.GetRight+1, satiryuksekligi-ek, Genislik-Genislik10-139, ": " & EgitimTarihi)

	res = pe1.SetBold(1)
	res = pe1.write(pe1.GetTop, pe1.GetRight+1, satiryuksekligi-ek, Genislik10, "EÐÝTÝM SÜRESÝ")
	res = pe1.SetBold(0)
	res = pe1.write(pe1.GetTop, pe1.GetRight+1, satiryuksekligi-ek, Genislik-Genislik10-135, ": " & EgitimSuresi)
	res = pe1.Line(pe1.GetBottom+1, SolMargin, 0, Genislik)	
	
	dikcizgiustbaslangic = pe1.GetTop
	
	res = pe1.selectfont("Arial", "12")
	res = pe1.setalign("CENTER")
	res = pe1.SetBold(1)
	res = pe1.write(pe1.GetBottom+1, SolMargin+1, satiryuksekligi-ek, 15, "S.NO")
	res = pe1.Line(dikcizgiustbaslangic, pe1.GetRight+0.5, (sayfaSatirsayisi)*(satiryuksekligi)+satiryuksekligi-1.5 , 0)
	
	res = pe1.SetBold(1)
	res = pe1.write(pe1.GetTop+1, pe1.GetRight+1, satiryuksekligi-ek, 69, "ADI SOYADI")
	res = pe1.Line(dikcizgiustbaslangic, pe1.GetRight+0.5, (sayfaSatirsayisi)*(satiryuksekligi)+satiryuksekligi-1.5 , 0)
	
	res = pe1.SetBold(1)
	res = pe1.write(pe1.GetTop+1, pe1.GetRight+1, satiryuksekligi-ek, 59, "BÖLÜMÜ")
	res = pe1.Line(dikcizgiustbaslangic, pe1.GetRight+0.5, (sayfaSatirsayisi)*(satiryuksekligi)+satiryuksekligi-1.5 , 0)
		
	res = pe1.SetBold(1)
	res = pe1.write(pe1.GetTop+1, pe1.GetRight+1, satiryuksekligi-ek, 38, "ÝMZA")
	res = pe1.Line(pe1.GetBottom+0.5, SolMargin, 0, Genislik)
	satirBaslangic = pe1.GetBottom

End Sub
'--------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Sub Print_form_satir(pe1, satirNo)
	Dim satirpozr

	KatilimciKodu = Trim(PERSM0_LIST.KATILIMCI(satirNo))

	If KatilimciKodu = "" Then Exit Sub
	SiraNo = SiraNo + 1
	sonSatir = pe1.GetBottom()
	
	If SiraNo > sayfaSatirSayisi Then
		Call Print_form_sayfasonu(pe1)
		res = pe1.PageBreak()
		Call Print_form_sayfabasi(pe1)
		satirpozr = satirBaslangic+1.5
		SiraNo = 1
		sayfaNo = sayfaNo + 1
	Else
		If satirNo = 1 Then
			satirpozr = satirBaslangic+1.5
		Else
			satirpozr = pe1.GetBottom+3
		End If
	End If
		
	res = pe1.selectfont("Arial", 12)
	res = pe1.setbold(0)
	Sol = solMargin + 1
	res = pe1.setalign("LEFT")
		
	KatilimciAdi = Trim(PERSM0_LIST.KATILIMCI_AD_SOYAD(satirNo))
	DepartmanKodu = Trim(Doc.GetDBFieldValue("PERSM0", "DEPARTMAN", KatilimciKodu, "KOD")) 
	DepartmanAdi = Trim(Doc.GetDBFieldValue("PERSM04", "ACIKLAMA", DepartmanKodu, "KOD")) 
	
	res = pe1.setalign("CENTER")
	res = pe1.write(satirpozr, Sol, satiryuksekligi-3, 15, satirNo)
	res = pe1.setalign("LEFT")
	Sol = Sol+17.5
	res = pe1.write(satirpozr, Sol, satiryuksekligi-3, 69, KatilimciAdi)
	Sol = Sol+72.5
	res = pe1.write(satirpozr, Sol, satiryuksekligi-3, 59, DepartmanAdi)
	Sol = Sol+61.5
	res = pe1.write(satirpozr, Sol, satiryuksekligi-3, 38, "")
	'res = pe1.Line(pe1.GetBottom+1, SolMargin, 0, Genislik)
 
	sonSatir = pe1.GetBottom()
End Sub
'----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Sub Print_form_sayfasonu(pe1)
	res = pe1.pensize(0.1)
	For i = 1 to sayfaSatirsayisi
		res = pe1.Line(satirBaslangic+i*satirYuksekligi, solMargin, 0, sagMargin - solMargin)
	Next
	
	res = pe1.selectfont("Arial", 8)
	res = pe1.SetBold(0)
	res = pe1.Write(AltMargin+1, sagMargin-100, 4, 45, "Sayfa No : " & sayfaNo)
	res = pe1.Write(AltMargin+1, sagMargin-45, 4, 45, "FR 010_ Revizyon 00 / 30.05.2013")
End Sub                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                     


----------------------------------------------------------------
Form Kaynak Kodu
Firma Kodu : BDORA_2014 / Uygulama : PFRM7S
Makro Kodu : STOK63-2
Hangi Tablo için : STOK63
Ylave Hangi Uygulamalar için : 
Açyklama : Budora - Sevk Ýrsaliyesi - kodlar detaylý
Kod : 
Public sayfaTuru, sayfaBoyu, sayfaEni, sonSatir, satirSonu, sayfaSatirSayisi, ST60,SiparisFaturaNiteligi,sat_rty
Public siraNo, satirBaslangic, satirYuksekligi, SagMargin, solmargin, ustmargin, chkodu
Public Grid(), eksatir
Sub PrintForm()
	Doc.CreateTableObject "XXX=STOK60T", "TMPCOPY"
	Set ST60 = Doc.GetTableObject("XXX")
	Set S60T = Doc.GetTableObject("STOK63T")
	ST60.CopyTable(S60T)
	'ST60.GroupBy2("KOD")

	Dim satirSayisi,pe1
	sat_rty = 0
	siraNo = 0

	sayfaBoyu = 203
	sayfaEni = 271
	satiryuksekligi = 4.3

	Set pe1 = doc.CreatePrintEngineOutput()
	res = pe1.SetConsolePrintOpt(1)
	res = pe1.ConsoleOutput_SetLinesPerPage(66)
	res = pe1.ConsoleOutput_SetCharsPerInch(17)
	res = pe1.Open(0, "Irsaliye", SayfaBoyu, SayfaEni, "")
	
	ustmargin = 0
	satirBaslangic = ustmargin + 18 * satirYuksekligi
	sayfaSatirSayisi = 30
	solmargin = 0
	sagMargin = SayfaEni - 10
	eksatir = "0"

	Call Alanlar()
'	If doc.ErrorNumber Then
'		MsgBox ("Hata main : " & doc.ErrorNumber)
'		Exit Sub
'	End If

	satirSayisi = ST60.GetRecCount()
	Call Print_form_sayfabasi(pe1)
	For i = 1 To satirSayisi
		satiryuksekligi = 3.5
		Call Print_form_satir(pe1, i)
		satiryuksekligi = 4.3
	Next
	Call Print_form_sayfasonu(pe1)

	if Doc.GetDocType0 = "TRACK601" Then
		res = pe1.PrintDoc(1)
	Else
		res = pe1.Show()
	End if
End Sub
'-----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Sub Print_form_sayfabasi(pe1)
	res = pe1.selectfont("Courier New", 7)

	chkodu = STOK60E.CH_KODU(1)
	chadi = STOK60E.CH_KODU_CARI00_AD(1)
	if trim(STOK60E.SEVKADRESI_1) = "" then
		adres1 = 	STOK60E.CH_KODU_CARI00_ADRES_1(1)
		adres2 = 	STOK60E.CH_KODU_CARI00_ADRES_2(1)
		adres3 = 	STOK60E.CH_KODU_CARI00_ADRES_3(1)
	else
		adres1 = 	STOK60E.SEVKADRESI_1(1)
		adres2 = 	STOK60E.SEVKADRESI_2(1)
		adres3 = 	STOK60E.SEVKADRESI_3(1)
	end if

	MusteriAd2 = Trim(Doc.GetDbFieldValue("CARI00","NAME2",chkodu,""))
	VergiDairesi = Trim(Doc.GetDbFieldValue("CARI00","VERGIDAIRESI",chkodu,""))
	VergiNo = Trim(Doc.GetDbFieldValue("CARI00","VERGINO",chkodu,""))

	tarih = Doc.StrtoNorDate(STOK60E.TARIH(1))
	if trim(STOK60E.RSEVKTARIH) = "" then
		sevktarih = Tarih
	else
		sevktarih = Doc.StrtoNorDate(STOK60E.RSEVKTARIH)
	end if

	res = pe1.Setalign("LEFT")
	res = pe1.write(ustmargin + 9* satirYuksekligi, solmargin + 165, 5,30, tarih)
	res = pe1.write(ustmargin + 10.5* satirYuksekligi, pe1.GetLeft, 5, 40, sevktarih)

	res = pe1.write(ustmargin + 7 * satirYuksekligi, solmargin + 22, 5, 80, chadi)
	res = pe1.write(ustmargin + 8* satirYuksekligi, solMargin + 22, 5, 80, adres1)
	res = pe1.write(ustmargin + 9 * satirYuksekligi, pe1.GetLeft, 5, 80, adres2)
	res = pe1.write(ustmargin + 10 * satirYuksekligi, pe1.GetLeft, 5, 80, adres3)
	res = pe1.write(ustmargin + 14 * satirYuksekligi, solmargin + 50, 5, 60, VergiDairesi & " " & vergino)
End Sub
'---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Sub Print_form_satir(pe1, satirNo)
	'res = pe1.SetTransparentMode(0)
	res = pe1.SetBkgColor(RGB(255,0,0))
	
	Dim satirpozr
	If trim(ST60.KOD(satirNo)) = "" Then Exit Sub
	Degisken = ""
	sonSatir = pe1.GetBottom()
	
	If siraNo > sayfaSatirSayisi Then
		Call Print_form_sayfasonu(pe1)
		siraNo = 0
		res = pe1.PageBreak()
		Call Print_form_sayfabasi(pe1)
		satirpozr = satirBaslangic 
	End If		

	siraNo = siraNo + 1
	
	If siraNo = "1" Then
		satirpozr = satirBaslangic+((siraNo-1))*satiryuksekligi
	Else
		satirpozr = sonSatir+0.5
	end if	
	
	res = pe1.selectfont("Courier New", 7)
	res = pe1.setbold(0)
	Sol = solMargin + 7
	
	For i = 0 To UBound(Grid,1)
		SutunGenisligi = Grid(i,1) 
		DegiskenAdi = Grid(i,0)
		stokkodu = Trim(ST60.KOD(satirno))						
		aciklama = ST60.NOTES(satirno)
		MStokKodu = Doc.GetCHStokKodu(stokkodu,chkodu)
		MStokAdi = Doc.GetCHStokAdi(stokkodu,chkodu)
		SipNo = ST60.SIPNO(satirno)
		ArtNo = ST60.SIPARTNO(satirno)
		MSipNo = doc.getdbfieldvalue("STOK40E", "CHSIPNO", sipno, "")
		SiparisFaturaNiteligi = Doc.GetDBFieldValue("STOK40E","MUHT_FATURA_NITELIK",STOK63T.SIPNO(satirno),"") 
		
		Select Case Trim(degiskenAdi)
			Case "_SIRA***"
				degisken = siraNo
			Case "_AD"
			
				If Trim(MStokKodu) = "" Then
					If Left(stokkodu,2)="ZZ" Then
						Degisken = Aciklama
					Else
						Degisken = Trim(Doc.GetDBFieldValue("STOK00", "AD", StokKodu, ""))
						'eksatir = eksatir+1
					End If
				End If		
			Case Else
			Degisken = ST60.GetFieldValue(DegiskenAdi, SatirNo)
		End Select
		
		res = pe1.setalign("LEFT")
		If Grid(i,4) = "N" Then
			res = pe1.SetAlign("RIGHT")
			Ondalik = Grid(i,6)
			Degisken = Doc.numformat(degisken, grid(i,1), grid(i,6))
		ElseIf Grid(i,4) = "D" Then
			Degisken = Doc.StrtoNordate(Degisken)
		End If

		if degiskenadi="SF_SF_UNIT" then
				degisken=Doc.GetDbFieldValue("STOK00","IUNIT",StokKodu,"")
		end if

		if trim(stokkodu) = "3-02-02-0554" then
			if degiskenadi="SF_MIKTAR" then
				degisken=ST60.SF_MIKTAR(satirno) * Doc.Stok00_toUnit(stokkodu,ST60.SF_SF_UNIT(satirno),"TAKIM",-1)
			elseif degiskenadi="SF_SF_UNIT" then
				degisken="TAKIM"
			end if
		end if

		res = pe1.write(satirpozr+0.5, Sol, (eksatir*satiryuksekligi)+satiryuksekligi, SutunGenisligi-2, Trim(Degisken))
		Sol = Sol + SutunGenisligi
		j = j + 1
	Next					 
	sonSatir = pe1.GetBottom()
End Sub
'------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Sub Print_form_sayfasonu(pe1)
	res = pe1.Setalign("LEFT")
	not1 = STOK60E.NOTES_1(1)
	not2 = STOK60E.NOTES_2(1)
	not3 = STOK60E.NOTES_3(1)
	Sofor = STOK60E.BOX1_2
	PlakaNo = STOK60E.BOX1_3

	if trim(SiparisFaturaNiteligi) = "NC" then
		res = pe1.write(UstMargin + 35 * satirYuksekligi, solmargin + 40, 5, 50, "FATURA EDÝLMEYECEKTÝR!....")
	Elseif trim(SiparisFaturaNiteligi) = "Z4" then
		res = pe1.write(UstMargin + 35 * satirYuksekligi, solmargin + 40, 5, 50, "REWORK ÝÞLEM UYGULANMIÞTIR!...")
	End if

	res = pe1.write(UstMargin + 38 * satirYuksekligi, solmargin + 40, 5, 50, Sofor)
	res = pe1.write(pe1.GetTop + 2 * satirYuksekligi, pe1.getLeft, 5, 30, PlakaNo)

	'res = pe1.write(UstMargin + 38 * satirYuksekligi, solmargin + 150, 5, 30, Doc.StrToNorDate(STOK60E.TARIH))
	'res = pe1.write(pe1.GetTop + 2 * satirYuksekligi, pe1.getLeft, 5, 30, Doc.Saat(5))

'	res = pe1.write(215, solmargin, 5, 250, not1)
'	res = pe1.write(220, solmargin, 5, 250, not2)
'	res = pe1.write(225, solmargin, 5, 250, not3)

'	res = pe1.write(240, solmargin + 125, 5, 65, Sofor)
'	res = pe1.write(244, solmargin + 125, 5, 65, PlakaNo)
End Sub

Sub Alanlar()
	ReDim Grid(6,7)
	Grid(0,0) = "_SIRA"
	Grid(1,0) = "KOD"
	Grid(2,0) = "_AD"
	Grid(3,0) = "SF_MIKTAR"
	Grid(4,0) = "_BOS"
	Grid(5,0) = "SF_SF_UNIT"
	Grid(6,0) = "SIPNO"
	
	Grid(0,1) = 9
	Grid(1,1) = 30
	Grid(2,1) = 53
	Grid(3,1) = 25
	Grid(4,1) = 8
	Grid(5,1) = 10
	Grid(6,1) = 26
	
	Grid(0,4) = "C"
	Grid(1,4) = "C"
	Grid(2,4) = "C"
	Grid(3,4) = "N"
	Grid(4,4) = "C"
	Grid(5,4) = "C"
	Grid(6,4) = "C"
	
	For i = 0 To UBound(Grid,1)
		If Grid(i,4) = "N" Then
			Degisken = Grid(i,0)
			ondalik = ST60.MaxSigDig(Degisken)	
			Grid(i,6) = Ondalik		
		End If
	Next	
End Sub

Form Kaynak Kodu

Public Parca, YariMamul, Mamul, ResimYok, Resim_Mamul, Resim_Y_Mamul, Resim_YOK,ExcelApp,S0P,resim,resim1,Sheet,k,Wb,yenisayfa

'---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Sub PrintForm()
	'bymozan
	Set k = CreateObject("Excel.Application")
	Set Wb = k.Workbooks.Add
	Set Sheet = Wb.ActiveSheet

	'******************************************
	birgunkacsaat = 9
	'******************************************

	TARIHARALIGIICINYIL = InputBox ("HANGÝ YIL ÝÇÝN EÐÝTÝM PLANI ?", "YIL SEÇÝMÝ","2013")
	YIL = TARIHARALIGIICINYIL

	k.Visible = False
	'sayfa sayýsý (40 adet parçada bir sayfa atlama)
	sayfasayisi = LISTET.GetRecCount() / 40
	sayfasayisi = Doc.Ceil(sayfasayisi)
	'-------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
	SATIR = 1
	KOLON = 1
	'--------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
	'LOGO RESMÝ KOY
	Resim1 = Doc.GetD7Directory_DataFirm & "images\logo.jpg"
	rtop = Wb.Sheets(1).cells(1, 1).Top+3
	rleft= Wb.Sheets(1).cells(1, 1).Left+40

	Wb.Sheets(1).cells(1, 1).Select
	Set resim = Wb.ActiveSheet.Pictures.Insert(Trim(resim1)) 
	resimadi=resim.Name
	wb.ActiveSheet.Shapes(resimadi).Select
	
	resim.Left = rleft 
	resim.Top = rtop
	k.selection.ShapeRange.LockAspectRatio = msoFalse
	k.selection.ShapeRange.Height = 40
	k.selection.ShapeRange.Width = 200
	'----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
	'SIRANO BAÞLIK YAZ
	Sheet.Range(Sheet.Cells(3, 1), Sheet.Cells(5, 1)).Merge
	Sheet.Cells(3, 1).Select
	k.Selection.Value = "Sýra No"
	k.Selection.Font.Bold = True
	k.Selection.Font.Size = 12
	k.Selection.Orientation = 90
	'----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
	'FAALÝYETLER BAÞLIK YAZ
	Sheet.Range(Sheet.Cells(3, 2), Sheet.Cells(5, 2)).Merge
	Sheet.Cells(3, 2).Select
	k.Selection.Font.Bold = True
	k.Selection.Font.Size = 12
	k.Selection.HorizontalAlignment = 3
	k.Selection.Value = "FAALÝYETLER"
	'----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
	'SORUMLU BAÞLIK YAZ
	Sheet.Range(Sheet.Cells(3, 3), Sheet.Cells(5, 3)).Merge
	Sheet.Cells(3, 3).Select
	k.Selection.Value = "SORUMLU/LAR"
	k.Selection.Font.Bold = True
	k.Selection.Font.Size = 12
	k.Selection.HorizontalAlignment = 3
	'----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
	'KATILIMCI SAYISI BAÞLIK YAZ
	Sheet.Range(Sheet.Cells(3, 4), Sheet.Cells(5, 4)).Merge
	Sheet.Cells(3, 4).Select
	k.Selection.Value = "KATILIMCI SAYISI"
	k.Selection.Orientation = 90
	k.Selection.Font.Bold = True
	k.Selection.Font.Size = 12
	k.Selection.HorizontalAlignment = 3
	'----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
	'EÐÝTÝM SÜRESÝ BAÞLIK YAZ
	Sheet.Range(Sheet.Cells(3, 5), Sheet.Cells(5, 5)).Merge
	Sheet.Cells(3, 5).Select
	k.Selection.Value = "EÐÝTÝM SÜRESÝ (saat)"
	k.Selection.Orientation = 90
	k.Selection.Font.Bold = True
	k.Selection.Font.Size = 12
	k.Selection.HorizontalAlignment = 3
	'----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
	'TOPLAM EÐÝTÝM SÜRESÝ BAÞLIK YAZ
	Sheet.Range(Sheet.Cells(3, 6), Sheet.Cells(5, 6)).Merge
	Sheet.Cells(3, 6).Select
	k.Selection.Value = "TOPLAM EÐÝTÝM SÜRESÝ (saat)"
	k.Selection.Orientation = 90
	k.Selection.Font.Bold = True
	k.Selection.Font.Size = 12
	k.Selection.HorizontalAlignment = 3
	'----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
	'EÐÝTÝM MALÝYET BAÞLIK YAZ
	Sheet.Range(Sheet.Cells(3, 7), Sheet.Cells(5, 7)).Merge
	Sheet.Cells(3, 7).Select
	k.Selection.Value = "EÐÝTÝM MALÝYETÝ"
	k.Selection.Orientation = 90
	k.Selection.Font.Bold = True
	k.Selection.Font.Size = 12
	k.Selection.HorizontalAlignment = 3
	'----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
	'P / G BAÞLIK YAZ
	Sheet.Range(Sheet.Cells(3, 8), Sheet.Cells(5, 8)).Merge
	Sheet.Cells(3, 8).Select
	k.Selection.Value = "P / G"
	k.Selection.Orientation = 90
	k.Selection.Font.Bold = True
	k.Selection.Font.Size = 12
	k.Selection.HorizontalAlignment = 3
	'----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
	KOLON = 8
	HAFTABASLANGISKOLONKONUMU = KOLON 
	'----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
	'HAFTALARI VE SAYILARI YAZ
	Set ddd=Doc.CreateSObject("d7Date")

	For AY = 1 To 12
		Sayac = 0
		j=0
		i=0
		AYDAKIHAFTASAYISI(AY) = 1
		'----------------------------------------------------------------------------------
		'AYLARDAKÝ GÜN SAYISINI BUL
		For i = 1 to 31
			GUN = i&"/"&AY&"/"&YIL 
			ddd.SetDate(GUN)
			ddd.GetNorDate()  'tarih formatýna çevirme iþlemi yapýyor

			kkk = ddd.IsValid()
			If kkk = False Then 
				if sayac = 0 Then
					AYGUNSAYISI= i-1
				End If
				sayac = sayac + 1
			Else
				AYGUNSAYISI= i
			End If
		Next
	'------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
		KOLON = KOLON + 1
		For j = 1 to AYGUNSAYISI
			BaslTarihi = j&"/"&AY&"/"&YIL 
			ddd.SetDate(BaslTarihi)
			BaslTarihi = ddd.GetNorDate()
			HaftaNo = ddd.GetWOY()
			If HaftaNo <> 0 Then
				If j = 1 Then					
					If AY = "1" Then AYYAZ = "Ocak"
					If AY = "2" Then AYYAZ = "Þubat"
					If AY = "3" Then AYYAZ = "Mart"
					If AY = "4" Then AYYAZ = "Nisan"
					If AY = "5" Then AYYAZ = "Mayýs"
					If AY = "6" Then AYYAZ = "Haziran"
					If AY = "7" Then AYYAZ = "Temmuz"
					If AY = "8" Then AYYAZ = "Aðustos"
					If AY = "9" Then AYYAZ = "Eylül"
					If AY = "10" Then AYYAZ = "Ekim"
					If AY = "11" Then AYYAZ = "Kasým"
					If AY = "12" Then AYYAZ = "Aralýk"
					KOLONBASL = KOLON
					Sheet.Cells(3, KOLON).Select
					k.Selection.Value = AYYAZ 
					AYKOLONBASLANGIC(AY) = KOLON
					Sheet.Cells(4, KOLON).Select
					k.Selection.Value = "HAFTA"
					k.Selection.Orientation = 90
					Sheet.Cells(5, KOLON).Select
					k.Selection.Value ="'"& HaftaNo & "."
 				Else
					If HaftaNo&"." <> Sheet.Cells(5, KOLON).Value Then
						KOLON = KOLON + 1
						Sheet.Cells(4, KOLON).Select
						k.Selection.Value = "HAFTA"
						k.Selection.Orientation = 90
						Sheet.Cells(5, KOLON).Select
						k.Selection.Value ="'"& HaftaNo & "."
						AYDAKIHAFTASAYISI(AY)=AYDAKIHAFTASAYISI(AY) + 1
					End If
				End If
			End If
			AYKOLONSOLUKONUM(AY) = KOLON
		Next
		Sheet.Range(Sheet.Cells(3, KOLONBASL), Sheet.Cells(3, KOLON)).Merge
	Next
	SONKOLON = KOLON
	'-----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
	'BAÞLIK HÜCRE BÝRLEÞTÝRME
	Sheet.Range(Sheet.Cells(1, 3), Sheet.Cells(2, SONKOLON-12)).Merge
	'BAÞLIK DEÐER
	Sheet.Cells(1, 3).Select
	k.Selection.Value = YIL & " EÐÝTÝM PLANI"
	k.Selection.Font.Bold = True
	k.Selection.Font.Size = 30
	k.Selection.HorizontalAlignment = 3
	'--------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
	'TARÝH YAZ
	Sheet.Cells(1, SONKOLON-11).Select
	k.Selection.Value = "Tarih:"
	k.Selection.HorizontalAlignment = 2
	k.Selection.Font.Bold = True
	k.Selection.Font.Size = 12
	k.Selection.RowHeight = 23 
	Sheet.Range(Sheet.Cells(1, SONKOLON-11), Sheet.Cells(1, SONKOLON-6)).Merge
	'TARÝH DEÐER
	Sheet.Cells(1, SONKOLON-5).Select
	k.Selection.Value = doc.Bugun
	k.Selection.HorizontalAlignment = 3
	k.Selection.Font.Size = 12
	k.Selection.RowHeight = 23 
	Sheet.Range(Sheet.Cells(1, SONKOLON-5), Sheet.Cells(1, SONKOLON)).Merge
	'--------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
	'REVÝZYON YAZ
	Sheet.Cells(2, SONKOLON-11).Select
	k.Selection.Value = "Revizyon:"
	k.Selection.HorizontalAlignment = 2
	k.Selection.Font.Bold = True
	k.Selection.Font.Size = 12
	k.Selection.RowHeight = 23 
	Sheet.Range(Sheet.Cells(2, SONKOLON-11), Sheet.Cells(2, SONKOLON-6)).Merge
	'REVÝZYON DEÐER
	Sheet.Cells(2, SONKOLON-5).Select
	k.Selection.Value = "'00"
	k.Selection.Font.Size = 12
	k.Selection.RowHeight = 23 
	k.Selection.HorizontalAlignment = 3
	Sheet.Range(Sheet.Cells(2, SONKOLON-5), Sheet.Cells(2, SONKOLON)).Merge
	'---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
	'HAFTA BÖLÜMÜ FONT VE HÝZALAMA
	Sheet.Range(Sheet.Cells(SATIR, HAFTABASLANGISKOLONKONUMU ), Sheet.Cells(SATIR+2, SONKOLON)).Select
	k.Selection.Font.Bold = True
	k.Selection.Font.Size = 10
	k.Selection.HorizontalAlignment = 3
	'------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
	If TARIHARALIGIICINYIL = "" Then 
		Msgbox "Yýl seçiniz !"
		Exit Sub
	End If
	BASLANGICTARIHI = TARIHARALIGIICINYIL & "/" & "01" & "/" & "01"
	ddd.SetDate(BASLANGICTARIHI)
	BASLANGICTARIHI = ddd.GetRevDate()

	BITISTARIHI = TARIHARALIGIICINYIL & "/" & "12" & "/" & "31"
	ddd.SetDate(BITISTARIHI)
	BITISTARIHI = ddd.GetRevDate()

	Set PRSM10 = Doc.Select1("xxx", "PERSM10", "PLNN_TARIH1>= '"+BASLANGICTARIHI+"' & PLNN_TARIH1<='"+BITISTARIHI+"'","",-1)

	'QStr = "Select * FROM PRSM10"
	'QStr = QStr & " WHERE PLNN_TARIH1>='"&BASLANGICTARIHI&"' "
	'QStr = QStr & " AND PLNN_TARIH1<='"&BITISTARIHI&"' "
	'Set PRSM10 = Doc.RunSQLQuery("df2344XXX2hdfh", QStr)	

	If PRSM10.GetRecCount = 0 Then
		Msgbox "Yanlýþ giriþ! Tekrar giriniz."
		Exit Sub
	End If

	'FAALÝYET DEÐERLERÝ
	SATIR = 6
	KOLON = 2
	For i = 1 to PRSM10.GetRecCount
		'SIRA NO YAZ
		Sheet.Range(Sheet.Cells(SATIR, KOLON-1), Sheet.Cells(SATIR+1, KOLON-1)).MERGE
		Sheet.Cells(SATIR, KOLON-1).Select
		k.Selection.Value = i 
		k.Selection.Font.Bold = True
		k.Selection.Font.Size = 10
		k.Selection.HorizontalAlignment = 3 
		'----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
		Sheet.Cells(SATIR, KOLON).Select
		EGITIMPLANKODU = PRSM10.KOD(i)
		EGITIMKODU = PRSM10.EGITIMKODU(i)
		EGITIMADI = Trim(Doc.GetDbFieldValue("PERSM08", "AD", EGITIMKODU, ""))
		k.Selection.Value = EGITIMADI 
		k.Selection.Borders(9).ThemeColor = 1
		k.Selection.Font.Bold = True
		k.Selection.Font.Size = 10
		k.Selection.HorizontalAlignment = 1
		'-----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
		SORUMLUKODU = PRSM10.TERC_EDILEN_EGITIMCI(i)
		SORUMLUADI = Trim(Doc.GetDbFieldValue("PERSM06", "AD_SOYAD", SORUMLUKODU, ""))
		KOLON = KOLON + 1
		Sheet.Range(Sheet.Cells(SATIR, KOLON), Sheet.Cells(SATIR+1, KOLON)).MERGE
		Sheet.Cells(SATIR, KOLON).Select
		k.Selection.Value = SORUMLUADI 
		k.Selection.Font.Bold = True
		k.Selection.Font.Size = 10
		k.Selection.HorizontalAlignment = 1
		KOLON = KOLON - 1
		'----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
		SURESAAT = PRSM10.SAAT(i)
		SUREGUN = PRSM10.GUN(i)
		'-------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
		BIRIMFIYAT = PRSM10.BIRIM_FIYAT(i)
		BIRIM = Trim(PRSM10.PARABIRIMI(i))
		'-------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
		'PLANLANAN
		PLANLANANTARIH = doc.StrToNorDate(PRSM10.PLNN_TARIH1(i))
		PLANLANANAY = Doc.StrDate_Month(PLANLANANTARIH)

		'KESÝNLEÞEN
		KESINLESENTARIH = doc.StrToNorDate(PRSM10.KESINLESEN_BS_TR(i))
		KESINLESENAY = Doc.StrDate_Month(KESINLESENTARIH)

		For y = 1 to 12
			If PLANLANANAY = y Then
				HAFTASAYISI = AYDAKIHAFTASAYISI(y)
				ddd.SetDate(PLANLANANTARIH)
				PLANLANANTARIH = ddd.GetNorDate()
				HaftaNo = ddd.GetWOY()

				For KOLONNO = AYKOLONBASLANGIC(y) to AYKOLONBASLANGIC(y)+HAFTASAYISI-1
					If HaftaNo & "." = Sheet.Cells(5, KOLONNO).Value Then
						Sheet.Cells(SATIR, KOLONNO).Select
						k.Selection.AddComment
						k.Selection.Comment.Visible = False
						k.Selection.Comment.Text "Planlanan Tarih: " & PLANLANANTARIH 
						k.Selection.Interior.ColorIndex = 6   '15:GRI,1:SIYAH,2:beyaz,3:kýrmýzý,4:yeþil,5:mavi,6:sarý,7:pembe,8:magenta,9:koyukahve,10:KOYUYEÞÝL,11:LACIVERT,12:BOKYESILI,12:MOR
						k.Selection.Interior.Pattern = 1
					End If
				Next
			End If

			KOLONNO = 0

			If KESINLESENAY = y Then
				ddd.SetDate(KESINLESENTARIH)
				KESINLESENTARIH = ddd.GetNorDate()
				HaftaNo = ddd.GetWOY()

				For KOLONNO = AYKOLONBASLANGIC(y) to AYKOLONBASLANGIC(y)+HAFTASAYISI-1
					If HaftaNo & "." = Sheet.Cells(5, KOLONNO).Value Then
						Sheet.Cells(SATIR+1, KOLONNO).Select
						k.Selection.AddComment
						k.Selection.Comment.Visible = False
						k.Selection.Comment.Text "Kesinleþen Tarih: " & KESINLESENTARIH 
						k.Selection.Interior.ColorIndex = 4   '15:GRI,1:SIYAH,2:beyaz,3:kýrmýzý,4:yeþil,5:mavi,6:sarý,7:pembe,8:magenta,9:koyukahve,10:KOYUYEÞÝL,11:LACIVERT,12:BOKYESILI,12:MOR
						k.Selection.Interior.Pattern = 1
					End If
				Next
			End If
		Next
		'------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
		'KATILIMCILAR
		Set PRSM10K = Doc.Select1("xxyx", "PERSM10K", "KOD='"+EGITIMPLANKODU+"'","",-1)
		
		'QStr = "Select KOD, AD FROM PERSM10K"
		'QStr = QStr & " WHERE Trim(KOD)='"&Trim(EGITIMPLANKODU)&"' "
		
		'Set PRSM10K = Doc.RunSQLQuery("df23XXX2hdfh", QStr)		
				
		KATILIMCISAYISI = PRSM10K.GetRecCount
		KATILIMCILAR = ""
		KatilimciSaatlikUcret = 0
		KatilimcilarinToplamSaatlikUcreti = 0
		For j = 1 To KATILIMCISAYISI
			EGITIMEKATILANLARKODU = PRSM10K.KATILIMCI(j)
			EGITIMEKATILANLARADI = Trim(Doc.GetDbFieldValue("PERSM0", "AD", EGITIMEKATILANLARKODU, "KOD"))
			EGITIMEKATILANLARSOYADI = Trim(Doc.GetDbFieldValue("PERSM0", "SOYAD", EGITIMEKATILANLARKODU, "KOD"))
			KATILIMCILAR = EGITIMEKATILANLARADI  & " " & EGITIMEKATILANLARSOYADI & "," & KATILIMCILAR
			'KatilimciSaatlikUcret = Doc.GetDbFieldValue("PERSM0BT2", "N_UCRET_SAATLIK", EGITIMEKATILANLARKODU+"1", "KOD+AP10")

			QStr = "Select N_UCRET_SAATLIK FROM PERSM0BT2"
			QStr = QStr & " WHERE Trim(KOD)='"&Trim(EGITIMEKATILANLARKODU)&"' "
			QStr = QStr & " ORDER BY TARIH1 DESC"

			Set PRBT2 = Doc.RunSQLQuery("dfXXX2hdfh", QStr)
			KatilimciSaatlikUcret = PRBT2.N_UCRET_SAATLIK(1)
			
			KatilimcilarinToplamSaatlikUcreti = KatilimcilarinToplamSaatlikUcreti + KatilimciSaatlikUcret
		Next
		
		Sheet.Cells(SATIR+1, KOLON).Select
		k.Selection.Value = KATILIMCILAR 
		k.Selection.Font.Bold = False
		k.Selection.Font.Size = 8
		k.Selection.HorizontalAlignment = 1
		'--------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
		'KATILIMCI SAYISI
		KOLON = KOLON + 2
		Sheet.Range(Sheet.Cells(SATIR, KOLON), Sheet.Cells(SATIR+1, KOLON)).MERGE
		Sheet.Cells(SATIR, KOLON).Select
		k.Selection.Value = KATILIMCISAYISI
		k.Selection.Font.Bold = True
		k.Selection.Font.Size = 10
		k.Selection.HorizontalAlignment = 3
		KOLON = KOLON - 2
		'---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
		'EÐÝTÝM SÜRESÝ
		KOLON = KOLON + 3
 		Sheet.Cells(SATIR, KOLON).Select
		'k.Selection.Value = SURESAAT & " saat"
		k.Selection.Value = SURESAAT

		k.Selection.Font.Bold = True
		k.Selection.Font.Size = 10
		k.Selection.HorizontalAlignment = 3
		SATIR = SATIR + 1
		
 		Sheet.Cells(SATIR, KOLON).Select
		'k.Selection.Value = SUREGUN & " gün"
		k.Selection.Value = SUREGUN * birgunkacsaat
		SATIR = SATIR - 1
		k.Selection.Font.Bold = True
		k.Selection.Font.Size = 10
		k.Selection.HorizontalAlignment = 3
		KOLON = KOLON - 3
		'---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
		'TOPLAM EÐÝTÝM SÜRESÝ
		KOLON = KOLON + 4
 		Sheet.Cells(SATIR, KOLON).Select
		'k.Selection.Value = SURESAAT*KATILIMCISAYISI & " saat"
		k.Selection.Value = SURESAAT*KATILIMCISAYISI

		k.Selection.Font.Bold = True
		k.Selection.Font.Size = 10
		k.Selection.HorizontalAlignment = 3
		SATIR = SATIR + 1
		
 		Sheet.Cells(SATIR, KOLON).Select
		'k.Selection.Value = SUREGUN*KATILIMCISAYISI & " gün"
		k.Selection.Value = (SUREGUN*KATILIMCISAYISI)*birgunkacsaat

		SATIR = SATIR - 1
		k.Selection.Font.Bold = True
		k.Selection.Font.Size = 10
		k.Selection.HorizontalAlignment = 3
		KOLON = KOLON - 4
		'---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
		'EÐÝTÝM MALÝYETÝ
		KOLON = KOLON + 5
 		Sheet.Cells(SATIR, KOLON).Select
 		
 		If BIRIMFIYAT = "0" Then
			k.Selection.Value = SURESAAT*KatilimcilarinToplamSaatlikUcreti
			k.Selection.NumberFormat = "0.00" & " " & BIRIM
 		Else
			k.Selection.Value = SURESAAT*KATILIMCISAYISI*BIRIMFIYAT
			k.Selection.NumberFormat = "0.00" & " " & BIRIM
		End If

		k.Selection.Font.Bold = True
		k.Selection.Font.Size = 10
		k.Selection.HorizontalAlignment = 4
		SATIR = SATIR + 1

 		Sheet.Cells(SATIR, KOLON).Select

 		If BIRIMFIYAT = "0" Then
			k.Selection.Value = birgunkacsaat*SUREGUN*KatilimcilarinToplamSaatlikUcreti
			k.Selection.NumberFormat = "0.00" & " " & BIRIM
 		Else
			k.Selection.Value = birgunkacsaat*SUREGUN*KATILIMCISAYISI*BIRIMFIYAT
			k.Selection.NumberFormat = "0.00" & " " & BIRIM
		End If 		

		k.Selection.Font.Bold = True
		k.Selection.Font.Size = 10
		k.Selection.HorizontalAlignment = 4
		SATIR = SATIR - 1
		KOLON = KOLON + 1
		'---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
		Sheet.Cells(SATIR, KOLON).Select
		k.Selection.Value = "P"
		k.Selection.Font.Bold = True
		k.Selection.Font.Size = 10
		k.Selection.HorizontalAlignment = 3
		k.Selection.Interior.ColorIndex = 6   '15:GRI,1:SIYAH,2:beyaz,3:kýrmýzý,4:yeþil,5:mavi,6:sarý,7:pembe,8:magenta,9:koyukahve,10:KOYUYEÞÝL,11:LACIVERT,12:BOKYESILI,12:MOR
		k.Selection.Interior.Pattern = 1
		Sheet.Cells(SATIR+1, KOLON).Select
		k.Selection.Value = "G"
		k.Selection.Font.Bold = True
		k.Selection.Font.Size = 10
		k.Selection.HorizontalAlignment = 3
		k.Selection.Interior.ColorIndex = 4   '15:GRI,1:SIYAH,2:beyaz,3:kýrmýzý,4:yeþil,5:mavi,6:sarý,7:pembe,8:magenta,9:koyukahve,10:KOYUYEÞÝL,11:LACIVERT,12:BOKYESILI,12:MOR
		k.Selection.Interior.Pattern = 1
		KOLON = KOLON - 6
		'---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
		SATIR = SATIR + 2
	Next
	SONSATIR = SATIR-1
	'-----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
	Sheet.Range(Sheet.Cells(3, 1),Sheet.Cells(5, SONKOLON)).Select
	k.Selection.Interior.ColorIndex = 15   '15:GRI,1:SIYAH,2:beyaz,3:kýrmýzý,4:yeþil,5:mavi,6:sarý,7:pembe,8:magenta,9:koyukahve,10:KOYUYEÞÝL,11:LACIVERT,12:BOKYESILI,12:MOR
	k.Selection.Interior.Pattern = 1
	'-------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
	SATIR = 3
	'HAFTALAR ARASI DÝKEY ÇÝZGÝLER
	For u = 1 to 12
		Sheet.Range(Sheet.Cells(SATIR, AYKOLONSOLUKONUM(u)), Sheet.Cells(SONSATIR, AYKOLONSOLUKONUM(u))).Select
		k.Selection.Borders(10).LineStyle = 1
		k.Selection.Borders(10).Weight = 4
	Next
	'---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
	Sheet.Cells(1, 3).Select
	k.Selection.Font.Bold = True
	k.Selection.Font.Size = 30
	k.Selection.HorizontalAlignment = 3
	'--------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
	'SIRANO KOLON AYARI
	SATIR = 1
	KOLON = 1
	Sheet.Columns("A:A").Select
	Sheet.Columns("A:A").EntireColumn.AutoFit
	k.Selection.HorizontalAlignment = 3
	'----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
	'FAALÝYET KOLON AYARI
	Sheet.Cells(3, 2).Select
	k.Selection.ColumnWidth = 50
	'----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
	'SORUMLULAR KOLON AYARI
	Sheet.Cells(3, 3).Select
	k.Selection.ColumnWidth = 17
	'----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
	'KATILIMCI SAYISI KOLON AYARI
	Sheet.Cells(3, 4).Select
	k.Selection.ColumnWidth = 5
	'-----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
	'EÐÝTÝM SÜRESÝ KOLON AYARI
	Sheet.Cells(3, 5).Select
	k.Selection.ColumnWidth = 8
	'-----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
	'TOPLAM EÐÝTÝM SÜRESÝ KOLON AYARI
	Sheet.Cells(3, 6).Select
	k.Selection.ColumnWidth = 8
	'-----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
	'EÐÝTÝM MALÝYETÝ KOLON AYARI
	Sheet.Cells(3, 7).Select
	k.Selection.ColumnWidth = 9
	'-----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
	'P / G KOLON AYARI
	Sheet.Cells(3, 8).Select
	k.Selection.ColumnWidth = 2
	'-----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
	'HAFTALAR KOLON AYARI
	Sheet.Range(Sheet.Cells(5, 9), Sheet.Cells(5, SONKOLON)).Select
	k.Selection.ColumnWidth = 2.5
	Sheet.Cells(5, 9).Select
	k.Selection.RowHeight = 14
	'-----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
	'LOGO ÝÇÝN MERGE
	Sheet.Range(Sheet.Cells(1, 1), Sheet.Cells(2, 2)).MERGE
	'-----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
	'IZGARA ÇÝZGÝLERÝ
	'------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
	Sheet.Range(Sheet.Cells(1, 1), Sheet.Cells(SONSATIR, SONKOLON)).Select
	k.Selection.Borders(1).LineStyle = 1  		'.Borders(1):sol dik çizgi,.Borders(3)=üst yatay çizgi,(4):alt yatay çizgi, (5):çapraz \,(6):çapraz:/,(10):sað dik çizgi
	k.Selection.Borders(1).Weight = 2  			'1:noktalý,2:çizgi
	k.Selection.Borders(4).LineStyle = 1		'.Borders(4):alt yatay çigi
	k.Selection.Borders(4).Weight = 2  			'1:noktalý,2:çizgi
	k.Selection.Borders(3).LineStyle = 1		'.Borders(3):üst yatay çigi
	k.Selection.Borders(3).Weight = 2 			'1:noktalý,2:çizgi
	k.Selection.Borders(10).LineStyle = 1  	'.Border(10):seçili alanýn en sað dik çizgisi
	k.Selection.Borders(10).Weight = 2  		'1:noktalý,2:çizgi, 4:kalýn
	k.Selection.VerticalAlignment = 2 'xlCenter 
	k.Selection.WrapText = True 
'-------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
	'FREEZE PANEL
	Sheet.Range("I6").Activate
	k.ActiveWindow.FreezePanes = True
	k.ActiveWindow.SmallScroll Down -3
	'-----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
	'k.Selection.HorizontalAlignment = 3
	'k.Selection.Interior.ColorIndex = 15   '15:GRI,1:SIYAH,2:beyaz,3:kýrmýzý,4:yeþil,5:mavi,6:sarý,7:pembe,8:magenta,9:koyukahve,10:KOYUYEÞÝL,11:LACIVERT,12:BOKYESILI,12:MOR
	'k.Selection.Interior.Pattern =1 '1:xlSolid,2:noktalý3:çizgili
	'k.Selection.Font.ColorIndex = 1 '1:SÝYAH 2:GRÝ,3:KIRMIZI
	'-----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
	Call Yaziciya_Yazdir()
	k.Visible = true
	Set Wb = Nothing
	Set k = Nothing

End Sub
'---------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Sub Yaziciya_Yazdir()
 	Sheet.Range(Sheet.Cells(1, 1),Sheet.Cells(SONSATIR,SONKOLON)).Select
	k.Selection.Name = "DATALAR" 
	Sheet.PageSetup.PrintArea = 	"DATALAR"

	With Sheet.PageSetup
   	.LeftHeader = ""
   	.CenterHeader = ""
   	.RightHeader = ""
  		.LeftFooter = ""
  		.CenterFooter = "Page &P of &N"
  		.RightFooter = ""
		.LeftMargin = k.InchesToPoints(0.518110236220472)
     .RightMargin = k.InchesToPoints(0.118110236220472)
     .TopMargin = k.InchesToPoints(0.318110236220472)
     .BottomMargin = k.InchesToPoints(0.196850393700787)
     .HeaderMargin = k.InchesToPoints(3.93700787401575E-02)
     .FooterMargin = k.InchesToPoints(2.193700787401575E-02)
		'.CenterHorizontally = True
		'.CenterVertically = True
		.Orientation = 2
		.Draft = False
		.Zoom = 40
		.PrintTitleRows = "$1:$5"
		.PrintTitleColumns = ""
	End With

End Sub                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                


----------------------------------------------------------------
Form Kaynak Kodu
Firma Kodu : BDORA_2014 / Uygulama : PFRM7S
Makro Kodu : F-FORM
Hangi Tablo için : PFRM7S
Ylave Hangi Uygulamalar için : 
Açyklama : 
Kod : 
Public strfind(8)
Sub PrintForm()
	
	Dim objDoc, objWord, Selection
	Set objWord = CreateObject( "Word.Application" )
	objWord.visible = True
	Set objDoc = objWord.Documents.Add()
	Set Selection = objWord.Selection
	'objWord.Selection.EndKey 6,0
	FirmKodu = Trim(Doc.Getd7FirmCode())
	Uygulama = Trim(Doc.GetDocType)

	Set PFR7SE = Doc.Select1("dd333d", "PFRM7SE", "", "EVRAKNO", -1)
	'Set MKR7SE = Doc.Select1("dd333d", "MAKR7SE", "EVRAKNO = '_GNPRG1'", "", -1)
	
	KayitSayisi = PFR7SE.GetRecCount()
	'-------------------------------------------------
	'Satyr bo?luk ayaryny yapar.
	Selection.ParagraphFormat.LineSpacing = 10
	objWord.ActiveDocument.ShowSpellingErrors = False
	'----------------------------------------------
	'Tab ayaryny yapar.
	objWord.ActiveDocument.DefaultTabStop = 15
	'---------------------------------------------
	For i = 1 To KayitSayisi
		EVRAKKODU_ORJ = Trim(PFR7SE.EVRAKNO(i))
		If Left(EVRAKKODU_ORJ, 2) <> "++" Then
			EVRAKKODU = Trim(PFR7SE.EVRAKNO(i))

			Kodlar = ""
			EKodlar = ""
			Selection.Paragraphs.Alignment = 1   '1:wdAlignParagraphCenter yani üst satyrdakini ortalar.
			Selection.TypeText "Form Kaynak Kodu"
			Selection.Typeparagraph
		
			Selection.Paragraphs.Alignment = 3
			Selection.TypeText "Firma Kodu : " & FirmKodu  & " / " & "Uygulama : " &  Uygulama
			Selection.Typeparagraph
			Selection.TypeText "Makro Kodu : " & EVRAKKODU 
			'------------------------
		
			'Selection.Paragraphs.Alignment = 1 wdAlignParagraphCenter yani üst satyrdakini ortalar.
			'2: wdAlignParagraphRight yani üst satyrdakini saga yaslar.
			'3: wdAlignParagraphRight yani üst satyrdakini sola yaslar.
		
			Selection.Paragraphs.Alignment = 3
			Selection.Typeparagraph
			Selection.TypeText "Hangi Tablo için : " & Trim(PFR7SE.DOCTYPE(i))
			Selection.Typeparagraph
			Selection.TypeText "Ylave Hangi Uygulamalar için : " & Trim(PFR7SE.ADDDOCTYPES(i))
			Selection.Typeparagraph
			Selection.TypeText "Açyklama : " & Trim(PFR7SE.ACIKLAMA(i))
			Selection.Typeparagraph
			Selection.TypeText "Kod : "
			Selection.Typeparagraph

			Set PFR7ST = Doc.Select1("dd333d", "PFRM7ST", "EVRAKNO='"&EVRAKKODU&"'",">SRNUM", -1)

			For k = 1 To PFR7ST.GetRecCount()
				Selection.Font.Color = vbBlue
				Kodlar = Kodlar & PFR7ST.SCRIPTTEXT_RO(k)
			Next

			Selection.TypeText Kodlar
			Selection.Typeparagraph
			Selection.Typeparagraph
			'-----------------------------------
			Selection.Typeparagraph
			'-----------------------------------
			Selection.Font.Color = vbBlack
			Set PFR7SE= Doc.Select1("dd333d", "PFRM7SE", "", "", -1)
		
			Selection.TypeText "----------------------------------------------------------------"
			Selection.Typeparagraph
		End If
	Next
	'--------------------------------------------------------------------------
	'Font lara makyaj
	For j = 1  To 8
		strfind(1) = "Makro Kaynak Kodu"
		strfind(2) = "Makro Kodu : "
		strfind(3) = "Hangi Tablo için : "
		strfind(4) = "Ylave Hangi Uygulamalar için : "
		strfind(5) = "Açyklama : "
		strfind(6) = "Kod : "
		strfind(7) = "Firma Kodu : "
		strfind(8) = "Uygulama : "

		Set objRange = objWord.ActiveDocument.Content
		
		With objRange.Find
		.Text = strfind(j)
		Do
			.Execute
			If .Found Then
				objRange.Font.Color = vbRed
				objRange.Font.Bold = True
				'If j = 1 Then objWord.ParagraphFormat.Alignment = wdAlignParagraphCenter
			End If
			Loop While .Found
			End With
		Next
		Selection.WholeStory
		'----------------------------------------------------
		'Satyr sonlaryndaki bo?lu?u alma
		Selection.ParagraphFormat.SpaceAfter = 0.5
		'---------------------------------------------------
		'Sayfanyn kenarlaryny daralt
		With objWord.ActiveDocument.PageSetup
			.Orientation = wdOrientLandscape
			bosluk = 15
			.TopMargin = bosluk
			.BottomMargin = bosluk
			.LeftMargin = 43
			.RightMargin = bosluk
		End With
		'--------------------------------------------------------------------------
		'Satyr numaralary ekler
    With Selection.PageSetup
        With .LineNumbering
            .Active = True
            .StartingNumber = 1
            .CountBy = 1
            .RestartMode = 1
            .DistanceFromText = 0
        End With
		End With
		'--------------------------------------------------------------------------
		objWord.visible = True
		desktoppath = CreateObject("WScript.Shell").SpecialFolders("Desktop")
		objDoc.SaveAs(desktoppath & "\" & FirmKodu & "_" & Uygulama)
		Msgbox "Masaüstüne kaydedildi..."
		Set objDoc = Nothing
		Set oWords = Nothing
		Set objWord = Nothing
End Sub
